104.	kalittokai, pāṭṭu 101,11
105.	pattiraṅkaḷ eṇ 1,2,3 ivaṟṟiṉ moḻi peyarppu lōkaṉiṉ malapārk kaiyēṭu (ṭuṭipayan’ḷa āyaṭayayeca āyarēyaṭa) ēṭu aiai, pak. 115-122.
106.	“iraṇṭām āṇṭaikketiril muppattāṟām āṇṭu” eṉṟa toṭarait tiru. viṣ (āca. ṟuhiḷā) (paracurāmar) vaṭṭattiṉ iraṇṭāvatil” eṉṟu moḻipeyarttuk ki.mu. 139 eṉṟu koṇṭār. āṉāl sar vālṭṭar eliyaṭ (ḷucai ṟuyaṭavanaca nuṭaṭaiṭiva)” iraṇṭāvataṟku etirāṉa atāvatu nērmāṟāṉa 36 ām āṇṭu atāvatu 861 eṉṟu koṇṭār. ṭākṭar paṉl (ṉuca. kṣaracanēṭaṭa) mutal āṇṭu eṇ āṭciyāṇṭaiyum iraṇṭāvatu āṇṭu eṇ maṉṉaṉ vayatāṇṭaiyum kuṟikkumeṉṟu karutiṉār. ṭākṭar kālṭuvel (ṉuca. ūyaṭaṉaṟanaṭaṭa) iraṇṭām āṇṭu eṇ maṉṉaṉ āṭci āṇṭaiyum, mutalāvatu āṇṭu eṇ viyāḻa vaṭṭam aṟupatil varum eṇṇaiyum kuṟikkumeṉṟu eṇṇiṉār. ṭākṭar halṭs (ṉuca. ḻaraṭavaṇaḷauh), intiyap paḻamai ēṭu (aiṉēyain havēiḷūrayacala) ēṭu ōō, pak. 288 kāṇka. ṭākṭar halṭs karutukiṟapaṭi, mutal āṇṭu maṉṉaṉ aracurimaiyāḷaṉāka urimai peṟṟu iḷavaracuppaṭṭamēṟṟa āṇṭaik kuṟikkumeṉṟum, iraṇṭātu avaṉ āṭci āṇṭaik kuṟikkumeṉṟum koṇṭār.
107.	90 āṇṭukaḷaṭaṅkiya kirakap parivirutti vaṭṭam: tīvak kuṟaiyiṉ teṟkiluḷḷa makkaḷ vaḻaṅkum 90 āṇṭu vaṭṭam peritum aṟiyappaṭāta oṉṟu eṉṟu karnāṭakattiluḷḷa vāraṉ (ṟuyacacanan) karutukiṟār. maturaiyil nāṟpatu āṇṭu vāḻnta kālattil pōrccukkīciya camaya kuruvākiya peski (kṣanaḷauhi) itaip pakuttārāyntār. aṅkuḷḷa tāyaka vāṉūlār karuttuppaṭi atu cevvāyiṉ 15 valañcuṟṟu maṇṭalaṅkaḷ, putaṉiṉ 22, viyāḻaṉiṉ 11, veḷḷiyiṉ 5 caṉiyiṉ 29, ñāyiṟṟiṉ 1 valañcuṟṟu vaṭṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ nāḷ eṇṇikkaiyiṉ perukkamāka amaintatu eṉṟu teriya varukiṟatu. itaṉ toṭaṅkukaṭṭam kaliyūḻi 3078 ām āṇṭu muṭivil atāvatu ki.mu. 24il nikaḻntatu. āṇṭukkaṇippu ñāyiṟṟu muṟai allatu vāṉmīṉ muṟaippaṭiyamaintatu. enta mēlaiyāṇṭukkum etirāṉa ivvāṇṭu eḷitilē 24aik kūṭṭi 90 āl vakuttuk kiṭṭa muṭiyum. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, ki.pi. 1830kku, 1830-uṭaṉ 24 kūṭṭa, 20 vaṭṭaṅkaḷ 54 āṇṭukaḷ kiṭaikkum : piriṉseppiṉ intiyap paḻamaikaḷ tāmas (yīcaiḷēunayī’ḷa aiṉēyain havēi ḷūravainaiḷa le kūhṭiayaḷa) ēṭu aiai, pakkam 158)
	kirakapparivirutti vaṭṭam tamiḻakattil āṇṭu `iraṭṭai eṇkaḷākat tarappaṭṭa ellāk kalveṭṭukaḷukkum pattiraṅkaḷukkum poruntuvatākum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka (malapār kaiyēṭu, ēṭu aiai, pak. 122il) kaḷḷikkōṭṭai arukiluḷḷa tiruvaṇṇūril kāṇappaṭṭa kēraḷa aracaṉ irāmaṉuṭaiya kalveṭṭiṉ āṇṭu 4 ām āṇṭukku etirāṉa 4 ām āṇṭu ākum itaṟkuc cariyāṉa kiṟistuva āṇṭu 4 o 90 + 4 - 24 = ki.pi. 340 kulacēkara tēvaṉiṉ tiruppuvaṉam paṭṭayam 13 ām āṇṭukku etirāṉa 12-ām āṇṭu ākum. atu 13 o 90 + 12 - 24 = ki.pi. 1158. kulacēkaratēvaṉ ilaṅkaiyaracaṉ mutalām parākkirama pāku (1146-1179) kālattavaṉ (makāvamcō - iyal 76 - 77).
108.	paṭṭayattiṉ mūlamoḻi tamiḻāṉālum āṅkila mutaṉūl āciriyariṉ āṅkila moḻipeyarppiṉ maṟu tamiḻ moḻipeyarppē iṅkē kīḻē tarappaṭukiṟatu. mūlappaṭṭayattiṉ vācakam tamiḻāka āciriyar kaikkuk kiṭṭavillai. atu kiṭṭiṉāl, mūlattamiḻē ivviṭattil ciṟappum payaṉum taruvatāka amaiyum eṉpatil aiyamillai. ēṉeṉil 1800 āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa, caṅkakālattukkuriya, pēccuvaḻakkut tamiḻukkum atu oru ciṟanta cāṉṟu ākum.
109.	`pala nūṟāyirattāṇṭu ceṅkōl naṭatti āḷaniṉṟa’ eṉpataṉ kuṟippu ippattirattil oru vāḻttākat terikiṟatu.
110.	`piṭiyalum payantalum’ eṉṟa toṭarukku ṭākṭar kuṇṭerṭṭiṉāl (ṉuca. ḻuraṉēnacava)’yāṉai mutaliya ūrtikaḷil cellutal’ eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷatum ceṉṉai ilakkiya iyal nūl nāḷēṭu (āyaṉacayaḷa tuṭiracayēṭa ṭika ṭuvainacayavaracana & ḷuunainuna) ōaiaiai pakuti ai pakkam 137; āṉāl cariyāṉa poruḷ “paṇamālaikaḷum poruḷākavum” eṉṟē tōṟṟukiṟatu. piṭi eṉpatu uṭaṉaṭikkācu eṉṟum, peṇyāṉai eṉṟum iru poruḷpaṭum. payaṇam allatu pacaṉam `paṅku’ eṉṟu poruḷ tarukiṟatu. `iṭuvaṭi’ eṉṟa col ṭākṭar kuṇṭerṭṭāl `vaḷaivukaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya vāyil  eṉṟu moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṉāl entap paṇṭai nūlilum atu intap poruḷil vaḻaṅkavillai. cariyāṉa poruḷ pātukaikaḷ allatu mitiyaṭikaḷ eṉpatē eṉṟu tōṟṟukiṟatu.
111.	kūnaṭaṭaiuhnacacala.
112.	ippattirattiṉ nērpaṭam oṉṟum moḻipeyarppum ṭākṭar halṭhiṉāl (ṉuca. ḻaraṭavaṇaḷauh) veḷiyiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. intiyap paḻaimai ēṭu (aiṉēyain havēiḷūrayacala) ēṭu ōō pakkam 285.
113.	`ivvāṟu nūṟṟuvar nāṭṭiṉ mēṟpārvaiyāḷarum kāppāḷarum āvar’ malapārk kaiyēṭu, (āyaṭayayeca āyarēyaṭa) ēṭu ai, pakkam 267. iṉṉoru pattiram irāmavaḷa nāṭṭiṉ 600 paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. āṅkilēyar malapārai veṉṟu koḷḷum varai inta ūrk kuṭiyaracukaḷ toṭarntu nilavi vantaṉa. “nāṭu allatu maṇṭalam pala ūrk kuṭiyaracukaḷ allatu taraikaḷiṉ tokutiyā yiruntatu. nāṭṭiṉ kuṭṭam allatu pēravai mikka pērāṟṟaluṭaiya pērāṇmai vāynta ōr avaiyāyiruntatu. tēvai ēṟpaṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil atu aracaṉ āṟṟalai otukkivaittu viṭṭu avarkaḷiṉ amaiccarkaḷai avarkaḷatu `varampumīṟiya ceyalkaḷukkut taṇṭikka muṭiyum!” ivvācakaṅkaḷ uṇmaiyil 1746il kōḻikkōṭṭil naṭaipeṟṟa uḷnāṭṭuk kalavara nilaiyai viḷakki eḻutumpaṭi kēṭṭuk koḷḷappaṭṭa pōtu, avviṭattil āṅkilak kiḻakkintiya vaṇikak kuḻāttiṉariṉ pērāḷāyiruntavar kuḻāttiṉarukku aṉuppiya viḷakkattiṉ vācakaṅkaḷēyākum. inta aṟivippu muḻuvatum eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvataṟkuriya taṉi mukkiyattuvam uṭaiyatu. ēṉeṉil aṉṟu naṭai muṟaiyilirunta ceytikaḷai atu tiṟampaṭa viḷakkukiṟatu. avar eḻutiyatāvatu: “inta nāyakarkaḷ kōḻikkōṭṭu makkaḷiṉ talaivarkaḷātalāl, āṅkilac caṭṭa maṉṟattaip pōṉṟa nilaiyiluḷḷavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ ellāc ceytikaḷilumē aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷaikaḷai ēṟṟup paṇipavarkaḷallar. aracaṉiṉ amaiccarkaḷ takāc ceyalkaḷ puriyumpōtu aracarkaḷ kaṇṭittu oṟukkiṟārkaḷ.” malapārk kaiyēṭu (āyaṭayayeca āyarēyaṭa) ēṭu ai pakkam. 89.
5. cōḻar
cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya maṇṭalaṅkaḷai āṇṭa aracariṉ taṉippaṭṭa vāḻkkai varalāṟukaḷilē, āṅkāṅkē tuṇṭut tuṇūkkāṉa kuṟippukkaḷē iṉṟu namakku vantu eṭṭiyuḷḷa paṇṭai ilakkiya ēṭukaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivaṟṟilum cila camayam orē aracaṉ palaviṭaṅkaḷil pala paṭṭappeyarkaḷāl kuṟikkappeṟukiṟāṉ. orē marapiṉ pala aracarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi orē peyarāl cuṭṭappaṭavum nērkiṟatu. intat tuṇṭut tuṇukkāṉa kuṟippukkaḷaiyellām tokuttuk kōttu, avaṟṟiliruntu toṭarpāṉa varalāṟu kāṇpateṉpatu eḷitāṉa ceytiyaṉṟu. atilum itil nāṉ mutal taṭavaiyāka muṉaiyavēṇṭiyi ruppatāl, putiya ārāyccikaḷ mūlam nāṉ kūṟiya takavalkaḷ piḻaipaṭṭavaiyāka nēriṉum nēralām. innilaiyil nāṉ piṉpavarum tamiḻaracar āṭci varalāṟṟuk kōvaiyai ki.pi. 50-kkum 150-kkum iṭaippaṭṭa kālattukkuḷḷāka varaiyaṟuttuk koḷḷa virumpukiṟēṉ.
it taṟuvāyaip payaṉpaṭutti, paṇṭait tamiḻ kavitaiyiṉ pakutikaḷ palavaṟṟai moḻipeyarttut tantuḷḷēṉ. ēṉeṉil, tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkak kōṭṭupāṭukaḷ paṟṟi nāṉ eṉ coṟkaḷāl evvaḷavu varuṇittālum, av varuṇaṉaiyaik kāṭṭilum paṉmaṭaṅku teḷivāṉa, viḷakkamāṉa karuttōviyattai innūṟpakutikaḷ taramuṭiyum.
nāṉ ēṟkeṉavē kuṟittuḷḷapaṭi, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṟa imayavarampaṉ ilaṅkaik kayavākuvuṭaṉum makata nāṭṭuk karṇaruṭaṉum camakālattavaṉ eṉṟa cilappatikārak kuṟippu, tamiḻ ilakkiya varalāṟṟil oru mukkiyamāṉa varalāṟṟu viḷakkak kal ākum. atē nūlil ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ taṉ talainakarāṉa vañciyil kaṇṇakikkuk kōyileṭukkum viḻāvukkuk kayavākuvai aḻaitta pōtu avaṉ 50 āṇṭukaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭavaṉā yiruntāṉeṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.1 kayavāku cērar talainakarukku avaṉ āṭciyiṉ kaṭaici āṇṭilēyē ceṉṟāṉ. accamayam ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ 50 āṇṭuṭaiyavaṉā yiruntamaiyāl, avaṉ ki.pi. 75-il piṟantavaṉākavēṇṭum eṉalām.
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ tāy karikālacōḻaṉ makaḷ. eṉavē karikālaṉ piṟanta āṇṭu avaṉ peyaraṉāṉa ceṅkuṭvaṉ piṟanta āṇṭai viṭa 40 āṇṭukaḷukkāvatu muṟpaṭṭatāyirukka vēṇṭum. avaṉ kiṭṭattaṭṭa ki.pi. 35-il piṟantavaṉ eṉṟu koḷḷalām. avaṉ mikavum ciṟu vayatilēyē aracirukkai ēṟi, vaḻakkamīṟiya nīṇṭa kālam cōḻanāṭṭai āṇṭatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē avaṉ āṭcikkālam ēṟattāḻa ki.pi. 50 mutal 95 varai nīṭittirukka iṭamuṇṭu.
karikālaṉ uruvappaḵṟēr iḷaiyōṉ allatu iḷañ cēṭceṉṉi eṉṟa iḷavaracaṉ putalvaṉ2. avaṉ piṟappataṟku muṉpē avaṉ tantai iṟantuviṭṭāṉ. pala urimaiyāḷar pōṭṭiyiṭṭuc cōḻa aracirukkaiyaik kaippaṟṟiyatuṭaṉ, tīyāl avaṉai aḻittuviṭavum cati ceytaṉar. avaṉ ivaṟṟiliruntu tappiṉālum, avaṉ kālalkaḷ tīppuṇ paṭṭuk karukiviṭṭaṉa. itaṉālēyē avaṉ karikālaṉ atāvatu kariya kālaiyuṭaiyavaṉ eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭāṉ. taṉ muṉṉōr kaḷukkuriya araca pīṭattai mīṭpatil avaṉ māmaṉ piṭart talaiyaṉ avaṉukkup pērutaviyāyiruntāṉ.4
iṭarkaḷiṭaiyē vaḷarntu avaṉ vāḻviṉ paṭippiṉai kaḷāl meyyaṟivu vāykkap peṟṟavaṉāyiruntāṉ. avaṉ kāla aracarkaḷiṭaiyē avaṉē aṟiviṟ ciṟantavaṉākavum, āṟṟalil mikkavaṉākavum viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. avaṉ iḷamaik kālam paṟṟiya oru nikaḻcci avaṉ camaya vāyppāṉa kūraṟivaiyum maṉa urattaiyum kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
an nāṭkaḷil ellāk kuṟṟa vaḻakkukaḷilum urimai vaḻakkukaḷilum maṉṉaṉē mutaṉmai muṟaiyāḷaṉāyiruntāṉ. immuṟaiyil karikālaṉiṭam oru cikkal vāynta vaḻakkut tīrppukku vantiruntatu.5 ataṉ muṭivu paṟṟi mutiyōrākiya avaṉ amaiccarkaḷ kavalaippaṭṭaṉar. iḷavēntaṉ avarkaḷ mukat tōṟṟattil karanta eṇṇaṅkaḷai uṇarntukoṇṭāṉ. avaṉ uṭaṉ tāṉē taṉ taṉiyaṟaikkuc ceṉṟu, pōli narai mayirk kōlampūṇṭu, kiḻavaṉuruvilē mīṇṭu vantāṉ. aracirukkaiyil mīṇṭum amarntu irutiṟattāraiyum mikat tiṟamaiyuṭaṉ kuṟukkucāvi, avarkaḷ vāymoḻiyiliruntē meynnilai kāṭṭic cariyaṉa tīrppu varuvittāṉ. meyyākavē naraittalaiyuṭaiya avaṉ mutumai vāynta amaiccarkaḷiṉ ārvap pārāṭṭukku itu urittāyiṟṟu.
karikālaṉ oru periya vīraṉ. veṇṇip peruveḷiyil naṭaipeṟṟa mutal pōril, avaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ, cēraṉ ākiya iru maṉṉarkaḷiṉ iṇaippaṭaikaḷaiyum veṉṟāṉ. taṉpaṭaikaḷait tāṉē naṭattiya cēraṉ mutalām ātaṉ ippōril mutukil puṇpaṭṭāṉ. itu an nāṭkaḷil kōḻaimaiyiṉ māṟāc ciṉṉamākak karutappaṭṭataṉāl, inta avamatippaip poṟāta tūya vīraṉāṉa cēraṉ tāṉāka  māḷvai etirēṟkat tuṇintāṉ. pōrnikaḻcciyiṉ pōtu avaṉuṭaṉirunta kaḻāt talaiyār eṉṉum pulavar tam maṉṉaṉ tōlvikku maṉam naivuṟṟu, ataṉāl avaṉ kuṭikaḷukku nērnta maṉamaṟukkattaiyum cūḻtuyar iruṭciyaiyum kīḻvarumpākkaḷil tīṭṭiyuḷḷār.6
“muracu muḻaṅkavillai. yāḻ icaiyai maṟantatu. akaṉṟa pālvaṭṭilkaḷ pāliṉṟi varaṇṭu kiṭantaṉa. cuṟucuṟuppuvāynta tēṉīkkaḷ tiraṭṭiya tēṉait tīṇṭuvārillai. uḻavar kaḻaṉikaḷil uḻutalait tavirntaṉar. ūrppuṟa veḷikaḷil viḻā payil kūṭṭaṅkaḷ etuvumillai. muḻu nilāk kalai niṟaivuṭaṉ eḻum camayattil ñāyiṟu malaikaḷiṉ piṉ maṟaivatupōla, em vīra maṉṉaṉ mutukil puṇpaṭṭataṉāl māṉam poṟāmal vāḷaip puṟatteṟintu (paṭṭiṉi kiṭantu) iṟantu viṭat tuṇintāṉ. antō, innāṭkaḷ evvaḷavu kaḷippiṟanta tuyaram kappiya nāṭkaḷ!”
iṉṉoru kaviñar veṇṇil kuyattiyār cōḻa aracaṉuṭaṉ pōril iruntār. cēraṉiṉ nalaṅkeṭṭa nilai avar uḷḷattaiyum taittatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu. karikālaṉai viḷittu avar piṉvarumāṟu kūṟukiṟār.7
“paranta kaṭalil cellumpōtu kappaliṉ pāykaḷai ōṭṭumpaṭi kāṟṟaiyē ēvum tiṟam vāynta vīraṉiṉ vaḻittōṉṟalē! karikālvaḷava! ip pōril vīram piṟakkap pōrāṟṟiya valimai vāynta yāṉaippaṭaikaḷiṉkōvē! uṉṉai etirttu niṉṟu pōrāṭi mutukil puṇ uṟṟa māṉakkēṭu tāṅkāmal pukaḻ māḷvu peṟum paṭi uṇavu maṟuttup paṭṭiṉi kiṭakkum avvaracaṉ uṉṉiṉum mēmpaṭṭavaṉ allaṉō?”
maṟṟoru pōril karikālaṉ oṉpatu maṉṉarkaḷ cērnta kūṭṭuppaṭaiyai etirttu muṟiyaṭittāṉ8. āyariṉ maṉṉar marapai vērōṭaḻittu, oḷinākar, aruvāḷar ākiya marapiṉarait taṉ āṭcikku uṭpaṭuttiṉāṉ9. nāṭōṭī marapiṉarāṉa kuṟumparai avaṉ kīḻaṭakkiṉāṉ10. avanti vēntar. vaccira maṉṉar, makatap pēraracar ākiyavarkaḷuṭaṉ avaṉ naṭpuṭaiyavaṉāy iruntāṉ11. piṟkālap pulavarkaḷ avaṉai vāṉaḷāvap pukaḻntu puṉainturait tuḷḷārkaḷ. avaṉ taṉ paṭaiyāṟṟalai intiya purāṇaṅkaḷil ulakiṉ naṭuviṭamākak kūṟappaṭṭa poṉmalai allatu mēruvaraiyil koṇṭu ceṉṟatākavum, ataṉ ucciyil taṉ pulicciṉṉam poṟittākavum avarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.12
kāviriyil āṇṭutōṟum varum paruva maḻai veḷḷam cōḻanāṭṭiṉ perumpakutiyai veḷḷak kāṭākki vantatu. itu mika aṭikkaṭi nikaḻntu vantataṉālēyē an nāṭṭukkup puṉal nāṭu allatu veḷḷam perukiya nāṭu eṉṟa peyar ēṟpaṭṭatu. taṉ etirikaḷai ellām vīḻttit taṉ pēraracil amaiti nāṭṭiya karikālaṉ ippōtu āṭcip pakutiyiluḷḷa nāṭukaḷaic cīramaip patil karuttuc celuttiṉāṉ. nāṭṭil aḻivu parappi aṭikkaṭi tollai tarum ivveḷḷattiṉ aḻivait taṭuttu niṟuttum māperun tiṭṭamoṉṟai mēṟkoṇṭāṉ.
avaṉ kāviri, irukaraikaḷilum neṭuka uyar karai eḻuppa muṉaintatuṭaṉ, ataṉ nīrvaḷattaip parappi oḻuṅku paṭuttumpaṭi aṇaikaḷum kālvāykaḷum kaṭṭa muṟpaṭṭāṉ.13 itaṉāl kāviriyiṉ nīr paraviya iṭaṅkaḷilellām ataṉpayaṉāka, vitaimaṇi ovvoṉṟum an nilattil ōrāyiramāka viḷaiyum eṉṟa paḻamoḻi ēṟpaṭalāyiṟṟu.
cōṇāṭṭiṉ talainakaram kaṭaṟkaraiyiliruntu miku tolai vilaki uṟaiyūriliruppatu nāṭṭiṉa vāṇika vaḷattukku ēṟṟataṉṟu eṉṟu matiyārnta ivvaracaṉ kaṇṭāṉ. ākavē avaṉ kāviriyiṉ kaṭal mukattiliruntu pukaḻvāynta vāṇikakkaḷattai araṇceytu atait taṉ talainakaram ākkiṉāṉ.14
vāṇikattukkum uḻavukkum avaṉ ivvāṟu taṉ aṟivārnta tiṭṭaṅkaḷāl tanta ūkkam viraintu viyattaku payaṉ tantatu. avaṉ nāṭṭiṉ celvamum vaḷamum paṉmaṭaṅku perukkamuṟṟatu. naṉṟimikka avaṉ kuṭikaḷ ikkāraṇattāl avaṉai eṉṟum karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ allatu peruñcōḻaṉ karikālaṉ eṉṟaḻaittaṉar.
karikālaṉukkup peruntirumāvaḷavaṉ eṉṟum peyar vaḻaṅkiṟṟu15. avaṉ āṭciyiṉ mālaippōtil, pāṇṭiyaṉ veḷḷiyampalattut tuñciya peruvaḻuti naṭpumuṟaiyil karikālaṉaik kāṇacceṉṟiruntatākat terikiṟatu. irumaṉṉar kaḷum oruṅkirunta camayam, kāvirippaṭṭiṉattup pulavar kārikkaṇṇaṉār iruvaraiyum nōkkip pāṭiyatu varumāṟu.16
“(palanāṭukaḷukkum)vaḷam koḻikkum puṉalaiyuṭaiya kāviriyaṉ kōmāṉ nī!”
“iv varacaṉō, vīram ceṟinta iṉattavarāṉa pañcavar kaḷiṭaiyē oru ciṅka ēṟu. mūttōr kaḷampaṭiṉum taḷarāmal kaḷamveṉṟu kuṭipurantāṭci ceyyum kuricil. tāymaram paṭṭālum ataṉ kiḷaiyiṉiṉṟu viḻutāka nilattilūṉṟi vērkkoṇṭu ataṉai vāḻvikkum kiḷaimaram pōṉṟavaṉ avaṉ. iḷaiyōṉāyiṉum nākak kūṭṭattiṭaiyē viḻum iṭiyaṟu pōla etirikaḷiṭaiyē ceṉṟu tākkic citaṟaṭittuḷḷāṉ.”
“nī aṟat taṅkum uṟantai vīraṉ. iv varacaṉō, ēṉaiya aracarkaḷaippōla nīrvaḷam vāynta kaḻaṉikaḷaiyum payir vaḷaṅkaḷaiyum maṭṭum uṭaiyavaṉallaṉ. avaṟṟuṭaṉ malai taru vaḷamākac cantaṉa maraṅkaḷaiyum kaṭal taru vaḷamāka muttukkaḷai yum uṭaiyavaṉ. iṭimuḻakkamiṭum muracumūṉṟuṭaṉ tamiḻk kalvikkirup piṭamākiya kūṭal nakarai aḷiceytu āḷupavaṉ. paṉaikkoṭi ēntiya veḷḷiyōṉum (palatēvaṉum) āḻi ēntiya kariyōṉum (tirumālum) ākiya iru tēvarkaḷaip pōla vīṟuṭaiyavarkaḷāy nīṅkaḷ iruvarum etirikaḷukku naṭukkantarattakkavarkaḷ. uṅkaḷai yotta ittakaiya iru peruvēntarkaḷiṉ naṭpaippōla iṉitāvatu ētēṉum uṇṭā?”
“eṉ uraikaḷai iruvīrum cevikoṭuttuk kēḷuṅkaḷ! nīṅkaḷ nīṭu vāḻka. uṅkaḷuḷ ovvoruvarum maṟṟavaruṭaṉ etirttu niṟkum vallamaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷ. āṉāl uṅkaḷ naṭpaṟā viṭṭāl, nīṅkaḷ intak kaṭal cūḻnta vaiya muḻutum veṉṟu koḷḷat taṭaiyirātu. ākavē oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar nallavarāy, nērmaiyuṭaiya varāy iruṅkaḷ. uṅkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷ vaḻi niṉṟu uṅkaḷaip pirikkum tīyōr uraikaḷukkuc cevicāyk kātiruppīrkaḷāka.”
“iṉṟirukkum ārvam kuṉṟāmal, uṅkaḷ naṭpu eṉṟum nīṭippatāka. pōrtoṟum pōrtoṟum uṅkaḷ vēlkaḷ veṟṟi kāṇpatāka. varippuliyiṉ koṭiyum, mīṉ koṭiyum uṅkaḷ etirikaḷ nilattiluḷḷa malaitōṟum alaiyāṭuva tāka.”
avaṉ īkaippaṇpum, avaṉ avaiyai aṇukiya kaviñar kaḷiṭam avaṉ kāṭṭiya vaḷḷaṉmaiyum avaṉatu āṭkiyiṉ kaṭaippakutiyil muṭattāmakkaṇṇiyārāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa porunarāṟṟuppaṭaiyil naṉku viritturaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.17 āciriyaraik karikālaṉ nallaṉpuṭaṉ varavēṟṟa ceytiyai virittu avar maṟṟōru kaviñariṭam akkarikālaṉaic ceṉṟu kāṇumpaṭi kūṟuvatupōlap pāṭal tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ip paṇṭaik kālattil tamiḻaracarkaḷ kaviñarkaḷiṭam koṇṭirunta naṭpiṇakkattaik kāṭṭukiṟatu eṉṟa muṟaiyil itu namakku iṟumpūtu taruvatākum. potu vācakarkaḷukkuc cuvaipaṭāta pakutikaḷai maṭṭum viṭṭuviṭṭu, pāṭṭiṉ moḻipeyarpaik kīḻē tarukiṟēṉ.18
“viḻāvayarum nakarantoṟum ceṉṟu, nakaril viḻā muṭintatum atai viṭṭuccellum pāṇaṉē!”
“uṉ maṉaivi peṭaimayilpōlum cāyaliṉaḷ. avaḷ kuḻal neytōyntu karumaiyuṭaiyavai. avaḷ nutal piṟaimati ottatu. kūṟṟuvaṉ cilaipōl vaḷainta puruvattiṉaṭiyilē meṉmai vāynta kaṇkaḷai uṭaiyavaḷ avaḷ. avaḷ itaḻkaḷ ilaviṉ pūvitaḻkaḷ ottavai. avaḷ kural icai pōṉṟiṉiyatu. avaḷ paṟkaḷ muttuvaricaipōṉṟavai. mayir kattirikkum kattiriyiṉ piṭipōṉṟu toṅkum kātaṇikaḷai aṇintavaḷ. nāṇattiṉāl caṟṟē vaḷainta kaḻuttiṉaḷ. uyar malaikaḷiṉ       ucciyil malarum ceṅkāntaḷ pōṉṟa melviral kaḷai uṭaiyavaḷ avaḷ...
“ittakaiya uṉ maṉaivi yāḻiṉ narampukaḷai meṉmai yākat toṭṭum, taivantum, viraintu, payiṉṟum, iṉṉicai pāṭa, nī tirunīr teḷittu vaḻipaṭṭu vaṉa teyvaṅkaḷaip pūcittāy. nontu kaḷaitta kāluṭaṉ naṭakkum vaḻippōk karukku niḻaliṉṟi ilaitaḷaiyaṟṟa maraṅkaḷai uṭaiyatāy, kāṭṭu yāṉaikaḷ tiriyum kāṭukaḷ vaḻiyākac cellum nī iṭaiyūṟiṉṟic ceṉṟu mīḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟu vēṇṭutal ceytāy.”
“vāḻi, pāṇarkaḷil ciṟanta pāṇaṉē vāḻi! uṉ naṟpēṟu uṉṉai iṅkē koṇṭuvantu viṭṭatu. iṉi ittakaiya īyāk kaṭuvaḻikaḷil nī cellavēṇṭuvatillai. iṉi nī vāḻvil vaḷappaṭuvāy! ēḻu narampukaḷai iyakkum tiṟaluṭaiyavaṉē! uṉṉaiyum uṉ kuṭumpattaiyum vāṭṭi vatakkiya vaṟumaiyai nī ōṭṭi oḻikka virumpuvāyāṉāl, iṉṉē eḻuka!”
“paḻutta kaṉi niṟainta maraṅkaḷaip paciyuṭaṉ tēṭum paṟavaiyaip pōla, nāṉ orunāḷ viṭiyaṟkalaiyil ōr aracaṉ araṇmaṉai vāyilukku ceṉṟēṉ. vāyil kāppōriṉ iṇakkampeṟṟuc cuṇaṅkāmal nēraṭiyākavē uḷḷē nuḻaintēṉ. naṭantu kaḷaittu aluttuc ceṉṟapōtilum, uṭaṉē eṉ kaḷai tīrntatu. makiḻcci nirampiṟṟu. maṉṉaṉai nāṉ uṭaṉē aṇūkiṉēṉ. kaiyiṟ koṇṭuceṉṟa ciṟu muracai iyakki, vaṇṇam pāṭiṉēṉ.”
“eṉṉai nīṇṭa nāḷ terintu paḻakkappaṭṭavaṉ pōla avvaracaṉ kaṉivuṭaṉ pēciṉāṉ. attakaiya aracaṉ vāyilil ceṉṟu irappatukūṭa oru māṉak kēṭāṉa ceyalalla eṉṟu nāṉ uṇarntēṉ. avaṉ eṉṉait taṉ muṉṉē amarumpaṭi kūṟiṉāṉ. avvaḷavu karuṇaiyuṭaṉ nōkkiṉāṉ. eṉ ākam muḻuvatum colla muṭiyāta kaḷippiṉāl pullarittatu.”
“viyarvaiyil naṉaintu puḻuṅki, īrum pēṉum āṭci ceyta eṉ āṭaikaḷai akaṟṟi, uyariya pūvēlaiyiṭṭa malmal āṭaiyāl eṉṉaip pōrttumpaṭi avaṉ uttara viṭṭāṉ. pūṇaṇinta puṉmuṟuval pūt mukattiṉarāṉa avaṉ paṇippeṇṭir poṟ kalaṅkaḷil veṟi yūṭṭum matuvai ūṟṟi ūṟṟit tara, nāṉ kuṭittuk kuṭittu eṉ kaḷai ayarccikaḷaiyum tuyarkaḷaiyum, muṟṟilum maṟantēṉ. paṭukkaiyil cāyntu ōyvu koṇṭēṉ.”
“viḻittu eḻuntavuṭaṉ maṭṭaṟṟa kuṭiyāl ēṟpaṭṭa veṟi mayakka maṉṟi vēṟu entak kuṟaiyum eṉakku illai eṉṟu kaṇṭēṉ. eṉ iṉpam ellaiyaṟṟatāyiruntatu. āṉāl atu uṇmaiyeṉṟu nampa muṭiyavillai. kaṉavō eṉṟu tayaṅkiṉēṉ. āṉāl viraivil atu muṟṟilum naṉavutāṉ eṉpatu eṉakku viḷaṅkiṟṟu.”
“eṉ māṇavarkaḷ avaṉ pukaḻ pāṭikkoṇṭiruntaṉar. atukēṭṭu avaṉ eṅkaḷai varavaḻaittāṉ. nāṅkaḷ ceṉṟu attakaiya taṟuvāykaḷukkēṟṟa vaḻakkappaṭi vaṇakkam terivittōm. avaṉ kampiyiliṭṭu vāṭṭappeṟṟa veḷḷāṭṭiṟaicciyai eṅkaḷmuṉ vaittāṉ. iṉi vayiṟu koḷḷa muṭiyātu eṉṟu kūṟumvarai eṅkaḷai avaṉ uṇpittāṉ. iv viruntayarum camayattilēyē aḻakiya āṭal naṅkaiyarkaḷ yāḻ kaiyil ēnti eṅkaḷmuṉ āṭalkaḷ nikaḻttiṉar.”
“palanāṭkaḷ viruntayarntu kuṭitta piṉṉum, iṉiya cōṟṟuṇavu aruntumpaṭi avaṉ eṅkaḷai vēṇṭiṉāṉ. pālil camaitta cōṟuṭaṉ iṉiya pala paṇṭaṅkaḷum eṅkaḷ muṉ vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. toṇṭai nirampumpaṭi nāṅkaḷ uṇṭōm.”
“immātiri pala nāṭkaḷ kaḻittapiṉ, nīṇṭa nāḷ kaṭunilam peyartta koḻumēḻipōla, eṅkaḷ paṟkaḷ tiṉṟu tiṉṟu maḻuṅkiṉa. uṇavum kuṭiyum eṅkaḷukku veṟuttuppēyiṟṟu. ataṉpiṉ oru nāḷ nāṉ aracaṉiṭam mellap pēcceṭuttēṉ.”
“etirikaḷiṭamiruntu tiṟaipeṟum iṟaivaṉē! eṉ ūrukkuc cella eṉakku viṭaitara vēṇṭukiṟēṉ” eṉṟēṉ. eṉṉiṭam veṟuppuṟṟavaṉ pōṉṟa pārvaiyuṭaṉ eṉṉaip pārttu, ivvaḷavu viraivilā emmai viṭṭup pōkiṟīrkaḷ? eṉṟu avaṉ kēṭṭāṉ. atē camayam eṉakku avaṉ kaḷiṟukaḷum, piṭikaḷum, maṭanaṭaip pōtakaṅkaḷum aṭaṅkiya yāṉait tiraḷkaḷai vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ. takutivāynta pariceṉa avaṟṟai nāṉ peṟṟu, vaṟumaikku mīḷā viṭaitantaṉuppiviṭṭu viṭaipeṟṟuk koṇṭēṉ.
“varaiyā vaḷḷalākiya ivaṉ pōrtteyvamāṉa murukaṉ pōṉṟa veṅkaṭuñcīṟṟamuṭaiya veṟṟi vēlēntiya uruvap paḵṟēr iḷaiyōṉiṉ maintaṉāvāṉ. tāyiṉ karuviliruntē avaṉ aracirukkaik kuriyavaṉākap piṟantāṉ. etirikaḷai yellām avaṉ taṉṉaṭipaṇintu ūḻiyam ceyyap paṇittavaṉ. avaṉuṭaṉ naṭappukkoḷḷāta vēntar nāṭukaḷilellām avaṉ tuṉpam paṭarvittāṉ.”
kaṭalakattilirunteḻum katiravaṉ uyara uyara oḷimikkup piṟaṅkuvatupōla, iḷamaiyiliruntē vīrattilum valumaiyilum vīṟumikkuyarntu, ippōtu kūṟṟuvaṉaip pōla valimaiyuṭai yavaṉāy, īṭeṭuppaṟṟa oru pēraracait tōḷkaḷil tāṅki yuḷḷāṉ. mutal vēṭṭaiyilēyē valam poruntiya yāṉaiyaik kollum iḷañciṅka ēṟupōla, veṇṇil kaḷattil āṟṟappaṭṭa avaṉ kaṉṉippōrilē, avaṉ pāṇṭiya cēra aracarkaḷai vīḻttiṉāṉ.
“āttiyiṉ oṇpūmālaikaḷai aṇinta pēraracaṉ karikālvaḷavaṉ ivaṉē.”
“perumai mikka ivvaracaṉai aṇūki nī avaṉ aṭi vaṇaṅkiṉāyāṉāl, avaṉ putitu piṟanta kaṉṟai nōkkum āveṉa uṉṉai nōkkuvāṉ. uṉ pāṭṭukkaḷaik kēṭpataṟku muṉṉāl, avaṉ uṉakkup paṭṭāṭai aṇivittup poṟkalaṅkaḷil matu aḷippāṉ, nī taṅku nāḷaḷavum avaṉ uṉṉai viruntayarvittu, viṭai koḷḷum camayam uṉ mayir muṭimītu poṟṟāmaraikaḷaic cūṭṭuvatuṭaṉ, uṉ maṉaivikkup poṉ mālaikaḷum, muttu mālaikaḷum, paricaḷippāṉ. cempiṭari mayir vāynta pālveḷḷaik kutiraikaḷ nāṉku iḻuttuc cellum tantattālāṉa mukaṭukaḷai yuṭaiya tērkaḷaiyum, pala yāṉaikaḷaiyum, nīrvaḷamuṭaiya ūrkaḷaiyum uṉakkup paricaḷippāṉ.”
“peṟṟa paricilkaḷai uṉ uḻaiyarkaḷukkup pakirin taḷittu viṭṭu nī vīṭu cella viraivāyāṉāl, appōtum ivvulakil nilavum etuvum nillā eṉpatuṇarntum uṉṉai viṭṭuppiriya maṉamillātavaṉāy, kaṉivuṭaṉ uṉṉait toṭarntu ēḻaṭi naṭantu, piṉ uṉṉait tērēṟṟi uṉakku viṭaikoṭut taṉuppuvāṉ.”
“cōḻa nāṭṭil nīṇṭa nāḷ āṭci ceytuvarum maṉṉaṉ ittakaiyavaṉ. iḷaiyōṉā yirukkumpōtē mutiyōr maṉam niṟaivuṟa nīti vaḻaṅkiyavaṉ avaṉ. kuṭikaḷiṭam aṉpum aruḷum niṟainta kuṟṟamaṟṟa avaṉ nīṇṭa āṭci ulakeṅkum pukaḻ parappiya oṉṟu. avaṉ nīṭu vāḻka!”
“neṟpōriṭaiyē tēṉīkkaḷ kūṭu kaṭṭi vāḻum vaḷamuṭaiya puṉal nāṭṭu vēntaṉ karikālaṉ kālaṭikaḷ mūvulakum vella uyarntavai. āṉāl antō, atu inta oru ulakattait tāṉ tāṇṭa muṭintatu; ataṟkuḷ tīp paṭṭuviṭṭatu.”
uṟaiyūr maruttuvaṉ tāmōtaraṉār cōṇāṭṭu ericcalūr māṭalaṉ, maturaikkumaraṉ ākiya kaviñarkaḷ immaṉṉaṉaip pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷum ‘puṟanāṉūṟu’ eṉṟa peyariya paṇṭaik kavitai nūlil uḷḷaṉa.19
karikāla cōḻaṉ makaḷ naṟcōṇai cēra aracaṉ iraṇṭām ātaṉukku maṇam ceyvikkappaṭṭāḷ. avaḷ ivvāṟu ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ cēraṉukkum, cilappatikāra āciriyar iḷaṅkō aṭikaḷukkum tāy āṉāḷ.20
karikālaṉ maṟaiviṉpōtu uṭaṉirunta karuṅkuḻalātaṉār tam tuyarattaik kīḻvarum pāṭalkaḷil kuṟikkiṉṟār:-
“etirikaḷ kōṭṭaikaḷai añcātu muṟṟukai ceytavaṉ; pāṇarkaḷukkum avarkaḷ kuṭikaḷukkum viruntaḷittu, ellaiyaṟṟa matu āṟu perukki avarkaḷai ūṭṭiyavaṉ; kaṭamai terinta kurukkaḷ vaḻi niṉṟu, peruntaṉmai mikka naṟkuṇa araciyal tuṇaitara, tirumaṟai viṉaimuṟai vaḻātu matil cūḻnta vēḷvic cālaikaḷilē vēḷvikaḷ āṟṟi, avvēḷvikkaḷattiṉ neṭiya tūṇkaḷmītu kaḻukukaḷ kuḻumumpaṭi ceytavaṉ.”
“ittakaiya aṟivum tiṟaṉum vāynta pēraracaṉ, antō, akaṉṟāṉ. avaṉai iḻantu ulakam veṟitākiyuḷḷatu. vēṉiliṉ kōṭaiyilē, kāl naṭaikaḷai aruntuvataṟkāka āyarkaḷāl taḻai kuṟaikkappaṭṭa vēṅkai marak kiḷaikaḷaip pōla, aṇiyiḻantu avaṉ aḻakiya mātaraciyar niṟkiṉṟaṉar.21”
karikālaṉai aṭuttu ēṟattāḻak ki.pi. 95-il nalaṅkiḷḷi aracaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉ kalaittiṟam ceṟinta nalliṇakkamuṭaiya aracaṉ. ataṉālēyē ‘nalaṅkiḷḷi’ allatu nalla kiḷḷi eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ muṉṉōṉ nāṭṭaic celva vaḷamōṅkiya nilaiyilē viṭṭuc ceṉṟataṉāl, amaitiyum oḻuṅkum kāppatu tavira avaṉukku vēṟu tēvaiyillā tiruntatu. āyiṉum avaṉ ayalaracukaḷait tākkavum taṉ āṭciyai virivupaṭuttavum āvaluṭaiyavaṉāy iruntāṉ.
avaṉuṭaya pōrp paṭai eḻuccikaḷuḷ oṉṟil avaṉ paṭaiyuṭaṉ pāḷaiyamiṭṭirukkum camayattil kōvūrkiḻār eṉṟa pulavar avaṉiṭam ceṉṟu ivvāṟu pāṭiṉār :22
“aṟattiṉ piṉṉē poruḷum, iṉpamum, celvatu pōla, uṉ māṟṟār iruvar (pāṇṭiyaṉ, cēraṉ) kuṭaikaḷum muḻunilā veṉat tuḷaṅkum uṉ kuṭaiyiṉ piṉ varukiṉṟaṉa. pukaḻavāvuṭaiya nī uṉ paṭaivīṭṭil taṅkuvatu tavira vēṟu eṅkē cellamuṭiyum?” 
“etirikaḷiṉ kōṭṭaimatilkaḷait tākki muṉai maḻuṅkiya tantaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya uṉ yāṉaikaḷ veṟitē cōmpiyirukka muṭiyāmal paṭapaṭakkiṉṟaṉa. kālil vīrak kaḻalkaḷaṇinta uṉ paṭaivīrarkaḷ akaṉṟaṭarnta kāṭukaḷaik kaṭantēṉum etirikaḷ nāṭṭil nuḻaiya irukkiṉṟaṉar. uṉ pōrkkutiraikaḷ kīḻkaṭaliliruntu puṟappaṭṭu mēl kaṭalil taṅkaḷ kuḷampukaḷai alampumvarai niṟpatillai. itaṉāl tuṇakkuṟṟu naṭuṅki nī tampakkam paṭai naṭattiviṭak kūṭumeṉṟu añci, vaṭapula aracarkaḷ kaṇṇimaiyātu viḻittiruntu (tam ellaikaḷaik) kākkiṉṟaṉar.”
avaṉ talainakariliruntu aṭikkaṭi veḷiyē ceṉṟu taṅkiyiruntataṉāl, maṉṉar kuṭiyiṉ iḷaiyōrmītu avaṉ āṟṟal taḷarntiruntatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu. neṭuṅkiḷḷi eṉṟa cōḻa iḷavaracaṉ avaṉukketirākak kiḷarnteḻumpaṭi tūṇṭap paṭṭāṉ. talainakaramākiya uṟaiyūṟai avaṉ kaippaṟṟik koṇṭāṉ.
ik kiḷarcci paṟṟik kēḷviyuṟṟa aracaṉ uṟaiyūrukku viraintu ceṉṟu atai muṟṟukaiviṭṭāṉ. muṟṟukaiyiṉ pōtu, iḷantattaṉ eṉṟa oru pāṭaṟ pulavaṉ uṟaiyūr ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉai ōr oṟṟaṉ eṉṟu aiyuṟṟu, neṭuṅkiḷḷi avaṉaik kaippaṟṟik kolai ceyya muṉaintiruntāṉ. accamayam kōvūrkiḻār eṉṟa pulavar avaṉiṭam ceṉṟu iḷantattaṉ uyirukku maṉṟāṭi avaṉaik kāttār.
ittaṟuvāyil kōvūrkiḻār neṭuṅkiḷḷiyai nōkkip pāṭiya pāṭal varumāṟu :23
“pulavarkaḷ paṟavaikaḷaippōla eṅkum paṟappavarkaḷ. puravalarkaḷai nāṭik kāṭukaḷaik kaṭappavarkaḷ. nāvaṟinta aḷavu avarkaḷaip pukaḻpavarkaḷ. peṟṟa paricil koṇṭu makiḻntu. tam uḻaiyarkaḷuṭaṉ viruntu ayarpavarkaḷ. mīttuvaitta laṟiyātu uṇpavarkaḷ. koṭuttalaṉṟip pēṇal aṟiyātavarkaḷ. avarkaḷ avāvuvatu pukaḻaṉṟi vēṟillai.”
“piṟar virumpi aḷippataṉṟi vāḻvukku vēṟu vakaiyaṟṟa iṉam pāṇar iṉam. ittakaiyōr eppōtāvatu piṟarukkut tīṅku ceyya eṇṇūvārkaḷā? orupōtum māṭṭārkaḷ. tampōṉṟa peru maṉṉarkaḷaip puravalaṉākappeṟṟa kāraṇattāl, maṟṟap pāṭakarkaḷuṭaṉ pōṭṭiyiṭṭu avarkaḷ talaiyiṟaṅkak kaṇṭu tarukkuṭaṉ naṭappavarkaḷ avarkaḷ.”
uṟaiyūriliruntu kiḷarcci ceyta iḷavaracaṉ cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ maṟṟōr araṇvāynta nakarāṉa āvūrukku ōṭiṉāṉ. nalaṅkiḷḷi avaṉai aṅkum piṉtoṭarntu āvūraiyum muṟṟukai yiṭṭāṉ. muṟṟukai ellaikaṭantu mikavum nīṭittataṉāl, nakaril vāḻvōr uṇaviṉṟip paṭṭiṉik kuḷḷāṉārkaḷ. āvūr makkaḷum paṭai vīrarum aṭainta iṉṉalkaḷ kīḻvarum pāṭalil viritturaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.24
“kaḷiṟukaḷ tattam piṭikaḷuṭaṉē kōṭṭaikku veḷiyē yuḷḷa ērikaḷil nīrāṭuvataṟku neṭunāḷ iṭṭuc cellappaṭā tirukkiṉṟaṉa. vaḻakkamāka avaṟṟiṟku ūṭṭappaṭum veṇṇey kalanta cōṟṟukkavaḷamum ūṭṭappaṭātirukkiṉṟaṉa. innilaiyil avai tām caṅkiliyāl piṇippuṇṭa kaṭṭut taṟikaḷiṉmītu cīṟukiṉṟaṉa. kuḻantaikaḷ pālillāmal aḻukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷ malariṉṟik kūntal muṭikkiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷ kuṭikka nīriṉṟi alamarukiṉṟaṉar.”
“ipṭiyē iṉṉum iṅkē nīṭittiruppatu pēriṭar taruvatu. virainaṭaik kutiraikaḷaiyuṭaiya vēntaṉē! nī takkapaṭi naṭappatāṉāl vāyilkaḷait tiṟantu um maṉṉaṉiṭam kōṭṭaiyai oppaṭaikka! allatu pōr ceyyavē karutiṉāyāṉāl. pōrvīrarkaḷai naṭāttic ceṉṟu etiriyuṭaṉ pōriṭuka! iraṇṭum ceyyāmal kōṭṭaiyiṉ valimai vāynta katavukaḷai aṭaittukkoṇṭu uyar matilkaḷukkup piṉ patuṅkiyiruppatu māṉakkēṭāṉa ceyalēyākum.”
maṉṉaṉukkuk kīḻppaṭiyāmaliruppatupaṟṟik kaviñar neṭuṅkiḷḷiyai iṭitturaittataṉ payaṉāka, kiḷarcci ceyta iḷavaracaṉaiyum maṉṉaṉaiyum iṇakkuvippatil veṟṟi kaṇṭār. im muyaṟciyil avar kūṟiya pāṭal oṉṟu varumāṟu.25
“avaṉ paṉaiyiṉ veṇpū aṇintavaṉallaṉ. kariya vēmpiṉ pūvum aṇintavaṉallaṉ. nī ātti mālai cūṭiyuḷḷāy. avaṉu m āttimālaiyē cūṭi varukiṟāṉ. uṅkaḷil evar tōṟṟālum, tōṟpatu uṅkaḷ cōḻar kuṭiyē. nīṅkaḷ iruvarum veṟṟipeṟuvīr eṉpatum muṭiyāta oṉṟu. uṅkaḷiruvariṭaiyē pōr nikaḻvatu uṅkaḷ paḻaṅkuṭikku aḻivu taruvatu. antō, uṅkaḷ pakaivar makiḻumpaṭi naṭaipeṟum ivvuḷ vīṭṭuk kalakam evvaḷavu koṭiyatu!”
an nāṭkaḷil ovvoru aracaṉum paṭait talaivaṉum pōrkkaḷattil vaḻakkamāka ovvoruvakai mālaiyaic cūṭiṉar. itaṉmūlam etirikaḷiṭamiruntu avarkaḷ tammai vēṟu pirittuk kāṭṭikkoṇṭaṉar. pāṇṭiyaṉ vēmpiṉ malarkaḷai vēyntukoṇṭāṉ. cēraṉ paṉaimalaraiyum cōḻaṉ ātti malaraiyum aṇintāṉ. mēlē tarappaṭṭa pāṭṭil, cōḻaṉuṭaṉ pōriṭuvatu pāṇṭiyaṉumalla, cēraṉumalla eṉpataik kaviñar ivvāṟu viḷakkiyirukkiṟār. iruvarum ātti mālaiyē aṇintiruppataṉāl, iruvarum cōḻarēyāyiṉar.
uḷnāṭṭup pōrkaḷāl alaippuṇṭu, nalaṅkiḷḷi āṭciyil cōḻa aracu celva vaḷamikkatāy amaiyavillai. veṟṟikaramāṉa āṭciyāḷaṉāka viḷaṅkuvataṟkuriya takutikaḷil cila avaṉiṭam illātiruntaṉa. avaṉ naṟkuṇamuṭaiyavaṉē yāṉālum, taṉ āṭci vaḷam paṟṟik taṟperumai koṇṭi runtāṉ. avaṉē pāṭiya kīḻvarum ippāṭal kāṭṭukiṟatu.26
“mellavantu eṉṉaṭi taḻuvi oruvaṉ kuṟaiyirantu kēṭpatāṉāl, nāṉ ip paḻam pēraracaikkūṭak koṭupnṉ. atumaṭṭumō? eṉ uyiraikkūṭa avaṉukkāka iṭarppaṭuttuvēṉ. āṉāl, eṉ vallamaiyaik kuṟaivāka matittu, aṟiviḻantu eṉ taṉṉāṇmaiyai etirttālō, tūṅkum pulimītu iṭaṟiviḻunta mūrkkaṉ pōla, avaṉ uyiriḻantu pōvāṉ. pāriya yāṉai iḷa mūṅkil taḷiraik kālaṭiyiliṭṭut tuvaittalpōla, attakai yōrait tākki aḻikkāviṭṭāl, nāṉ nallōr nāṭātu nīṇṭa kūntalai ūṭaiya potu makaḷirai aṇaintēṉākuka.”
avaṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭil araṇamainta ēḻu nakaraṅkaḷaik kaippaṟṟiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. araciṉ ellaiyai virivupaṭutta vēṟecceyalum avaṉ ceytatākak kuṟippiṭappaṭavillai. tollai nirampiya kuṟukiya kāla āṭcikkupiṉ avaṉ iṟantatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu. aracavaikku varuvōriṭam aṉpiṇakkam kāṭṭumpaṭi avaṉukku mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttāṉ aṟivuṟuttiya kīḻvarum pāṭṭuc cuvaikaramāṉatu.27
“nūṟitaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya kaṟaiyaṟṟa tāmarai malaraip pōla, ivvulakaiyāṇṭa peruṅkuṭiyil piṟantavarkaḷaik kūrntu kavaṉittuppār. avarkaḷil mikac cilarē pāṭṭilum, kataiyilum pukaḻ parappiyuḷḷārkaḷ. tāmaraiyiṉ ularnta ilaiccarukukaḷ pōla avarkaḷ pukaḻiṉṟi vīḻntuḷḷārkaḷ. āṉāl kaviñar pāṭalkaḷukku ilakkāṉa ceyalkaḷ ceyta periyōrkaḷō, mēlulakaṅkaḷil vāṉa vūrtikaḷil cellukiṉṟaṉar eṉṟu kēḷvippaṭukiṟōm.”
“aṇṇal cēṭceṉṉi nalaṅkiḷḷiyē! etuvum māṟimāṟi vāḻntum maṭintumē varuvataik kāṇpāy. ellām aḻintu mīṇṭum piṟakkiṉṟaṉa. kallātavarkaḷ kūṭat taṇmatiyattaip pārttu itai aṟintukoḷḷalām. atu yāvarum kāṇa (vaḷarntu tēyntu) māṟi varukiṟatu.”
“ākavē, uṉ tayavai nāṭivaruvōr evarāyiṉum, avar valintōrāyiṉumcari, melintōrāyiṉumcari, ellāriṭamum aṉpiṇakkamuḷḷavaṉāyiru. piṟarukku eṉṟum utavāmal taṉakkeṉa vāḻum kayavarkaḷ uṉakku etirikaḷāyirukkaṭṭum.”
nalakkiḷḷi maṟaivuṟṟapiṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ cōḻa aracirukkai ēṟiṉāṉ. āyiṉum avaṉ urimai cōḻar kuṭikkuriya maṟṟa iḷavaracarkaḷiṉ etirppukkāḷāyiṟṟu. avarkaḷil oṉpatiṉ marukkuk kuṟaiyātavarkaḷ kiḷarnteḻuntu nāṭṭait tuṇṭāṭa viruntaṉar. āṉāl kiḷḷivaḷavaṉiṉ maittuṉaṉāṉa ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉcēraṉ oṉpatiṉmaraiyum orucēra nērivāyilil muṟiyaṭittuk kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ āṭciyurimaiyai nilai nāṭṭiṉāṉ.28
kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ itaṉpiṉ malāṭṭut talaivaṉ malaiya māṉait tākkiṉāṉ. im maṉṉaṉai etirtta oṉpatu iḷavaracar kaḷukku avaṉ ātaravu tantiruntatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu. ik kāraṇattāl kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ avaṉ āṟṟalai aṭakka muṭivu ceytāṉ. avaṉ malaiyamāṉai muṟiyaṭittukkoṉṟu, iḷa vayatiṉarāṉa avaṉ ciṟuvarkaḷaik kaippaṟṟi avarkaḷai yāṉaik kālaṭiyiliṭṭuttuvaittu aḻikka muṟpaṭṭāṉ.
acciṟuvar cārpil kōvūrkiḻār talaiyiṭṭu aracaṉuṭaṉ ivvāṟu vātāṭiṉār.
“iṭukkaṭpaṭṭōraik kāttup puṟāvukkut taṇṇaḷi ceytavaṉ marapil vantavaṉ nī! intac ciṟuvar pulavarkaḷai ātirittup pukaḻpeṟṟa kuṭiyil vantavarkaḷ. cīṟiya yāṉaiyaik kaṇṭu añciya avarkaḷ, ippōtu maṉṉavaiyaik kaṇṭu maruḷukiṉṟaṉar. eṉ coṟkaḷukkuc cevi cāyttapiṉ, uṉakku etu viruppamō atu ceyka!”29
kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭaiyeṭuttu maturaivarai ceṉṟāṉ. āṉāl, pāṇṭiyap paṭaiyiṉ talaivaṉāṉa paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉāl annakar matil puṟattē muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.30
taṉ maittuṉaṉāṉa cēraṉceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ maṟaivuṟṟapiṉ, cēra nāṭṭiṉ oru pakutiyāyirunta kōṅku nāṭṭukkuḷ avaṉ paṭaiyeṭuttāṉ. cērar paṭaikaḷiṉ etirppu mikavum valimai yaṟṟatāyiruntataṉāl, cōḻa aracaṉ taṉ paṭaikaḷaic cērar talainakarāṉa vañcimānakariṉ vāyilukkē koṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ. cēra aracaṉ kōṭṭaikku veḷiyilvaravē tuṇiyavillai. itaṉāl nakar matilukku veḷiyilirunta kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ, vayalkaḷ tōṭṭaṅkaḷaik kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ aḻittāṉ.
cōḻaṉ paṭaiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟirunta cila kaviñarkaḷ aracaṉai nōkkip pāṭiya cila pāṭalkaḷ varumāṟu:- 
“veṟṟiyuṭaiya vēlaiyum vīramikka paṭaiyaiyum uṭaiya aracē! peruntaṉmaiyuṭaṉ puṟāvaik kātta cempiyaṉ marapiṉaṉē!”
“malaimēluḷḷa maraṅkaḷellām kaṭṭerittuc cāmpa lākumpaṭi viḻum iṭiyēṟupōla, nī ik kōṭṭaikku veḷiyē nallaṉa yāvaṟṟaiyum aḻikkiṟāy.”
“malaiyiṉaṭiyil kukaiyiṉuḷḷē oḷintu aintalaiyuṭaiya naccup pāmpu uṟaivatupōla, pittaḷai vēynta vella muṭiyāta kōṭṭaikkuḷ pala yāṉaikaḷaiyuṭaiya vēntaṉ patuṅkiyirukkiṟāṉ.”31
maṟṟoru pāṭṭu varumāṟu:-
“oru puṟāvaik kāppaṟṟa eṇṇit taṉṉaiyē niṟai kōliṉ taṭṭukaḷil ēṟṟi eṭaiyiṭṭavaṉ marapil vantavaṉē! ettakaiya aṉpaṟac ceyalkaḷai nī ceytālum avaṟṟāl uṉ pukaḻmarapai nī mikutippaṭutta muṭiyātu.”
“uṉ pukaḻmikka muṉṉōrkaḷai eṇṇumpōtu, avarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉ vāṉakattil ilaṅkiya viyattaku māyak kōṭṭaikaḷaiyē etirttaḻittāṉ! nī eṉṉa veṟṟi peṟṟālum uṉ veṟṟi marapukku eṉṉa muṉṉēṟṟamum ēṟpaṭātu.”
“vēkamikka pōrkkutiraikaḷaiyuṭaiya vaḷavaṉē! ātti mālai aṇintavaṉē! etirikaḷait tākkuvatil irumpupōṉṟa karam uṭaiyavaṉē! poṉ aḷikkum uyar imaya malaimītu taṉ viṟkoṭi poṟitta periya aḻakiya tēraiyuṭaiya vāṉavaṉai vella vañci muṟṟukaiyilnī muṉaintuḷḷāy. uṉ vallamaiyai nāṉ evvāṟu pāṭuvēṉ?”32
maṟṟum oru pulavaratu pāṭal varumāṟu:-
“nī koṉṟaḻippāyō! kāppāṟṟuvāyō! etu nallateṉpatai nīyē aṟivāy.”
“poṉṉiṟak kaḻaṟkāykaḷuṭaṉ vaḷaiyum cilampu maṇinta iḷamakaḷir viḷaiyāṭum maṇal mikunta porunaik karaiyilē,  kollaṉ vāḷarattāl arāvik kūrākkappaṭṭa nīṇṭa piṭiyuṭaiya kōṭalikaḷ matiṟpuṟattē maraṅkaḷai veṭṭic cāykkum ōcai kōṭṭaikkuḷ araṇmaṉaiyilēyē etirolittuk koṇṭi runtum (kōḻaiyaippōlat), taṉ talainakarattukkuḷ aṭaipaṭṭuk kiṭakkum ōr aracaṉuṭaṉ pōrpurivataṟku (uṉ paṭaicūḻa), mālaiyaṇinta muracu muḻaṅka nī celvatu eṉpatu māṉakkēṭāṉa ceyalōkum.”33
kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ mutalil ilaṅkaiyil mēlkaraip pakutiyil āṭci ceyta nācu aracaṉ vālaivaṉaṉ makaḷākiya pīlivaḷaiyai maṇantukoṇṭāṉ34. itaṉaiyaṭuttu avaṉ taṉ maṉaiviyāka, tirumālē vāmaṉaṉākap piṟantu vella muṉainta purāṇa aracaṉāṉa māvaliyiṉ marapil piṟantavaṉāka urimai kōriya (perumpālum mahiṣamaṇṭalam allatu maicūrukku uriyavaṉāy irukkak kūṭiya) ōr aracaṉ makaḷāṉa cītattakkaiyai ēṟṟāṉ35. cītattakkai mūlam avaṉukku utayakumāraṉ eṉṟu oru makaṉ iruntāṉ. avaṉ aḻakum, pukaḻum, vāynta iḷaiñaṉā yiruntāṉ. āṉāl, ōriravu taṟceyalāka avaṉ kolaik kāḷāṉāṉ.36
itaṉpiṉ aracaṉ nāka iḷavaracimūlam piṟanta taṉ maṟṟa makaṉukkuc colliyaṉuppiyatākat terikiṟatu. avaḷ taṉ makaṉai maṇipallavattiliruntu kāvirippaṭṭiṉam vanta oru vaṇikak kappalil aṉuppivaittāḷ. vaḻiyil, iruṇṭu puyalārnta ōr iravil, kappal celvōr oru tīvil iṟaṅkiṉārkaḷ. iḷavaracaṉai aṅkēyē viṭṭuviṭṭu, puyalamaintatum kappalil ceṉṟu viṭṭārkaḷ. iḷavaracaṉ illātatu kaṇṭatum, avarkaḷ tirumpic ceṉṟu, attīvaiyum aṇimaik kaṭaṟkaraiyaiyum turuvi eṅkum tēṭiṉārkaḷ. etuvum payaṉillātupōyiṟṟu.
iḷavaracaṉ kāṇamaṟpōṉa tuyaramikka ceyti intira viḻā toṭaṅkappaṭum camayattilēyē kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukku eṭṭiṟṟu. aracaṉ viḻāvaik kavaṉikkātu viṭṭu viṭṭu makaṉait tēṭap puṟappaṭṭāṉ.
kaṭaliṉ oru pēralai iccamayam kāvirippaṭṭiṉattiṉ mītu paravi annakarai aḻittatu.
annāḷaiya mata nampikaiyuṭaiya makkaḷāl, vāṉavar vēntaṉāṉa intiraṉuṭaiya viḻāvai naṭattāta kuṟṟattiṟkāka makkaḷait taṇṭikkavē, kaṭal teyvamākiya maṇimēkalai anta alaiyai aṉuppiṉāḷ eṉṟu nampappaṭṭatu.37
itaṉaiyaṭuttuc cila nāṭkaḷukkuḷ, kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ kuḷamuṟṟam eṉṉum iṭattil uyir nīttāṉ. avaṉ uyir nīppiṉpōtu uṭaṉirunta kaviñarkaḷuḷ oruvar appōtu tamakku ēṟpaṭṭa uṇarccikaḷaik kīḻvarum pāṭalil tantuḷḷār.38
“valimai vāynta tērkaḷaiyum veṟṟip paṭaikaḷaiyum uṭaiya vaḷavaṉ uyiraik kavaravanta kūṟṟuvaṉ maṉṉaṉaik kaippaṟṟum tōṟṟattuṭaṉō āṭkoṇṭu cellum pāvaṉai yuṭaṉō vantiruntāl, am maṉṉaṉ cīṟṟattukkut tappiyirukka māṭṭāṉ. orupōtum avvāṟirātu. avaṉ pukaḻpāṭum pāṇaṉpōla ēntiya kaiyuṭaṉ avaṉ uyirai irantu vēṇṭiyē vantirukkavēṇṭum.”
kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉukkuppiṉ āṇṭavaṉ irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa maṟṟoru kiḷḷi āvāṉ. avaṉ pāriya vēḷvi naṭattiṉāṉ. ap peruviḻāviṟku avaṉ aṇṭaiyayal aracarkaḷāṉa ukkirapperuvaḻutiyaiyum cēra aracaṉ māveṇkō allatu vaṉappu vāynta aracaṉaiyum aḻaittāṉ. av viḻāvil kalantukoṇṭa avvaiyār oruṅkē vīṟṟirunta mūvaracarkaḷaiyum nōkkik kīḻvarumāṟu pāṭiṉāṉ :- 39
“vēntarkaḷē! koṭiyamainta tērum veṇkoṟṟak kuṭaisayam vāykkappeṟṟa kōmāṉkaḷē! nīṅkaḷ iṉṟupōl eṉṟum ēntiya kaiyarāṉa pārppaṉarukku akkaikaḷil poṟ pūkkaḷai nīruṭaṉ aḷittu, pūṇaṇintu, polivuṟṟa uṅkaḷ paṇippeṇṭir poṟkālattil ēntiyaḷitta iṉiya matuvuṇṭu uṅkaḷaip pukaḻum pāvalarukku vilaiyēṟiya paricilkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvīrkaḷāka!”
“ivvulaka vāḻvai viṭṭakalumpōtu nīṅkaḷ ippōtu ceyyum aṟaviṉai maṭṭumē uṅkaḷukkut tuṇainiṟkum.”
“maṇṇurimaiyuṭaṉ nīṅkaḷ mūvarum iṅkē oruṅkirukkum kāṭci irupiṟappāḷar ōyāviḻippuṭaṉ pakalumiravum pēṇum tiruvārnta muttīyiṉ kāṭcipōṉṟatu.”
“nīṅkaḷ nalamuṭaṉ vāḻvīrāka! vāṉattu mīṉkaḷ pōlavum maḻai nīrttuḷikaḷ pōlavum eṇṇaṟṟa palavāṉa nāṭkaḷ nīṅkaḷ vāḻvīrāka!”
pāṇṭiyarkula marapukkoṭi

6 pāṇṭiyar
māṇpukaḻk karikāla cōḻaṉuṭaṉ camakālattavaṉāṉa pāṇṭiya aracaṉ āriyappaṭaikaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ. atāvatu āriyariṉ paṭaikaḷaiveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āvāṉ.1
tekkaṇattiluḷḷa āriya aracarkaḷuḷ ētō oruvaṉ avaṉ āṭciyiṉpōtu tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭaiyeṭutta tākat terikiṟatu. pāṇṭiya aracaṉ avarkaḷmītu kuṟippiṭṭat takka toru peruveṟṟipeṟṟu avarkaḷait tiruppit turattiṉāṉ. itaṉāl avaṉukku āriyappaṭai kaṭantavaṉ eṉṟa pukaḻppeyar kiṭṭiṟṟu.
ip peruveṟṟi paṟṟiya vēṟu vivaram etuvum ippōtu namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa tamiḻ nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭavillai. āyiṉum cilappatikāram eṉṟa peruṅkāppiyattil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa putumai vāynta avaṉ muṭivu mūlam avaṉ niṉaivu piṉ marapiṉariṭaiyē nīṭittuḷḷatu.2
araciyiṉ cilampukaḷil oṉṟait tiruṭiyavaṉ eṉṟa aiyuṟaviṉ mītu, kāvirippaṭṭiṉattiluḷḷa vaṇikaṉāṉa kōvalaṉaik kolaiceyyumpaṭi avaṉ araṇmaṉaik kāvalarkaḷukku uttaraviṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. āṉāl av vaṇimaṇi araciyiṉuṭaiyataṉṟu eṉpatai avvaṇikaṉ maṉaivi kaṇṇaki maṉṉaṉ uḷaṅkoḷḷa meyppittu viṭṭāḷ.
kaṇṇaki taṉ tāyakattuc cōḻa maṉṉar marapaik kuṟippiṭṭu, avarkaḷil oruvaṉ oru kaḻukiṉ avā niṟaivēṟṟi oru puṟāvaik kāppāṟṟat taṉ tacaiyaiyē aṟuttut tantataiyum, tērai ōr āṉ kaṉṟiṉmītu celutti ataṉ uyirai māytta kuṟṟattukkāka maṟṟoruvaṉ taṉ makaṉaiyē koṉṟataiyum eṭuttu iṭitturaittiruntāḷ. māṉamum veṭkamum mīkkūra maṉṉaṉ uṇarviḻantu taviciliruntu kīḻē viḻuntāṉ. viḻuntavaṉ mīṇṭum eḻavēyillai. avaṉ araciyum avaṉ citaiyēṟi uyir nīttāḷ.
iv varacaṉ pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷil oṉṟu kalviyiṉ nalaṅkaḷaip pukaḻntu, tam piḷḷaikaḷaik kalvi payiṟṟuvikku māṟu kuṭikaḷai valiyuṟutti vēṇṭukiṉṟatu. punāṉūṟṟil iṭampeṟṟu namakkuvanteṭṭiyuḷḷa ap pāṭal varumāṟu:- 3
“vēṇṭutal aṟintu āciriyarukku utavuka. avarukku niṟaiyapporuḷ taruka, avarai vāymaiyuṭaṉ piṉpaṟṟik kalvipeṟuka, taṉ piḷḷaikaḷuḷḷēyē kallātavaṉait tāy virumpamāṭṭāḷ. kuṭumpattil piṟantavaruḷ mutaṉmai mūttava ṉukkalla. aṟiviṉ mikkavaṉai aracaṉum virumpi aḻaittu aṟivurai kōruvāṉ. nālāya vakuppukkaḷuḷ, iḻinilaippaṭṭa oruvaṉ kaṟṟavaṉāṉāl, uyarvuṭaiyavaṉ kūṭa avaṉiṭam ceṉṟu avaṉvaḻi niṟpāṉ.”
neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ tiṭīr muṭivukēṭṭu koṟkaiyil maṇṭalat talaivaṉāy amarntiruntu āṇṭa pāṇṭiya iḷavaracaṉ veṟṟivēṟ ceḻiyaṉ maturaikku viraintu vantu aracirukkai ēṟiṉāṉ.4
mikac curuṅkiyakāla āṭcikkuppiṉ avaṉ iṟantu, avaṉ makaṉ iraṇṭām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ ciṟuvaṉāyirukkumpōtē aracaṉāṉatākat terikiṟatu.
avaṉ paṭṭattukku vantavuṭaṉē, nāṭu cōḻarkaḷāl paṭaiyeṭukkappaṭṭatu. cōḻar paṭai maturaivarai muṉṉēṟiṟṟu, āyiṉum pāṇṭiyap paṭaittalaivaṉāṉa paḻaiyaṉmāṟaṉ oru mutaltaramāṉa veṟṟiyai aṭaintu, paṭaiyeṭuttavarkaḷait taṅkaḷ nāṭṭukkē ōṭṭiṉāṉ.
iḷaiyōṉāṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ ip pōr nikaḻcciyil uṭaṉiruntatākat terikiṟatu. paṭaiyeṭuppaip paṟṟik kēḷvip paṭṭapōtu, taṉ nāṭṭiṉmītu paṭaiyeṭuttavarkaḷait turattu vatākac cūḷ eṭuttukkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. akkālat tamiḻaracar palaraip pōlavē, ceyyuḷiyaṟṟuvataip poḻutu pōkkākak koṇṭiruntavaṉ ivviḷamaṉṉaṉ. iccamayam avaṉ ceyyuḷiṟ kūṟiya cūḷurai kīḻvarumāṟu:-5
“eṉ māṟṟārākiya aracarkaḷ tammiṭam paṭaikkala mēntiya vīrarkaḷum tērkaḷum pōrkkutiraikaḷum tūṇkaḷai otta kālkaḷōṭu oli etiroliyuṭaiya periya maṇikaḷ tūṅkum kaḻuttaiyuṭaiya pāri yāṉaikaḷum uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu tarukki, iḷaiyōṉ eṉṟu eṉṉai eḷimaippaṭṭavaṉākak karuti, accuṟuttalum avamatippum kalanta coṟkaḷ vīci, eṉṉai etirttup paṭaiyeṭukkat tuṇintuḷḷaṉar.”
“kaḷattil mōtukiṟa pōril nāṉ avarkaḷai muṟiyaṭittu, avarkaḷ pōrmuracaṅkaḷuṭaṉē avarkaḷaic ciṟaikoḷḷēṉāyiṉ, tammaik kākkum āṟṟalaṟṟu etirikaḷiṭam cikkiviṭṭa koṭuṅkōlaṉ eṉṟu eṉkuṭikaḷ eṉṉait tūṟṟaṭṭum! kalaiyum aṟivum nirampiya māṅkuṭi marutaṉait talaivaṉākak koṇṭa ulakam pukaḻum kaviñar pāṭalukku nāṉ takutiyaṟṟava ṉākaṭṭum! ēḻaiyarukkō, eṉ ātaravu nāṭupavarukkō illai eṉṟu kūṟattakka iḻinilaiyai nāṉ aṭaiyaṭṭum!”
pāṇṭiyar paṭaiyuṭaṉ iccamayattilirunta kaviñar iṭaikkuṉṟūrkiḻār pāṇṭiyaṉ it taṟuvāyil koṇṭirunta tōṟṟattaik kīḻvarumāṟu pāṭukiṟār.6
“taṉ paḻaimaiyāṉa nakarukku veḷiyēyuḷḷa ēriyil kuḷittu vēmpiṉ oḷimikka taḻai mālaiyait talaiyilaṇintu, vīramikka ceḻiyaṉ taṉ muṉṉē muracamuḻaṅka yāṉaimītēṟip pōrkkaḷattukku vantuḷḷāṉ. avaṉetirē aṇivakuttu niṟuttappaṭṭa paṭaivīrarō eṇṇilar.”
“antō! iṉṟaiya vempōril ettaṉai pēr māḷātu piḻaittirukkappōkiṟārkaḷō?”
“kiṇkiṇi aṇinta avaṉ kālkaḷil ippōtu vīrak kaḻalkaḷ oḷitarukiṉṟaṉa. vaḷamikka cikaiyaṇiyirunta neṟṟimītu uḻiñait taḻaiyuṭaṉ kalanta oḷimikka vēppantaḻaikaḷ miṭaiyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aḻakiya kāppuk kaḷaṇinta kaikaḷil ippōtu valimai vāynta vil ēṟiyuḷḷatu. taṉicciṟappuṭaṉ ivvāṟu kāṭciyaḷikkum ivviḷaiñaṉ yār?”
“vāḻka ivaṉ! mārpil malarmālai kāṇappaṭṭālum, ciṟuvaṉāka iruntapōtu aṇinta poṉmalai iṉṉum kaḻaṟṟappaṭavillai. iṉṟutāṉ pālillā uṇavu avaṉukkup parimāṟappaṭṭatu.”
“etiriyiṉ paṭaikaḷ aṇiyaṇiyāka vantu tākkuvataik kaṇṭu avaṉ acaiyavillai. avarkaḷaikkaṇṭu avaṉ añca villai; ēḷaṉam ceyyavumillai. taṉ paṭaivīrar avarkaḷait tākki aḻittu, avarkaḷ kataṟalai vāṉam etirolikkumpaṭi ceytapōtum, avaṉ taṉ paṭaikaḷil veṟṟi kaṇṭu makiḻntā ṉillai; perumai koṇṭāṉillai!”
paṭaiyeṭuppāḷarkaḷait tiruppi ōṭṭuvatuṭaṉ avaṉ maṉaniṟaivu aṭaiyavillai. cōḻa nāṭṭukkē pōrait tiruppikkoṇṭu cellat tuṇivukoṇṭāṉ. tiṟamai vāynta  vilvīrarum, vēlvīrarum aṭaṅkiya oru perumpaṭai tiraṭṭi avarkaḷaic cōḻanāṭṭiṉ teṉ mākāṇamākiya miḻalaik kūṟṟattiṉuḷ celuttiṉāṉ.7 avaṉ talaivaṉ paḻaimaiyāṉa vēḷāḷar marapiṉaic cārnta evvi. evviyai muṟiyaṭittu avaṉ miḻalaik kūṟṟattait taṉ āṭciyuṭaṉ cērttuk koṇṭāṉ. attuṭaṉ avaṉ cēranāṭṭiṉ kīḻpuṟa mākāṇamāṉa muttūṟṟuk kūṟṟattaiyum veṉṟu kaikkoṇṭāṉ.
iv vīracceyalkaḷ aṇṭaiyayal aracar aṉaivariṉ poṟāmaiyaiyum kiḷaṟiṟṟu. cōḻarum, cērarum, cērntu oru māperiya kūṭṭu amaippu uṇṭupaṇṇiṉar. atil avarkaḷ tam paṭaikaḷai vēṟu aintu aracar paṭaikaḷuṭaṉ iṇaittaṉar. av varacarkaḷ potiyat talaivaṉ titiyaṉ, takaṭūrt talaivaṉ atiyamāṉ eḻiṉi, erumaiyūraṉ atāvatu erumai nāṭṭiṉ (maicūriṉ) āṭciyāḷaṉ, iruṅkōvēṇmāṉ, porunaṉ ākiyavarkaḷ.8
avarkaḷ pāṇṭiyaṉ valimaiyai noṟukkiviṭa eṇṇi ṉārkaḷ. āṉāl pāṇṭiyaṉ nerukkaṭikkēṟṟa niṟaitiṟamai yuṭaiyavaṉāṉāṉ. avaṉ avarkaḷ pōkkukaḷaik kūrntu kavaṉittu vantāṉ. oṉṟupaṭṭuc ceyalāṟṟavō, taṉ nāṭṭiṉuḷ pukuntu nilaṅkaḷai aḻivu ceyyavō avarkaḷuk vāyppa ḷikkāmal avaṉ tuṇintu avarkaḷ cantippiṭamākiya talaiyālaṅ kāṉattilēyēceṉṟu avarkaḷait tākkiṉāṉ.
tiṭumeṉak tākkappaṭṭālum, nēcappaṭaikaḷ vīṟuṭaṉ pōriṭṭaṉa. pōr oru muḻuppakalum mummuramāka naṭai peṟṟa piṉṉarē pāṇṭiyaṉ avarkaḷaik kaḷattiliruntu turatta muṭintatu. tamiḻakattiluḷḷa ellā maṉṉarum, talaivarkaḷum avarkaḷ paṭaikaḷiṉ poṟukkiyeṭutta uyirppakutikaḷum ippōril īṭupaṭṭaṉa. ikkāraṇattiṉālēyē, pāṇṭiyaṉ itil aṭaintaveṟṟi akkālattiṉ talaiciṟanta vīrac ceyalākak karutappaṭṭatu. avaṉ kuṭikaḷ aṭainta makiḻccikku ellai illai. avaṉ aracavaiyil pulavarkaḷum, pāṇarkaḷum vantu kuḻumi, annāḷaiya talaiciṟanta vīraṉeṉṟu avaṉai vāyārap pukaḻntārkaḷ.
itaṉpiṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ tāṉē nēraṭiyākac cēra nāṭṭukkuḷ oru paṭaiyeṭuppu naṭattiṉāṉ. taṉippaṭṭa kaṇṇiṉ vaṭivamaiti kāraṇamāka yāṉaikkaṇ eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭa maṉṉaṉ cēy ciṟaippiṭikkappaṭṭāṉ. cēraṉ ciṟaiyiliruntu tappiyōṭiṉāṉ9. neṭuñceḻiyaṉ avaṉai mēlkaṭaṟkaraivarai turatticceṉṟu muciṟi nakararukil avaṉai mīṇṭum tōṟkaṭittāṉ10. cēraṉiṉ kīḻ āṇṭa talaivaṉāṉa aḻumpilvēḷiṉ āṭcip pakuti avaṉiṭa miruntu paṟikkappaṭṭatu.11
pāṇṭiyaṉ kuṭṭanāṭṭut talaivarkaḷai veṉṟu an nāṭṭaiyum taṉ aracuṭaṉ cērttukkoṇṭāṉ. ‘tuḷuvar’ eṉṟa marapiṉariṭamiruntu muttuveḷḷil eṉṟa kaṭal tuṟaimukattaiyum, nākarkaḷiṭamiruntu (maṉṉar kuṭāviluḷḷa) cāliyūr eṉṟa peyarpeṟṟa vāṇikakkaḷattaiyum veṉṟāṉ.
iraṇṭām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ karikālaṉ māḷvukkuc cila nāḷ muṉṉart tavicēṟiṉāṉ. talaiyālaṅkāṉattu veṟṟikkup piṉ avaṉ apperuñcōḻaṉuṭaṉ naṭpuṟavu koṇṭiruntāṉ. kāvirippaṭṭiṉattuk kārikkaṇṇaṉ pāṭal itaṉaik kāṭṭu kiṟatu. karikālaṉaip paṟṟiya innūl kuṟippil itu muṉpē tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu.12 neṭuñceḻiyaṉ ‘veḷḷiyampalam’ eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭa maturaiyiluḷḷa civaṉ kōyilil māṇṭāṉ. ik kāraṇattāl ataṟkup piṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyattil avaṉ veḷḷiyampalattut tuñciya peruvaḻuti eṉṟu aḻaikkap paṭṭāṉ. avaṉ āṭcikkālam peritum ki. pi. 90 mutal 128 varai āyirukkakkūṭum.
iraṇṭām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ mutaṉmaiyāka matitta kaviñar māṅkuṭi marutaṉār āvar. avaṉaip pukaḻntu maturaikkāñci eṉṟa mullainilac ciṟu kāviyam13 iyaṟṟiyavar avarē. tam puravalaṉaippaṟṟik kaviñar mikac cīriya paṇpōviyam tīṭṭiyuḷḷār.14
atu varumāṟu :
“naṭpiṉ nī eṉṟum uṟutiyuṭaiyavaṉ. tēvarkaḷē uṉakku ācaikāṭṭi mayakki, poṉṉulakattaiyum, amutattaiyum vaḻaṅkuvatākak kūṟiṉālum, nī avaṟṟil vacappaṭamāṭṭāy. mikavum valimai vāynta maṉṉavar uṉṉai etirttālum iṉṉoruvar āṇaiyai nī eṟpatillai. teṉmalaikaḷil vāṇaṉ putaittuḷḷa taṅkam muḻutum uṉakkuk kiṭaittāl kūṭa, nī tīcceyal ceyya iṇaṅkamāṭṭāy. naṉmai ceyvatilēyē nī eṉṟum makiḻvāy.”
ip pāṭaliṉ nōkkam maṉṉaṉ maṉattai ulakiyal avākkaḷiliruntu viṭuvittut taṉ uyirnilaiyiṉ naṉṉilaiyil ava eḻuppuvatēyākum. teyvap paṟṟārvamuṭaiya muṉṉōrkaḷai niṉaivūṭṭi avarkaḷ muṉmātirikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟumpaṭi aracaṉukkuk kūṟum iṟuti aṭikaḷ varumāṟu : 15
“pala vēḷvikaḷ ceyta mutukuṭumiyaippōlavum, ulakiṉ paḻamperuṅ kalaikaḷ payiṉṟa talaiciṟanta kaviñar pārāṭṭiyuḷḷa nilantaru tiruviṉ neṭīyōṉaippōlavum, pala aṟivarkaḷāl uṉ vīramum, naṟkuṇaṅkaḷum, pukaḻapperukiṉṟaṉa. vellutaṟ kariyavaṟṟai veṉṟu, āṭciyellai parappiyum; aṟiviṟ ciṟantavar aṟintaṉayāvaiyum aṟintu uṉ pukaḻai nilai nāṭṭiyum nī kaṭal naṭuvē nilavum kālai eḻuñāyiṟṟaiyum, vīṇmīṉkaḷukkiṭaiyē tikaḻum muḻunilāvaiyum uṉ aracurimaik kuṭikkuriya iḷavaracar cūḻtara, iṟavāp pukaḻvāynta māṇpumikka māṟaṉ uḷḷiṭṭa pōrppukaḻk kōcar tuṇaitara, aimperuṅ kuḻuviṉ matippum, kuṭimaṉṉar kuḻāttiṉ matippum peṟṟu vāḻkiṟāy.”
“oḷimikka pūṇkaḷaṇinta uṉ paṇippeṇkaḷ poṟ kālattil ēntiya naṟumaṇamikka matuvai nāḷ tōṟum paruki apperumaiyuṭaṉ muḻuniṟaivāṇāḷ peṟṟu vāḻvāyāka!”
paṭaivīrarkaḷiṭam avaṉ kāṭṭiya aṉpu, paṭai vīṭṭil puṇpaṭṭavariṭam avaṉ koṇṭa karicaṉai ākiyavai nakkīrar iyaṟṟiya neṭunalvāṭaiyil viritturaikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.16
atu varumāṟu:-
“naḷḷiravil kuḷir naṭukkukiṟa vāṭaik kāṟṟu vīcukiṟatu. āyiṉum maṉṉaṉ pantam ēntiya orucila paṇi makkaḷuṭaṉ taṉ kūṭārattaiviṭṭup puṟappaṭukiṟāṉ. cēṇamiṭṭuc ciṟumaṇi kaḷāl oppaṉai ceyyappaṭṭa ciṟappu mikka pōrkkutirai oṉṟu avaṉpiṉṉē celkiṟatu.”
“muttukkōvaikaḷ tūkkiya veṇkuṭai oṉṟu maḻait tūṟaliliruntu kākkum muṟaiyil aracaṉ talaikku mēlākap piṭikkappaṭukiṟatu. iṭatu kaiyāl nīṇṭu toṅkum taṉ āṭaiyaip piṭittukkoṇṭu, aracarimaic ciṉṉamāṉa vāḷaik kaiyilēntiya kaṭṭuṭal vāynta ōr iḷaiñaṉ tōḷmītu valakkai iṭṭavaṇṇam avaṉ celkiṟāṉ.”
paṭaittalaivarkaḷil oruvaṉ vēppantār ‘cūṭṭiya vēlait tāṅkiyapaṭi muṉceṉṟu, muṉṉāḷ pōril puṇpaṭṭa vīrar ovvoruvaraiyum taṉittaṉi cuṭṭikkāṭṭa avaṉ nalamucāviṉāṉ.’
iraṇṭām neṭuñceḻiyaṉukkuppiṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirap peruvaḻuti aracaṉāṉāṉ. 48 pulavar muṉṉilaiyil tiruvaḷḷuvariṉ māḷāp pukaḻ nūlāṉa tirukkuṟaḷ araṅkēṟṟap paṭṭa avaik kaḷattiṉ aracaṉ eṉṟa muṟaiyil tamiḻ ilakkiya māṇavar ellārumē ivvaracaṉ peyarai aṟivar. ivvaracaṉukkuk kāṉappēr tanta ukkirap peruvaḻuti eṉṟa paṭṭappeyar uṇṭu. itaṉ poruḷāvatu, vella muṭiyāta kōṭṭaiyeṉṟu annāḷil karutappaṭṭa kāṉappēr eṉṟa araṇ vāynta nakarattai avaṉ veṉṟu taṉ āṭkikkīḻk koṇṭuvantāṉ eṉpatē.
ikkōṭṭaiyai veṉṟa ceytiyai ivvaracaṉaip pāṭiya pāṭaloṉṟu kīḻvarumāṟu pukaḻnturaikkiṟatu.17
“cāvāp pukaḻuṭaiya vēntē, vāḻi! māperuṅ kōṭṭaiyākiya kāṉappērai veṉṟu kaikkoṇṭa niṉ vīrattaik kaviñar pārāṭṭiyuḷḷār.”
“akkēṭṭaiyiṉ matilkaḷ vāṉattaic ceṉṟeṭṭiṉa eṉṟu tōṟṟiṉa. ataṉ ñāyilkaḷ viṇmīṉkaḷpōla maṉṉiṉa. kōṭṭaiyaic cūḻnta akaḻi kaṭalpōṉṟa ellaiyaṟṟa āḻa akalamuṭaiyatu. ataṟkappāluḷḷa muṭkāṭu ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirkaḷ eṉṟum nuḻaintatillai eṉṟu kūṟumaḷavu aṭarttiyāṉatu.”
“ik kōṭṭaiyai iḻanta vēṅkaimārpaṉ appēriḻappai eṇṇi nāḷtōṟum varuntukiṟāṉ. ēṉeṉil paḻukkak kāycciya irumpil ūṟṟiya nīrpōla, atu eṉṟum mīṇṭum perutaṟkariyatu.”
“uṉ etirikaḷ aḻivuṟṟu maṟakkappaṭakkaṭavar! uṉ vēl eṉṟum veṟṟiyē kāṇakkaṭavatu!”
ivvaracaṉap paṟṟi vēṟu etuvum teriyavillai. avaṉ āṭci orucila āṇṭukaḷukku mēṟpaṭa nīṭittirukkavillai eṉṟē peritum eṇṇattakum. avaṉai aṭuttuvanta aracaṉ naṉmāṟaṉ allatu nalla pāṇṭiyaṉ eṉpavaṉ. avaṉ avaik kaḷattiluḷḷa pulavar pāṭiya pāṭalkaḷuḷ iraṇṭu varumāṟu:18

“malarmālaiyaṇinta māṟaṉē! oru malaiyai villākavum, oru pāmpai  nāṇākavum koṇṭu oppaṟṟa ōr ampeytu acurariṉ mūvaraṇkaḷai aḻittuc cāvilā vāṉavarkaḷukku veṟṟi tēṭittanta kariya miṭaṟuṭaiya iṟaivaṉiṉ piṟaiyaṇiyap peṟṟa neṟṟiyil tulaṅkukiṉṟa anta oppaṟṟa kaṇṇaippōla, maṉṉariṭaiyē nī oppuyarvaṟṟavaṉāka viḷaṅkukiṟāy!”
“mēlīṭāyp pārppavarukku ōr aracaṉiṉ āṟṟal avaṉuṭaiya mūrkkamāṉa pōr yāṉaikaḷ, virainaṭaiyuṭaiya pōrkkutiraikaḷ, neṭuṅkoṭiyuṭaiya tērkaḷ, añcā neñcuṭaiya vīrarkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷaip poṟuttateṉṟu tōṟṟakkūṭum. āṉāl uṇmaiyil ataṉ kaṭaikāl naṭunilai nērmaiyē.”
“ākavē uṉ kuṭimakkaḷiṭam orutalaiccārpu kāṭṭātē. ayalnāṭṭavariṭam nērmai kēṭākavum naṭakkātē. veṅ katiravaṉaippōla vīṟum, meṉmai vāynta veṇṇilāp pōṉṟa taṇṇaḷiyum uṉ nāṭṭil vaṟumaiyoḻiya maḻaipoḻiyum mukiliṉaṅkaḷpōṉṟu aṉpātaravum uṭaiyavaṉāy iruppāyāka!”
“veṇṇuraiyai mukaṭṭil koṇṭa kaṭalalaikaḷ karaimītu ōyātu mōtum murukaṉukkuriya tirunilaivāynta centil eṉṉum aḻakiya tuṟaimukappaṭṭiṉattiṉarukē vaṉpāṟṟukkaḷ koṭuvantu kuvikkum maṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷiluḷḷa nuṇmaṇalkaḷ pōṉṟa eṇṇaṟṟa āṇṭukaḷ nī vāḻvāyāka!”
maṟṟoru pāṭal varumāṟu :-
“kaṭuñciṉattil nī kūṟṟuvaṉ pōṉṟavaṉ. etiraṟṟa valimaiyil nī palatēvaṉaippōṉṟavaṉ. pukaḻil, taṉṉaip pukaḻātavarkaḷai aḻikkum tirumāl pōṉṟavaṉ. tāṉ virumpiya aṉaittum muṭikkum tiṟaṉil nī murukak kaṭavuḷ pōṉṟavaṉ. uṉṉāl iyalātatu yātu?”
“eṉavē uṉṉiṭam irappavarkkut taṅkutaṭaiyiṉṟit tārāḷamāy vāri vaḻaṅku.”
“yavaṉar tam nalla kappalkaḷil koṇṭu varukiṟa kuḷinta matuvai, oḷimikka vaḷaikaḷaik kaikaḷil aṇinta uṉ paṇip peṇkaḷ aḻakiya poṟkalattil ēnta, ataṉai nāḷtōṟum paruki uṉ nāṭkaḷai iṉpamākak kaḻippāyāka!”
“veṟṟi vāḷ ēntiya māṟa! iruḷakaṟṟum taḻal pōṉṟa katirparappum katiravaṉpōlavum, mālaiyil oḷi tarum taṇkatirparappum iṉiya veṇṇilāppōlavum nilavulakiṉ maṉṉaruḷ talaiciṟantavaṉāka nīṭu vāḻvāyāka!”
immaṉṉaṉ ilavantikaippaḷḷi eṉṟa iṭattil māḷvuṟṟa taṉāl avaṉ piṉ vanta pulavarkaḷāl ilavantikaip paḷḷit tuñciya naṉmāṟaṉ eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭāṉ.
cērar kuṭimarapuk koṭi
mutalām ātaṉ
(ki. pi. 40-55)

(veṇṇil pōril mutukil puṇpaṭṭu vaṭakkiruntiṟantavaṉ)

iraṇṭām ātaṉ = cōṉai, (karikāla cōḻaṉ makaḷ)
(ki. pi. 55-90)

(vāṉavarampaṉ allatu celvakkaṭuṅkō)


aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
iyal - 6 pāṇṭiyar

6.	puṟam 77-79.

8.	akam 36.

18.	puṟam 55, 56

7. cērar
nām eṭuttukkoṇṭa kālattiya mutal cēra aracaṉ mutalām ātaṉē. avaṉ karikāla cōḻaṉ kālattavaṉ. karikālaṉ varalāṟṟil ēṟkaṉavē kuṟittuḷḷapaṭi, veṇṇil pōril1 karikālaṉait taṉ paṭaikaḷiṉ talaimaiyil etirttuc camarceyta pōtu, avaṉ mutukil puṇpaṭṭāṉ. itaṉ avamatippaip poṟukkamāṭṭāmal, avaṉ vaṭakkiruntu iṟakkat tuṇintāṉ. māḷvilum avaṉai viṭṭuppiriya maṉamillāta pala ārvat tuṇaivarkaḷ iccamayam tāmum maṉamāra avaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟiruntu uyirviṭṭaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.2
mutalām ātaṉaiyaṭuttu aracirukkai ēṟiyavaṉ celvakkaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭappeyar koṇṭa iraṇṭām ātaṉ āvāṉ. karikālaṉ makaḷ cōṇaiyai maṇantataṉmūlam karikālaṉ naṭpaip peṟṟāṉ3. akkālat tamiḻ aracarkaḷuḷ vallamai mikka pēraracaṉ marumakaṉ eṉṟa muṟaiyil avaṉ āṭci amaitiyum vaḷamum uṭaiyatāyiruntatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu.
pārppaṉarāṉa kapilar inta aracaṉiṉ taṉi naṭappukkuriya varāyiruntār. iṉpamāka aḷavaḷāvikkoṇṭirukkum nērattil orunāḷ cēra aracaṉ kaviñar kaiyaip paṟṟi eṭuttu, atu evvaḷavu meṉmaiyāyiruntatu eṉpataik kūrntu nōkki viyantāṉ. pukaḻccik kalaiyil valla kapilar maṉṉaṉ kaiyiṉ vīra valimaiyaip pukaḻntu, attakaiya vīraṉ ātaravil vāḻum kaviñarkaḷiṉ kaikaḷ melliyaṉavāka irāmal vēṟu eppaṭi irukka muṭiyum eṉṟu aracaṉ pārāṭṭai nayam paṭa avaṉ ticaiyil tiruppiṉār.
iccamayam avar pāṭiya pāṭaliṉ karuttu varumāṟu 4:-
“vīramikka aracaṉē! uṉ taṭantōḷkaḷ aḻakumikka aṇaṅkukaḷukkuk kātalnōy ūṭṭupavai. atē camayam avai uṉ etirikaḷukku accam tarupavai. uṉ taṭakkaikaḷō, poṉ vēynta aṅkucattāl yāṉaiyai ūkki irumput tāḻiṭṭa uṉ etirikaḷiṉ camaṉ ceyyappaṭṭa karaikaḷaiyuṭaiya āḻnta akaḻikaḷaittāvumpaṭi vīṟumikka uṉ pērkkutiraiyai naṭattic cellumvaṇṇam kaṭivāḷaṅkaḷaip piṭittiḻup pavaiyum avaiyē! tavira nī tēril cellumpōtu uṉ tōḷmītu toṅkum amput tūṇiyiliruntu ampukaḷai eṭuttu avaṟṟai eyyumpaṭi villaivāṅki viṟalpaṭa vaḷaippavaiyum avaiyē! ittakaiya āṟṟal vāynta ceyalkaḷac ceyvataṉāl, uṉ tōḷkaḷ nīṇṭu tiraṇṭum, kaikaḷ tuvaṉṟu valimai peṟṟum uḷḷaṉa.”
“āṉāl uṉṉaip pāṭum pulavarkaḷukkō, ventu poṅkiya cōṟum, neyyil poritta iṟaicciyum, maṇamūṭṭiya kaṟikaḷum, tiṉpataṉṟi vēṟu vēlai kiṭaiyātu. itaṉāl avarkaḷ kaikaḷ meṉmai vāyntavaiyāyviṭṭaṉa.”
maṟṟoru camayam pulavar aracaṉaippaṟṟit tuṇikaramāṉa kaṟpaṉaiyum uvamai nayamum mikka oru pāṭalaip pāṭiṉār. appāṭal katiravaṉai nōkkip pāṭappeṟṟuḷḷatu. katiravaṉukkum maṉṉaṉukkumiṭaiyē atu pala oppumai vēṟṟumaikaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭi, ovvoṉṟilum katiravaṉaiviṭak kāvalaṉē mēmpaṭuvataik5 kaviñar nayampaṭa viḷakkiyuḷḷār.6
“vaiyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻukap
pōkam vēṇṭip potuccoṟ poṟāatu
iṭañciṟi teṉṉum ūkkam turappa
oṭuṅkā uḷḷat tōmpā īkaik
kaṭantaṭu tāṉaic cēral ātaṉai
yāṅkaṉam ottiyō, vīṅkucelal maṇṭilam?
poḻutoṭu varaiti, puṟakkoṭut tiṟatti;
māṟivaruti, malaimaṟain toḷitti;
akaliru vicumpiṉāṉum
pakalviḷaṅ kutiyāl, palkatir virittē!”
itu muṟṟilum collaṇit tiṟamāyiṉum, uvamai oppīṭu muṟṟilum putumai nayamuṭaiyatāyirukkiṟatu. ippukaḻcci yāl aracaṉ mikavum makiḻntirukkavēṇṭumeṉpatil aiyamillai. pulavarukku avaṉ pala ūrkaḷai itaṟkup paricāka aḷittāṉ.
iraṇṭām ātaṉukku iru putalvarkaḷ iruntaṉar. mūttavaṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ. iḷaiyavarē iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, aḵtāvatu araca tuṟavi āvar. piṉ kūṟappaṭṭavarē cilappatikāra kāviyam pāṭiyavar. tam vāḻkkai nikaḻcciyoṉṟai ivar ikkāviyattil varaintuḷḷār.7
oru camayam iḷaṅkōvum avar uṭaṉ piṟantāṉum avarkaḷ tantai vīṟṟirunta aracirukkaiyiṉ arukē kolu maṉṟattil amarntiruntaṉar. ac camayam ōr aṟivar aracaṉmuṉ tōṉṟiṉār. avar maṉṉaṉ mukappolivuṭaṉ iru putalvarkaḷiṉ mukappolivukaḷaiyum kūrntu nōkkiṉār. maṉṉaṉ viraiyil vāḻkkai niṟaiveytuvāṉ eṉṟum, iḷaiya putalvaṉē aracaṉāvataṟkuriya aṟikuṟiyuṭaiyava ṉeṉṟum varuṅkuṟiyuraittār.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ tam uṭaṉ piṟantāṉiṭam uṇmaiyāṉa paṟṟuṭaiyavarāyiruntār. ākavē aracirukkaikkut tāṉ varakkūṭum eṉṟa nampikkai muṟṟilum aṟum vaṇṇam, akkaṇamē ulaka vāḻkkai tuṟantu nikaṇṭavātikaḷukkuriya tuṟavaṟam mēṟkoḷḷat tuṇintār.
maṉṉaṉ cikkaṟpaḷḷiyil uyirnīttāṉ. itu mutal avaṉ cikkaṟpaḷḷit tuñciya celvak kaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉ, atāvatu cikkaṟpaḷḷiyil uyirnītta vīra maṟam vāynta maṉṉaṉ ātaṉ eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭāṉ.
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṟa imayavarampaṉ tantaiyākiya iraṇṭām ātaṉukkuppiṉ ēṟattāḻak ki.pi.90-il tavicēṟiṉāṉ. paṭṭayattukku vanta ciṟitu nāḷilellām avaṉ viyalūraik kaippaṟṟiṉāṉ. innakaram iruṅkōvēṇ māṉiṉ talainakaram. avaṉ nāṭu malaiyaṭarntatu. atil taṅkam veṭṭi yeṭukkappaṭṭatu.8
itaṉaiyaṭuttuc cila nāṭkaḷukkuḷ cōḻar tavicu karikāla cōḻaṉiṉ peyaraṉum ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ cēraṉiṉ maittuṉaṉu māṉa kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukku urimaiyāyiṟṟu. cōḻa iḷavaracar oṉpatiṉmar avaṉukketirākak kiḷarnteḻuntaṉar. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ oṉpatu iḷavaracaraiyum nērivāyil eṉṉumiṭattil muṟiyaṭittut taṉ maittuṉaṉ aracurimai kāttāṉ.9
itaṉpiṉ avaṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭiṉ teṉpakutiyil āṇṭa paḻaiyaṉmāṟaṉiṉ talainakarāṉa mōkūrait tākkiṉāṉ. paḻaiyaṉ māṟaṉāl tīṅkukku āḷāṉa aṟukai eṉṟa talaivaṉukkākavē avaṉ inta naṭavaṭikkaiyilīṭupaṭṭāṉ10. patiyiḻanta tāy cōṇai kaṅkai nīrāṭuvataṟkāka avaṉ vaṭaticai ceṉṟāṉ. pala āṇṭukaḷ kaḻittup putumaivāynta cūḻnilaiyil avaṉ mīṇṭum kaṅkai cellavēṇṭi vantatu. annikaḻcci cilappatikārattil kīḻvarumāṟu viritturaikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu11:-
“vāṉavarkōṉ kōtai kūrvāḷ ēntiyavaṉ. kaṭal cūḻnta kaṭampu eṟintavaṉ. imayamalaiyiluḷḷa vāṉavar kaṇṭu viyakkum paṭi taṉ viṟkoṭiyai imayamalaimītu poṟittavaṉ.”
“orunāḷ avaṉ taṉ araciyuṭaṉ veḷḷimāṭattē vīṟṟiruntāṉ. appōtu avaṉ pacuṅkāṭukaḷ niṟainta malaikaḷaik kāṇacceṉṟāṉ. ammalaikaḷiṉmītu mukilkaḷ nīṅkātu taṅkiṉa. ataṉ aruvikaḷ iṭaiviṭātu iṉṉōcai yuṭaṉ āravārittaṉa. avaṉuṭaṉ pūñcōlaikaḷil āṭi viḷaiyāṭum eṇṇaṅkoṇṭa vāṉavar makaḷir palar vañci nakariliruntu ceṉṟiruntaṉar. aṉaivarum periyāṟṟiṉ karaiyilirunta oru maṇaṟkuṉṟiṉ mītu taṅkiṉārkaḷ.”
“periyāṟu ivviṭattilēyē malaikaḷaiviṭṭiṟaṅkit tāḻ nilaṅkaḷai nōkkip pāykiṟatu. ataṉ nīr paravic ciṟu tīvukaḷaiyum, cōlaikaḷaiyum, māṭakūṭaṅkaḷaiyum, ampalaṅ kaḷaiyum, aḷāvic ceṉṟatu. iṅkiruntu aracaṉ cuṟṟiyuḷḷa aḻakiya kāṭcikaḷaik kaṇṭu makiḻntāṉ. āṟu taṉ parappiṉmītu palvakai malarkaḷai ēntikkoṇṭu ceṉṟatu. malaikkuṟavar āṭalpāṭalkaḷ kaṇṭu maṉṉaṉum maṅkaiyarum makiḻntārkaḷ.”
“kāṭṭu yāṉaikaḷaik kuḻippoṟiyil vīḻttiyum, tēṉ kūṭukaḷaik kiḻittum vēṭarkaḷ ceyta āravāramum, aruviyiṉ ārpparippum, yāṉaikaḷiṉ piḷiṟalum, aracaṉuṭaṉ vanta vīrarkaḷiṉ kavacaṅkaḷiṉ calacalappum cērntu avarkaḷ cevikaḷil olittavaṇṇam iruntaṉa.”
“aracaṉum avaṉ tuṇaivarum ivvāṟu iṉpuṟṟirunta vēḷaiyil vēṭarkaḷ yāṉaiyiṉ veṇ tantaṅkaḷ, maṇaṅ kamaḻum akil kaṭṭaikaḷ, kavarimayir viciṟikaḷ, tēṉ kuṭam, cantaṇak kaṭṭaikaḷ, cātiliṅkam, vīram, kentaka añcaṉakkal, ēlakkāy, miḷakukkuvaikaḷ, kūvalkiḻaṅku māvu, iṉiya kavalaik kiḻaṅkukaḷ, tēṅkāy, māmpaḻam, palāppaḻaṅkaḷ, pacuntaḻai, uḷḷi, karumpu, taḻaikkoṭi mālaikaḷ, pākku, vāḻaik kulaikaḷ, ciṅkak kuruḷaikaḷ, yāḷikkuruḷaikaḷ; pulikkuṭṭikaḷ, yāṉaikkaṉṟukaḷ, kuraṅkuk kuṭṭikaḷ, karaṭikkuṭṭikaḷ, malai yāṭukaḷ, malai māṉkaḷ; kattūrimāṉ kuḻavikaḷ, kīrikaḷ, mayilkaḷ, puṉukuppūṉaikaḷ, kāṭṭuk kōḻikaḷ, kiḷikaḷ ākiya vaṟṟaic cumantavaṇṇam avaṉaik kāṇa vantaṉar.”
“malai vaḷaṅkaḷāṉa taṅkaḷ viḷaiporuḷkaḷaik kāṇikkaiyāka avaṉmuṉ vaittu avarkaḷ vaṇaṅki vāḻttiṉārkaḷ.”
“vāḻka, nīṭu veṟṟiyuṭaṉ vāḻka, aracē!”
“eḻupiṟappum nāṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ kuṭiyurimaiyuṭaiyōm.”
“kāṭṭilē vēṅkai marattiṉ niḻalilē oru mārpakamiḻanta aḻakār aṇaṅku orutti taṉiyē kiṭantu tuṉpuṟṟu uyirnīttāḷ. avaḷ ennāṭṭiṉaḷ. ekkuṭiyiṉaḷ eṉṟu yām aṟiyōm.”
“paṉṉūṟāyiram āṇṭunī aḷuti, aracē!”
“maṉṉaṉuṭaṉirunta iṉtamiḻ ācāṉ cāttaṉ maṉṉaṉ nalamārnta kāṭci kaṇṭu makiḻntu, maṉṉaṉukkum, maṉṉaracikkum tuyarārnta avvaṇaṅkiṉ varalāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.”
“avaṉ uraittāvatu.”
“avaḷ kāvirippaṭṭiṉattiluḷḷa oru vaṇikaṉ maṉaivi. an nakarattiluḷḷa ōr āṭal aḻakiyiṭam taṉ celvam yāvum pōkkik kaṇavaṉ taṉ kaṟpuṭaiya maṉaiviyuṭaṉ maturaikku vantāṉ. ap peṇṇiṭam appōtu irunta orē celvam avaḷ kāl cilampukaḷ iraṇṭumē. kaṇavaṉ avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟai viṟṟu, ataṟku vilaiyākak kiṭaitta paṇattaikkoṇṭu putitāka vaṇika vāḻkkai toṭaṅka eṇṇiṉāṉ.”
“taṉ pōtākkālamāka, avaṉ maṉṉaṉ poṟkollaṉai aṭaintāṉ. appoṟkollaṉ ēṟkeṉavē araciyiṉ       cilampukaḷil oṉṟait tiruṭiyiruntavaṉ. ākavē ayalāṉoru vāṉal tiruṭupōṉa cilampē viṟpaṉai nāṭi vantirukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. tiruṭaṉaik koṇṭēkavanta kāvalariṭam vaṇikaṉaik kāṭṭiṉāṉ.”
“tiṭumeṉa nērnta nikaḻcci māṟāṭṭattāl tikaittu niṉṟa vaṇikaṉ cilampu taṉ kaiyil vanta vakaiyaik kāvalariṭam viḷakkikkūṟa muṭiyātavaṉāy niṉṟāṉ. avaṉaiyē tiruṭaṉeṉṟu aiyuṟṟa kāvalar avviṭattilēyē avaṉaik kolaiceytu cilampai araṇmaṉaikkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar.”
“vaṇikaṉ kollappaṭṭa avalac ceyti avaṉ maṉaivi cevikaḷil paṭṭatu. ac camayam avaḷ āyarpāṭiyil taṅki yiruntāḷ. kaṇkaḷiliruntu nīr oḻukiya vaṇṇam, avaḷ kaṇavaṉ uṭal kuṟṟuyirāyk kurutipaṭintu kiṭanta iṭam ceṉṟāḷ. araciyiṉ cilampukaḷuḷ oṉṟait tiruṭiyatākak kuṟṟañcāṭṭappaṭṭut taṉ kaṇavaṉ kolaiyuṇṭāṉeṉṟa ceytiyai makkaḷiṭamiruntaṟintu ventuyarāṟāta patiyiḻanta anta aṇaṅku maṉṉaṉaik kāṭci nāṭi avaṉmuṉ ceṉṟāḷ. kaṇavaṉ kaiyilirunta cilampu pāṇṭiya aracikkuriya taṉṟeṉṟum, taṉatē eṉṟum avaḷ maṉṉaṉaṟiyak kāṭṭiṉāḷ.*”
“ceyta paḻikkañciya maṉṉaṉ uṇarviḻantu, taviciliruntu viḻuntu tirumpa eḻātu uyirviṭṭāṉ. araciyum avaṉ īma aḻalil pāyntu māṇṭāḷ.”
“vāḻviḻanta naṅkai taṉ mārpakaṅkaḷil oṉṟait tiruki atai maturait terukkaḷil eṟintāḷ. tīya paḻi cūḻnta annakaram tīyil aḻika eṉṟu varaṅkōriṉāḷ. itaṉpaṭi araṇmaṉaiyum nakariṉ oru pakutiyum tīkkiraiyāyiṉa.”
“avaḷ itaṉpiṉ maturaiyai viṭṭakaṉṟu mēl ticai vaḻiyākacceṉṟu cēranāṭaṭaintu aṅkē māṇṭāḷ.”
“ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉum avaṉ araciyum ivvaralāṟu kēṭṭu mikavum urukiṉar. avappēṟuṟṟu kaṟpuṭaiya an naṅkai teyvamāka vaḻipaṭuvataṟkuriyavaḷ eṉṟu araci kuṟittāḷ.”
“maṉṉaṉ ik karuttai ēṟṟāṉ. taṉṉaic cūḻntirunta aṟivāḷarai nōkki avarkaḷ aṟivurai kōriṉāṉ. potiya malaiyiliruntu oru kaṟcilai peyartturuvākki, ataik kāvariyil nīrāṭṭalām allatu imaya malaiyiliruntu kaṟcilai peyartturuvākkik kaṅkaiyil nīrāṭṭalām eṉṟu avarkaḷ karutiṉārkaḷ.”
“potiyattil kalleṭuttuk kāviriyil nīrāṭṭuvatu maṟakkuṭiyil vanta taṉpōṉṟavarkku aḻakalla eṉṟu maṉṉaṉ cāṟṟiṉāṉ. imayamalaiyiliruntē kalkoṇaruvateṉṟu tuṇintāṉ.”
“avaṉ amaiccaṉ villavaṉkōtai avaṉai nōkki ivvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.”
“pallāṇṭu veṟṟiyuṭaṉ vāḻvīrāka, aracē!”
“koṅku nāṭṭup pōrkkaḷattil tōṟṟōṭiya uṉ māṟṟaracarkaḷ taṅkaḷ pulikkoṭiyaiyum mīṉ koṭiyaiyum tuṟantuviṭṭucceṉṟuḷḷaṉar. um veṟṟiyiṉ pukaḻ ulakeṅkum parantuḷḷatu.”
“um aṉṉaiyārākiya araciyar kaṅkai nīrāṭiya camayam, tām koṅkaṇar, kaliṅkar, karunāṭar, paṅkaḷar, kaṅkar, kaṭṭiyar, vaṭavāriyar ākiyōr kūṭṭuppaṭaimītu tākkiya veṟṟit tākkutalai eṉ kaṇkaḷ eṉṟum maṟakkamāṭṭā.”
“ākavē teyvac cilaikkuk kal peṟumpaṭi imaya malaikkuc celvatu tam viruppamāyiṉ, atu paṟṟiya ceyti eḻuttu mūlam vaṭapula aracarukku aṉuppappaṭutal naṉṟu eṉṟāṉ.”
aḻumpil vēḷ ippōtu eḻuntu pēciṉāṉ.
“veḷināṭṭu nilavarat tūtarkaḷ vañciyiṉ vāyililēyē taṅkiyuḷḷārkaḷ. tam paṭaiccelaviṉ ceytiyai avvaracarkaḷ avarkaḷ mūlamē aṟintukoḷḷaṭṭum. payaṇa vivarattai nam talainakarattilēyē muracaṟaivittāl pōtumāṉatu eṉṟāṉ.”
maṉṉaṉum avaṉ kuḻāttiṉarum itaṉ piṉṉar viraivil vañcikkut tirumpivantārkaḷ.
maṉṉaṉ kaṭṭaḷaippaṭi kaṅkaikkuc cellavirukkum paṭaippōkku vivaram maṉṉurimai yāṉaimītu koṇṭu cellappaṭṭa muracaṅkaḷāl nakareṅkum paṟaicāṟṟappaṭṭatu.
“maṉṉaṉ tiruōlakkamaṇṭapam ēṟi, arimāṉuruvaṅkaḷāl tāṅkappaṭṭa irukkaiyil amarntāṉ. avaṉai varavēṟkak kuḻumik kāttirunta talaimaik kuruvum, talaimaikkaṇiyum, araciyaṟpēramaiccarkaḷum, paṭaipperuntalaivarkaḷum, “nīṭuvāḻka maṉṉavar maṉṉaṉ,” eṉṟu vāḻtteṭuttup piṉṉar, paṭaippōkkup paṟṟiya avaṉ kaṭṭaḷai yāteṉak kēṭṭaṉar.”
“paṭait talaivarkaḷaiyum, vīrappaṭai maṟavaraiyum nōkki maṉṉaṉ muḻaṅkiṉāṉ.”
“vaṭavaracar tamiḻ maṉṉarkaḷaippaṟṟi ēḷaṉamākap pēciṉar eṉṟu imayamalaiyiliruntuvanta yāttirikarkaḷ kūṟiṉar. ākavē avvaracarkaḷai muṟiyaṭittu, avarkaḷ tam talaiyiṉmītu teyvac cilaikkuriya kallai ēṟṟivarumpaṭi valukkaṭṭāyappaṭuttiyaṉṟi ip paṭaippōkkiṉiṉṟu mīḷēṉ” eṉṟāṉ.
“talaimaikkuru maṉṉaṉai nōkkik kaṭṭuraittāṉ.”
“kaṭuñciṉamāṟṟuka, imayavarampaṉē! vaṭavaracar ēḷaṉamākak pēciyatu uṉṉaiyeṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkillai. uṉṉaip pōrk kaḷattil etirēṟṟu niṟkattakkavar pāṇṭiyarum, cōḻarum aṉṟi vēṟu yār innilattil uḷar?”
“tañcameṉṟu vantōrkaḷukku ellām nīyēyaṉṟō takka kāppu?”
“vāṉakamaṉaikaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭaiyum, kōḷiṉaṅkaḷ, mīṉiṉaṅkaḷ, nilaikaḷaiyum vāṉkaṇippiṉ aintu uṟuppuk kaḷaiyum uḷḷaṭakkiya vāṉūluṇarnta talaimakkaṇi eḻuntu pēciṉāṉ:”
“vallamai poruntiya vēntē! veṟṟi eṉṟum niṉatākuka! ticaiyil intak kaṇamē puṟappaṭat tiruvuḷaṅkūruti yāṉāl, uṉ māṟṟaracarkaḷaṉaivarum vantu aṭivīḻvar” eṉṟāṉ.
“itukēṭṭu maṉṉaṉ, taṉ maṉṉurimai vāḷum, koṟṟak kuṭaiyum, vaṭaticai celka eṉak kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭāṉ.”
“maṟavar makiḻntu ārpparittārkaḷ. peru muracaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkiṉa. koṭikaḷ paṟantaṉa. aimperuṅkuḻuviṉarum, eṇ pērāyattārum, kurumāṟum, kaṇimāṟum, muṟaivarum amaiccarum, oruṅkukūṭi, ‘maṉṉaṉ vāḻka’ eṉṟu vāḻttiṉar. pūmālaivēynta maṉṉurimai vāḷum, koṟṟakkuṭaiyum maṉpērurimaiyuṭaṉ araca yāṉaimītu ēṟṟi nakarppuṟattiluḷḷa oru kōṭṭaikkuk koṇṭucellappaṭṭaṉa.”
“tiruvōlakka maṉṟamēṟi maṉṉaṉ itaṉpiṉ taṉpaṭait talaivarkaḷukkum araṇmaṉai vāyil talaivarkaḷukkum virun tāṭṭaḷittāṉ. ataṉpiṉ talaimītu vañcimālaiyaṇintu puṟappāṭṭāṉ. tirumālkōyil ceṉṟu vaḻivāṭu ceytapiṉ avaṉ taṉ yāṉai ēṟiṉāṉ. kōyil kuruviṭamiruntu civa perumāṉ tiruvaṭic ciṉṉam peṟṟu ataṉaip paṟṟārvattuṭaṉ taṉ talaiyilum, tōḷilum aṇintāṉ. poṟkōyililuḷḷa tirumāliṉ kōyilkuravar nalvāḻttuṭaṉ, kālāḷ, kutirai, tēr, yāṉai ākiya nāluṟuppukkaḷamainta paṭai puṭaicūḻa, paṭaittalaivar tuṇaivara, veṇkoṟṟak kuṭai niḻaliṉkīḻ yāṉaimītamarntu maṉṉaṉ vañciyaiviṭṭup puṟappaṭṭāṉ.”
“avaṉ nīlakirik kuṉṟukaḷil mutal taṅkal amaittāṉ.”
inta nīlakiri taṟkāla nīlakiriyāyt tōṉṟavillai. atu oricā mākāṇattil palacōr12 arukil vaṅkavirikuṭāviliruntu 16 allatu 18 kaltolaivil ceṅkuttāka niṉṟa oru malai. annāḷaiya paḻaiya kaṭalōṭikaḷāl atu nīlakiri eṉṟaḻaikkap paṭṭatu.
cēra nāṭṭiliruntu oricāvukkuc cellum payaṇam kaṭal vaḻiyē naṭaipeṟṟatākak kāviyattiṉ muṭivil kūṟappaṭum kuṟippāl terikiṟatu. paṭaiyuṭaṉ kaṭal kaṭantu kaṅkaik karaiyai aṭaintatāka aṅkē ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pukaḻap peṟukiṟāṉ.
“cēra aracaṉ nīlakiriyiṉ aṭivārattil paṭaivīṭṭil taṅkiyiruntapōtu, makatappēraracāṇṭa karṇariṭam paṇiceyta cañcayaṉ eṉṟa paṭaittalaivaṉ nūṟu āṭal naṅkaiyarkaḷuṭaṉum, irunūṟṟeṭṭup pāṭakarkaḷuṭaṉum, nūṟu kaḻaikkūttāṭi kaḷuṭaṉum, nūṟutēr, patiṉāyiram kutiraikaḷ, irupatiṉāyiram vaṇṭikaḷ, āyiram meykkāvalar ākiyavarkaḷuṭaṉum vantu maṉṉaṉaik kaṇṭāṉ.”
“vāyil kāppōṉ cañcayaṉum avaṉ paṭaiyum vantatu aṟivittāṉ. ataṉmītu avarkaḷait taṉ koluvirukkaikku varavaḻaikkumpaṭic cēramaṉṉaṉ kaṭṭaḷaiyiṭṭaruḷiṉāṉ.”
“cañcayaṉ itaṉpaṭi maṉṉaṉ muṉvantu talai tāḻtti niṉṟu vaṇaṅkik kīḻvarumāṟu ceytiyuraittāṉ.”
“nilavulakamāḷum maṉṉavaṉē, nīṭu vāḻka!”
“uṉ neruṅkiya naṭpurimaiyuṭaiya catakarṇar eṉ mūlamāka intac ceytiyai aṉuppiyuḷḷār :”
“teyvac cilaiceyvataṟku uyarnta imayamalaiyiliruntu kalpeṟa vāṉavar vēntaṉ tiruvuḷam nērntāl, atu peṟavum kaṅkaiyil nīrāṭṭavum nāṅkaḷ utavak kāttirukkiṟōm.”
“cēra aracaṉ cañcayaṉukku maṟumoḻi pukaṉṟāṉ.”
“pālakumāraṉ maintarkaḷākiya kaṉakaṉum, vijayaṉum taṅkaḷ nāviṉ kāvalum kāppum iḻantavarkaḷāyt tamiḻaracar valimai yaṟiyāmal avarkaḷaip paḻittuḷḷārkaḷ. ippaṭaikaḷ itaṉāl veñciṉattait tam karuvakam koṇṭu muṉṉēṟi varukiṉṟaṉa. itaic catakarkaṇarukku aṟivikka! kaṅkaip pēriyāṟu kaṭantu eṉpaṭaikaḷ cellumpaṭi oru kappaṟpaṭaiyai avar tiraṭṭi vaikkak kaṭavar!”
“cañcayaṉai aṉuppiviṭṭu, aracaṉ meykkāvalar āyira varaiyum taṉ koluvirukkaiyil vara icaivaḷittāṉ. avarkaḷi ṭamiruntu maṇamikka cantaṇakkaṭṭaikaḷum muttukkaḷumaṭaṅkiya tiṟaiyai ēṟṟāṉ.”
“piṉ taṅkal kalaittu maṉṉaṉ paṭaiyuṭaṉ kaṅkaik karaiyai aṭaintu, karṇarkaḷ tiraṭṭit tantakappaṟpaṭaiyiṉ utaviyāl avvāṟṟaik kaṭantāṉ.”
“vaṭakaraiyil karṇarkaḷē vantu maṉṉaṉai varavēṟṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṭamiruntu viṭaipeṟṟukkoṇṭu cēraṉ piṉṉum vaṭakkēyuḷḷa uttarai nāṭu ceṉṟāṉ.”
“iṅkē kuyilāluvam eṉṟa iṭattil kaṉakaṉ, vicayaṉ ākiya āriya aracarum, avarkaḷ nēca maṉṉarāṉa uttaraṉ, vicittiraṉ, uruttiraṉ, pairavaṉ, cittiraciṅkaṉ, taṉuttaraṉ, cuvētaṉ ākiyavarkaḷum naṭattiya paṭaiyaic cantittāṉ.”
“āriya aracariṉ māperum paṭaiyaik kaṇṭa cēra aracaṉ kaḻipēruvakai eytiṉāṉ. muracu muḻaṅka, caṅkam iyampa, kāḷaṅkaḷ ārpparikka avaṉ paṭai naṭattit tākkiṉāṉ. vil vīrarkaḷum vāḷ vīrarkaḷum nīṭittu mummuramāṉa pōril īṭupaṭṭārkaḷ. paṭukaḷam accantarum kurutikkaḷamāyiṟṟu.”
“nilamuḻuvatum iṟantupaṭṭa vīrar uṭalkaḷum, kutirai yāṉai ākiyavaṟṟiṉ uṭalkaḷum nirampikkiṭantaṉa. pōr muṭivil tamiḻar etirikaḷaṉaivaraiyum turattiyaṭittaṉar.”
“kaṉakaṉum, vicayaṉum maṟṟum pala aracarum ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉiṭam ciṟaippaṭṭārkaḷ.”
“kaṉakaṉ vicayaṉ ākiyōr maṉṉurimai uṭaikaḷaic cēraṉ āṇṭi uṭaiyāka māṟṟumpaṭi ceytāṉ. piṉ avarkaḷait taṉ amaiccaṉ villavaṉ kōtaiyuṭaṉum kāvaṟpaṭaiyuṭaṉum imayattukku aṉuppiṉāṉ.”
pōr muṭivuṟṟapiṉ maṉṉaṉ kaṅkaiyiṉ teṉkaraiya ṭaintāṉ. aṅkē ōr aḻakiya pūṅkāviṉarukil karṇarkaḷ aracaṉukkāka oru ciṟanta araṇmaṉaiyaiyum, uyar paṇit talaivarkaḷum, paṭait talaivarkaḷum, taṅkuvataṟkāṉa pala māṭa māḷikaikaḷaiyum, kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷaiyum, kaṭṭiyiruntārkaḷ. iv viṭattil vantu maṉṉaṉ pōril vīrac ceyalāṟṟiyavarkaḷukkup paricukaḷum paṭṭaṅkaḷum, pukaḻuṭaṉ māṇṭa vīrarkaḷiṉ piḷḷai kaḷukku naṉmatippuc ciṉṉaṅkaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ.
muppattiraṇṭu mātaṅkaḷ veḷināṭṭil kaḻittataṉpiṉ, maṉṉaṉ vañcikku mīṇṭāṉ. araciyum kuṭikaḷum kōlā kalattuṭaṉ avaṉai varavēṟṟaṉar.
kaṅkaik karaiyiliruntē cēraṉ āriya aracar kaṉaka vicayar kaḷait taṉ kāvaṟpaṭaiyuṭaṉ pāṇṭiya cōḻa aracarkaḷukkuk kaṇ kāṭkiyāka aṉuppiyiruntāṉ. kāvaṟpaṭait talaivaṉ nīlaṉ kāvirippaṭṭiṉattukkum maturaikkum ceṉṟapiṉ vañcikkut tirumpiṉāṉ.
“irakkattukkuriya āriya aracarkaḷ naṭattappaṭṭa vakai mikak koṭumai vāyntateṉṟu cōḻa pāṇṭiya aracar kaṇṭippatāka nīlaṉ maṉṉaṉiṭam kūṟiṉāṉ. tamiḻaracariṉ ivvurai kēṭṭu maṉṉaṉ maṉam kumuṟiṉāṉ.”
“appōtu aracaṉ muṉṉilaiyil irunta pārppaṉaṉ māṭalaṉ maṉṉaṉiṭam ivvāṟu pēciṉāṉ.”
“maṉṉavar maṉṉa! uṉ veṟṟi nīṭuka!”
“kāṭṭāṉaikaḷ miḷakuc ceṭiyiṉ niḻalil uṟaṅkum viyalūrai nī veṉṟirukkiṟāy. nēri vāyiliṉ kaṭum pōril nī oṉpatu cōḻarai muṟiyaṭittirukkiṟāy. iṭumpilil nī peṟṟa veṟṟi peritu. ippōtu mākaṭal kaṭantuceṉṟu nī kaṅkaik karaiyil uṉṉai etirtta āriya aracarai muṟiyaṭittirukkiṟāy.”
“veṟṟip paṭaiyaiyuṭaiya vēntaṉē! aṟivārnta maṉṉaṉē! iṉi nī ciṉantaṇivāyāka. porunaiyāṟṟiṉ maṇaliṉum pala āṇṭu nī vāḻvāyāka.”
“eṉ coṟkaḷai ēḷaṉam ceyyātu ciṟitu cevicāyattuk kēṭpāyāka.”
“ippōtu nī aimpatu āṇṭuṭaiyāy. āyiṉum ituvarai nī uṉ vāḻnāḷ muḻutum pōrilēyē kaḻittiruk kiṟāy. ettakaiya tiruvēḷviyum nī ceytatillai.”
“uṉ paḻamai vāynta vīra marapil iṟavātu piḻaittōr yāvarum illai. kaṭal naṭuvil kaṭampai eṟintavaṉumcari, imayamalaimītu viṟpoṟi patittavaṉum cari; taṉṉaip pukaḻntu pāṭiya pārppaṉaṉ vēḷvikaḷaic ceyvatiṉ mūlam tuṟakka maṭaiyumpaṭi ceytavaṉum cari; kūṟṟuvaṉaik kaṭantavaṉum cari, yavaṉar nāṭāṇṭu taṅkam viḷaivikkum malaikaḷai aṭainta vaṉum cari; vīrappaṭai celutti akappā aḻittavaṉum cari, ayiraiyiṉ nīrilum iru kaṭalilum kuḷittōṉumcari; talainakar vañciyil nāṟpūtaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭu niṟuvi vēḷvikaḷai naṭattiṉa vaṉum cari-ellārumē māṇṭu maṭintupōyuḷḷaṉar!”
“’celvamum āṭciyum nillā’ eṉpataṉai nī aṟivāy. tamiḻarkaḷaip paḻitta āriya aracar tōlviyum ikaḻum aṭaintatu nīyē kaṇṭa kāṭci. iḷamaiyum nillātu eṉṟu nāṉ kūṟa vēṇṭiyatillai. uṉ mayir naraikkat toṭaṅkiyuḷḷatē ataṉaik kāṭṭum.”
“ivvāṟu toṭarntu, pārppaṉariṉ piṟappiṟappuk koḷkaiyai valiyuṟutti viḷakki, irācacūyam eṉṉum māpeṟum vēḷviyaic ceyyumpaṭiyum ataṉmūlam varupiṟappukkaḷil taṉ uyirnilaiyiṉ nalattaip pēṇum paṭiyum māṭalaṉ aracaṉai vēṇṭiṉāṉ.”
“maṉṉaṉ itaṉpaṭiyē naṭantukoṇṭāṉ. mikapperiya aḷavil irācacūyam naṭantuvataṟkuriya ēṟpāṭukaḷ toṭaṅkum paṭiyum, attuṭaṉ kaṇṇakikkuk kōyil eṭukkumpaṭiyum uṭaṉē kaṭṭaṉaikaḷ piṟappikkappaṭṭaṉa.”
“iv viḻākkaḷil koṅka maṉṉarum, māḷava maṉṉarum ilaṅkaiyaracaṉ kayavākuvum kalantukoṇṭaṉar.”
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉakkup piṉ yāṉaikkaṇ-atāvatu yāṉaiyiṉ kaṇpōṉṟa kaṇkaḷai uṭaiyavaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭap peyaruṭaiya cēy aracurimai ēṟṟāṉ. avaṉ vīramikka aracaṉ. ayalaracarkaḷukku ōyāttollai koṭuttukkoṇṭu avaṉ taṉ āṭciyiṉ ellaippuṟaṅkaḷilēyē cuṟṟik koṇṭirun tāṉ. ippaṭai naṭavaṭikkaiyiṉ payaṉākavē pāṇṭiya aracaṉ iraṇṭām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ cēranāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭai yeṭuttup pōrttiṟac cūḻccikaḷāl avaṉaic ciṟaip piṭippatil veṟṟi yaṭaintāṉ eṉṟu terikiṟatu.13 āyiṉum cēraṉ tappic ceṉṟu mīṇṭum taṉ āṭciyai valuppaṭuttik koṇṭāṉeṉṟu tōṟṟukiṟatu.
cōḻa aracaṉ irācacūyamvēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷiyum yāṉaikkaṭcēyuṭaṉ aṭikkaṭi pōriṭṭatāka aṟikiṟōm. malāṭṭukkōmāṉ tērvaṇmalaiyaṉ ippōril cōḻaṉukkut tuṇai tantatākat tōṟṟukiṟatu.14
pakaivarkaḷiṭamiruntu viḷaṅkil eṉṟa nakaraik kāttatāka ivvaracaṉaippaṟṟik kuṟuṅkōḻiyūrkiḻār eṉṟa pulavar pukaḻntu pāṭiyuḷḷār. avar pāṭalakaḷil oṉṟu varumāṟuṣa:-
“uyarnta kollimalaikku iṟaivaṉē! vīramikka yāṉaik kaṭcēey! uṉ paṭai vīṭukaḷ kāval etuvum tēvaiyillātapaṭi avvaḷavu akalap parappuṭaiyaṉavā yuḷḷaṉa. aṅkē kuṭittu viruntāṭṭayarum uṉ vīrarkaḷiṉ āravāram karaimītu mōtum kaṭaliṉ muḻakkam pōṉṟatu.”
“kuṭiyaracar celuttiya tiṟaikoṇṭu nī uṉṉaic cārntavar kaḷai ūṭṭukiṟāy. uṉ varaiyaṟṟa celvam eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavuka.”
 “uṉṉaip pāṭum pulavarkaḷ piṉ vēṟu yāraiyum pāṭa vēṇṭuvatillai. avarkaḷ eṉṟum vaṟumaiyuṟāta aḷavu nī kaivaṇmaiyuṭaiyāy.”
“uṉṉāl āḷappaṭṭa nāṭu nilavulakil oru poṉṉāṭu pōṉṟatu eṉṟu kēḷviyuṟṟu nāṉ iṅkē vantēṉ. eṉ kaṇkaḷ kaṇṭu makiḻcciyaṭaikiṉṟaṉa.”
“eṉṟum ceyalūkkamuṭaiya nī uṉ paṭaikaḷai eppōtum veḷināṭukaḷil celutti iṟavāp pukaḻaiyum veṉṟuviṭa eṇṇūkiṟāy!”
ivvaracaṉ māḷviṉpōtu uṭaṉirunta pulavar kūṭalkiḻār an nikaḻccikku ēḻu nāṭkaḷukku muṉpē, an nikaḻcciyai ōr oḷimikka erimīṉ vīḻcci kuṟittuk kāṭṭiṟṟu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟār. it taṟuvāyil avar pāṭiya pāṭṭu taṉicciṟappuṭaṉ nammāl kavaṉikkattakkatu. ēṉeṉil pārppaṉariṉ vēṟākat tamiḻarukku vāṉūlārāycci uṇṭu eṉpataiyum, nāḷmīṉkaḷukkum16 vāṉaka maṉaikaḷukkum17 taṉittamiḻp peyarkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpataiyum atu teḷivupaṭuttukiṟatu.18
“kuṭṭanāḷaṉṟu (kārttikai nāḷ), katiravaṉ āṭṭiṉ maṉaiyil (mēṭattil) irunta camayam naḷḷiravil muṭappaṉai (aṉurātā) mutal viṇmīṉiliruntu kuḷam (puṉarvacu) iṟuti viṇmīṉvarai vāṉattil kāṭciyaḷikka paṅkuṉi mātam mutaṟpātiyil uccattilirunta nāḷ mīṉ uccattiliruntu cāyntu vara, eṭṭāvatu nāḷ mīṉ eḻuntataṉ piṉṉum eṭṭavātu nāḷ mīṉ aṭaivataṟku muṉṉum vāṉamuḻuvatum oḷivīciya ōr erimīṉ kāṟṟiṉ etirtticaiyil aṉaṟpoṟi citaṟiyavaṇṇam vaṭakiḻakku nōkki viḻuntatu. itaik kaṇṭa nāṉum maṟṟap pāṇarkaḷum aruvikaḷ niṟainta vaḷamikka nāṭṭiṉ vēntaṉ māḷviliruntu kāppāṟṟappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu uḷamāra vēṇṭikkoṇṭavarkaḷāy, ivvaccantarun tīkkuṟiyiṉ payaṉait tuyaramikka uḷḷattu ṭaṉ etirnōkkiyiruntōm.”
“”ēḻām nāḷ kālai muṭivu nāḷ vantu nērntatu. etirikaḷaic ciṟaippaṭuttuvatil vallavaṉum īkaiyil vaṇmaiyuṭaiyava ṉumāṉa aracaṉ ippōtu vāṉavar ulakam ceṉṟuviṭṭāṉ. avaṉ pāriya yāṉai tumpikkaiyai nilattil nīṭṭik kiḷarcciyaṟṟuk kiṭakkiṉṟatu. avaṉ periya muracaṅkaḷ kavaṉippāraṟṟu nilattiṉmītu puraḷkiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ veṇkoṟṟak kuṭai piṭiyiṉṟivaṟitē kiṭakkiṟatu. avaṉuṭaiya virainaṭaik kutiraikaḷ vāṭṭattuṭaṉ koṭṭilil niṟkiṉṟaṉa. antō, vāṉavar makaḷir tuṇaiyai nāṭiyō em aracaṉ taṉ aṉpukkuriya araciyarai maṟantuviṭṭāṉ!”
yāṉaikkaṭcēykkup piṉ peruñcēralirumpoṟai aracaṉāṉāṉ. mōcikīraṉār eṉṉum pulavar avaṉ aṉpukku riyavar eṉṟu terikiṟatu. orunāḷ araṇmaṉaiyiṉ orupuṟattē mettaikaḷiṭṭup putumalarpparappiya paṭukkai oṉṟu kiṭakkak kaṇṭu, kaviñar atil ciṟitu cāyntu, cāyntavaṇṇam uṟaṅki viṭṭār. avvaḻicella nērnta aracaṉ kaviñar cōrvuṟṟu viyarttukkiṭappatu kaṇṭu, aṅkirunta oru viciṟi eṭuttu avarukku viciṟiṉāṉ. pulavar eḻuntu aracaṉ taṉakku viciṟuvatu kaṇṭu tiṭukkiṭṭār.
paṭukkai uṇmaiyil aracaṉ pōrmuracattukkuriya tiruvirukkai eṉṟu itaṉ piṉṉarē uṇarnta pulavar kīḻvarum pāṭalaip pāṭiṉār.19
“naṟu neyyiṉ nuraipōla melliya malarkaḷ parappappaṭṭa intap paṭukkaiyaik kaṇṭu, atu nīṇṭa mayiliṟakukaḷāl uḻiñaiyiṉ poṉ malarkaḷ piṇaitta maṇimālaikaḷālum aṇivikkappaṭṭuk karuṅkaṇkaḷuṭaṉ aḻakuruvamainta muracattuk kuriyateṉṟu aṟiyāmal atil paṭuttuk kaṇṇayarntuviṭṭēṉ. inta eṉatu tuṭukkut taṉattukkāka nī eṉṉai iru tuṇṭukaḷāka veṭṭiyirukka vēṇṭum. āṉāl nīyō uṉ vāḷai uṟaiyiliruntu uruviṉāy illai. ituvē peritu.”
“tamiḻ nilameṅkum uṉ pukaḻ parappa itu pōtāteṉṟu nī uṉ valimai mikka kaikaḷāl tuyilum eṉ neṟṟi kuḷira viciṟiṉāy.”
“naṟceyalkaḷiṉ pukaḻāl ivvulakai niṟaittavarkaḷukkē poṉṉulakiṉ anta iṉpavāyil tiṟakkappaṭum eṉṟa uṇmaiyai aṟintataṉāl pōlum aruntiṟal vēntaṉākiya nī ivvāṟu ceytāy? eṉṟār.”
avaṉ kuṭimaṉṉaruḷ oruvaṉāṉa eḻiṉi avaṉuk ketirākak kiḷarnteḻuntataṉāl, avaṉ oru perum paṭai tiraṭṭik koṇṭu ceṉṟu avaṉatu takaṭūrkkōṭṭaiyai muṟṟukaiyiṭṭāṉ. eḻiṉi, atikamāṉ eṉṟa peyaruṭaiya mikap paḻamaiyāṉa vēḷir kuṭittalaivaṉ. avarkaḷ talainakaramākiya takaṭūr cēlamāvaṭ ṭattiluḷḷa taṟkālat tarumapuri eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. eḻiṉi akkōṭṭaiyaic ceppam ceytu valimaip paṭuttit taṉiyāṭciyurimai peṟuvatu allatu ammuyaṟciyil maṭivatu eṉṟu uṟutikoṇṭāṉ. āṉāl cēra maṉṉaṉ nakara muḻuvatum vaḷaittu veḷittoṭarpiliruntu atait tuṇṭupaṭuttiṉāṉ.
nakaraic cuṟṟiyirunta muṭkāṭu cēra vīrarāl aḻikkappaṭṭatu. akaḻi tūrkkappaṭṭatu. kōṭṭai vāyilkaḷ yāṉaikaḷāl piḷakkappaṭṭaṉa. cērarpaṭai kōṭṭaikkuḷ pukuntu pāyntatu. uḷḷē naṭanta caṇṭaiyil eḻiṉiyum avaṉ paṭaittalaivar kaḷum vīra aruñceyalkaḷ pala ceytu, etiriyiṉ peruntokai kāraṇamākavē iṟutivarai pōriṭṭu ovvoruvarāka maṭittārkaḷ.
muṟṟukaiyiṉpōtu aricilkiḻār, poṉmuṭiyār eṉṟa pulavarkaḷ cēra aracaṉ paṭaivīṭṭiliruntārkaḷ. iṉṟuvarai namakku eṭṭiyuḷḷa avarkaḷ pāṭal pakutikaḷ kōṭṭait tākkutaliṉ cila nikaḻccikaḷai namakku uyirccittiraṅkaḷākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cērar paṭaikaḷ takaṭūrai aṇūkiyapōtu, eḻiṉiyum avaṉ paṭaittalaivaṉ perumpākkaṉum kōṭṭaikku veḷiyē avarkaḷaic cantikkat taṅkaḷ paṭaikaḷai veḷiyē koṇṭu vantaṉar. perumpākkaṉ tākkutalai etirnōkkit taṉ paṭaiyiṉ muṉpu niṉṟāṉ. pulavar aricilkiḻārum poṉmuṭiyārum cēra aracaṉukku avaṉaic cuṭṭi kāṭṭi, avaṉ avarkaḷai nōkki ēḷaṉac cirippuc cirippatai eṭutturaittārkaḷ.20
“mālaikaḷaiyaṇinta anta vīra iḷaiñaṉ ūkkamikunta kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭiya miṉṉoḷi pāyum aḻakiyatēril ēṟik koṇṭu, nam paṭaiyai nōkkit taṉ valimaiyil makiḻnta vaṉāy niṟkiṟāṉ. avaṉ veṟi koṇṭu nammait tākkiṉāl, vīṟumikka matayāṉaikaḷaik kūṭa avaṉāl vīḻttamuṭiyakkūṭum. tākkutalai etirnōkkiyavaṇṇam miṉṉiṭum taṉvēlai vīciyapaṭi avaṉ taṉ etirikaḷaik kaṇṭu nakaittu niṟkiṟāṉ.”
“karuntāṭiyuṭaiya anta añcā neñcukoṇṭa vīra iḷaiñaṉ taṉ pōrkkutiraikaḷiṉ kaṭivāḷaṅkaḷaip paṟṟiya vaṇṇam oru taṭavai pūrikkum taṉ tōḷkaḷaip pārkkiṟāḷ. piṉ taṉ piṉṉē aṇivakattu niṟkum yāṉaik kūṭṭaṅkaḷaik kaṇṇālaḷakkiṟāṉ. yāṉaikaḷukkupiṉṉē niṟkum tērvaricai kaḷai nōkkukiṟāṉ. taṉ pōrkkutiraikaḷai nōkkukiṟāṉ. piṉ paṟantuvarum ampukaḷaiyum taṉ kaiyiluḷḷa vēlaiyum kaiyaiyum pārttu nammai nōkkic cirikkiṟāṉ.”
mutal nāḷ pōril eḻiṉiyiṉ paṭaikaḷ kōṭṭaikkuḷ turattappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭām nāḷil kōṭṭaiyaic cuṟṟiyuḷḷa muṭkāṭu aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. ‘muṟṟukaiyiṉ vēkampaṟṟi eṉṉa karutukiṟīrkaḷ’ eṉṟu cēra aracaṉ ac camayam pulavarkaḷiṭam karuttucāva, avarkaḷ nilaiyaip pāṭalkaḷil tīṭṭiṉar.21
“nēṟṟu eḻiṉiyiṉ kutiraikaḷ māṉkaḷpōl tuḷḷi yōṭiṉa. yāṉaikaḷ malaikaḷpōl cōrvuṟṟuc cāyntaṉa. cīṟivarum ampumārikaḷāl maṟa vīrarkaḷ kalakalattuk kalaintaṉar. pōril putuppuṇpaṭṭu acciṉṉaṅkaḷai uṭalil aṇintuḷḷa aracaṉē! iṉṟu kollappaṭṭavarkaḷiṉ ampēṟuṇṭa talaikaḷ nam yāṉaikaḷāl eṟṟuṇṭu, pātaikaḷiṉ arukellām piṟanta paṉampaḻaṅkaḷ pōlak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. cōrvaṟiyāta nam karuṅkaṇ iḷaiñarkaḷ kāṭṭaiyaḻittu āravārittavaṇṇam etirikaḷaik kaṇṭa kaṇṭa iṭaṅkaḷilellām avarkaḷaik koṉṟaḻikkiṉṟaṉar.”
“nāṉai muracu muḻaṅka nām kōṭṭaikkuḷ celvōm.”
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	puṟam, 65.

3.	cilappatikāram  ōōaiō.

6.	“mūlattiṉ col nayattai moḻipeyarppil kāṭṭuvataritāṉālum ataṉ poruḷ nayam iṅkē tarappaṭukiṟatu” eṉṟa vācakattuṭaṉ ivviṭattil puṟam 8ām pāṭaliṉ āṅkila moḻi peyarppu mūlanūlil tarappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻākkattil tamiḻ mūlamē nūlil tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. moḻipeyarppu varumāṟu.
	“kṣacaipāva ṭicab vāyava ayacauhnaḷava yīcaṭiraṉaṭala in vāna ḷamala!
	kṣanāiṉē vāna hṭaiṭaḷa vāṭira hṉainaḷava kacaṭiaṭiraca ḷaipāva,
	haṉē vāṭira yacava ḷananan le ṉayala yaṉē nē’naca le nipāva
	kūhṭirapā ḷaṭiyacaipē in vāna ḷamala ḷaṭi ṟaṉaina yaṉē hipā,
	haṭaṭinē vāṭira ṉayacanaḷava nṭiva vaṭi ḷāṭiṟa vāla kayauna,
	kṣarava uṭianaḷava parayacaṉanaṉa le ya vāṭiraḷayaṉē cayalaḷa!
	ḻaṭiṟa uyaḷēva vāṭira enai ṟaivā ūhnacayaṭa havāyan bṭiṭaṉa;
	ṟuhṭi ūṭiravēṭanaḷaḷa uhnaikaḷa in kanayaṭavala ṉaṭivā hṭiṭaṉa,
	ṟuhṭi manṭiṟaḷa nṭi kanayaca ṭin vāna yevavaṭana kanaiṭaṉa,
	haṉē uṭiravēḷa nṭi uṭiḷava vāna ṟanayama yaṉē yīṭiṭica vaṭi ḷānaiṭaṉa,
	ṟuhṭi ḷauṭicaḷē ṉaieṉainaṉa naayīcaina kaṭica hḷai ḷaṟayala,
	haṉē ṭanayaṉaḷa vāna ṭanapaiṭiḷē vāyava ḷaavaina yaṉē ḷaṭayala?”
