8. vēḷirum kuṭimaṉṉarum
pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka muṭiyuṭaiya vēntarkaḷāyt tamiḻakattil cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar eṉṟa aracarkaḷ maṭṭumē iruntuvantaṉar. itaṉ payaṉākavē avarkaḷ oru tokaippaṭa mūvēntarkaḷ eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar. āyiṉum tamiḻakattilē mikap paranta pakutikaḷai āṇṭa palvēṟu vēḷirum kuṭimaṉṉarum iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ perumpālum mūvaracaruḷ yārēṉum oruvarukkuk kuṭiyurimaippaṭṭavarākavē viḷaṅkiṉar. avvappōtu avarkaḷil cilar taṅkaḷ mēlāṭciyait tūkki eṟintuviṭṭut taṉiyurimaiyil maṉṉarāka muyaṉṟatuṇṭu. āṉāl avarkaḷ mika viraivil kīḻaṭakkappaṭṭārkaḷ.
kuṭi maṉṉaruḷ mutalāvatākak kūṟak kūṭiyavaṉ tiraiyaṉ. avaṉ tiraiyar atāvatu kaṭalaracar kuṭicārntavaṉ. im marapaiyē cōḻarum taṅkaḷ kuṭimarapākak koṇṭiruntaṉar. pukaḻ peṟṟa karikālacōḻaṉ ciṟuvaṉāyirunta kālattil kāñcit tiraiyaṉ cōḻa aracaiyē kaippaṟṟi ataṉmītu nīṇṭanāḷ āṭci ceytāṉ. tiraiyaṉaip pukaḻntu pāṭappeṟṟa perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭaiyiṉ mūlam ivvaracaṉ palvēl tiraiyaṉ eṉṟa paṭṭattaiyum toṇṭaimāṉ eṉṟa paṭṭattaiyum tāṅkiyiruntāṉeṉṟum perumpukaḻ vīraṉeṉṟum aṟikiṟōm.1
tiraiyaṉ uṭaṉaṭiyākat taṉ āṭcip pīṭattaic cōḻar kaḷiṉ paṇṭaittalainakarāṉa uṟaiyūrukku māṟṟik koṇṭi runtāl, avaṉ cōḻa aracurimaiyil toṭarntu nīṭittut taṉ marapiṉarukkē atai viṭṭucceṉṟirukka muṭiyum. āṉāl avaṉ kāñciyilēyē taṅkiyiruntāṉ. ākavē karikālaṉ taṉ muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ aracirukkaiyaip peṟuvatil taṭṅkal mikuti illātu pōyiṟṟu.
cōḻarukku uṭpaṭṭa piṟa kuṭimaṉṉar vēṅkaṭam, malāṭu, miḻalaikkūṟṟam ākiyavaṟṟai āṇṭaṉar. vēṅkaṭat talaivarkaḷuḷ pulli eṉpavaṉ māmūlaṉār,2 kallil āttiraiyaṉār eṉṟa pulavarkaḷuṭaṉ camakālattavaṉ.3
ivvēḷir eppōtum tamiḻakattiṉ vaṭaellaiyaṭut tirunta vaṭukaruṭaṉ ōyātu pōrilīṭupaṭṭiruntaṉar.
malāṭṭiṉ kōmāṉkaḷiṉ marapup peyar malaiyamāṉ eṉpatu malaiyamāṉ āṭcip pakutiyil mukkiya nakaram peṇṇai yāṟṟiṉ karaiyil taṟpōtu tirukkōyilūr eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭum kōval ākum4. muḷḷūrmalai eṉṟa akalapparappuṭaiya ceḻippāṉa mēṭṭu nilappakuti avaṉ āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭiruntatu. iṅkē kuṭiyēṟiyirunta pala pārppaṉarkaḷukku malaiyamāṉ kāri nilaṅkaḷai māṉiyamākak koṭuttāṉ. avarkaḷil perun tokaiyiṉar appakkam vantu tiraṇṭu vāḻnta kāraṇattāl, tamiḻk kaviñar ataṉai ‘āriyar nirampiya pukaḻ vāynta muḷḷūr’ eṉṟu kuṟittaṉar5.  pārppaṉap pāṭakarkaḷukku vaḷḷaṉmaiyuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkiya kāriyaip pārppaṉak kaviñar kapilar kīḻvarumāṟu pukaḻntu pāṭiyuḷḷār.
“vīrakkaḻalaṇinta kāri! uṉ nāṭu muttī pēṇum pārp paṉarukkuriyatu. ataik kaṭal koḷḷa muṭiyātu. etiri kaippaṟṟa muṭiyātu. mūvaracaruḷ oruvar kuṭi eṉṟavakaiyil avarkaḷ uṉakkut tarum paricukaḷaiyellām nī uṉ kuṭipukaḻum pāṇarka ḷukkuk koṭuttuviṭukiṟāy. vaṭamīṉ pōṉṟa kaṟpuṟutiyuṭaiya uṉ tuṇaivi tavira, vēṟu uṉ uṭaimai eṉa nī etuvum vaittukkoḷḷavillai. āyiṉum nī kuṟaivaṟṟa iṉpamuṭai yavaṉāy irukkiṟāy.”6
iṉṉoru kaviñar kīḻvarum pāṭalkaḷāl avar ātaravu kōriṉār : 
“etirikaḷiṉ yāṉaikaḷuṭaiya neṟṟikaḷai aṇi ceyta poṟpaṭāṅkaḷiṉ takaṭukaḷaip paṟittu avaṟṟāl poṉṉālāṉa tāmaraippūkkaḷ puṉaintu pāṇarkaḷiṉ talaiyil cūṭṭiyavaṉ vīramarapil vantavaṉē! aṭarnta kāṭukaḷiṉūṭāka aruvikaḷ cevikaḷait tuḷaikkum āravārattuṭaṉ viḻum muḷḷūr malaiyiṉ vīṟuṭaik kōmāṉē!”
“uṉṉaiyum uṉ kuṭimarapaiyum pāṭi uṉ peyarai iṟavāp peyarākkiyuḷḷa aṟivārnta pārppaṉaṉaiviṭa (kapilaṉaiviṭa) aṟiviṟ ciṟantavaṉ ulakil illai. avaṉ pāṭṭiṉ ciṟappukkuppiṉ maṟṟappāṇar uṉṉaip pāṭuvataṟku vaḻiyaṟṟavarāyviṭṭārkaḷ.”  
“vāṉavaṉ (cēraṉ) karaikkut taṅkam cumantuvarum kappalkaḷ ulavum mēlaik kaṭalilē vēṟu enta marakkalamum cella muṭiyātu atupōla (kapilar pāṭiya) uṉṉaip pāṭavakai yaṟiyātu tikaikkiṉṟōm.” 
“yāṉaikaḷuṭaṉum, iṭiyeṉa muḻaṅkum muracaṅkaḷuṭaṉum pōrceyyavanta māṟṟaracarkaḷaic citaṟaṭitta peṇṇait tīrattiṉ kāvala! vaṟumaiyāl untappaṭṭu, uṉ pukaḻāl īrkkap paṭṭu uṉ aruḷnāṭi varukiṉṟōm.”7
kāri kollimalait talaivaṉ ōriyuṭaṉ pōruṭaṟṟiṉāṉ. avaṉaip pōril koṉṟu, kollimalaiyai ataṉ nēriya urimaiyāḷaṉāṉa cēraṉukkē tantāṉ8. maṟṟa vēḷirkaḷai veṉṟa veṟṟikaḷāl ekkaḷippuṟṟu avaṉ taṉiyurimaiyuṭaṉ aracāḷa eṇṇi muṭiyaṇintukoṇṭatākat terikiṟatu. itaṉālēyē avaṉ ‘tirumuṭikkāri’ atāvatu muṭiyēṟṟa kāri eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭāṉ.
muṭiyēṟṟa cila nāṭkaḷukkuḷ avaṉ talaikkaṉattai aṭakka eṇṇiya cōḻa aracaṉ oru perumpaṭaiyuṭaṉ malāṭṭiṉmītu paṭaiyeṭuttu veṅkurutip pōroṉṟil kāriyai muṟiyaṭittukkoṉṟāṉ.
cōḻaṉ kāriyiṉ putalvarkaḷaik koṉṟu malaiyamāṉ kuṭimarapai aḻittuviṭa eṇṇiyiruntāṉ. āṉāl kōvūrkiḻār eṉṟa pulavariṉ iṭaiyīṭṭiṉāl avarkaḷ uyirkaḷ kāppāṟṟappaṭṭaṉa.9
iṅṅaṉam māḷviliruntu kāppāṟṟappeṟṟa kāriyiṉ putalvaṉ kaṇṇaṉ taṉ vāḻval cōḻa aracaṉukkuc ceyta kaimmāṟu ciṟitaṉṟu ēṉeṉil, cila āṇṭukaḷukkuḷḷēyē cōḻaṉ etirikaḷāl mikavum nerukkuṇṭu talainakarai viṭṭōṭi aṇūkamuṭiyā uyaramuṭaiya muḷḷūr malaiyil, taṉ kuṭimaṉṉaṉ kaṇṇaṉ āṭcip pakutiyilēyē aṭaikkalam pukavēṇṭi vantatu.
tantaiyaippōlavē vīrañceṟinta kaṇṇaṉ maṉṉar ātaravāḷariṉ uḷaccōrvakaṟṟik kalainta paṭaikaḷaiyum tiraṭṭiṉāṉ. etirikaḷai mīṭṭum etirttu muṭiyaṭittuc cōḻaṉ valimaiyai maṟupaṭiyum nilaināṭṭiṉāṉ. innaṉṟi maṟavātu cōḻamaṉṉaṉ kaṇṇaṉaiyē taṉ amaiccaṉākki avaṉukkuc cōḻiya ēṉāti eṉṟa uyarpaṭṭam vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ10.
miḻalaik kūṟṟattaiccērnta pukaḻcāṉṟa talaivaṉ māvēḷ evvi. avaṉ vēḷāḷar marapiṉaṉ. avaṉ kuṭimarapu āyirattu eṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa annāḷilkūṭap paḻamai cāṉṟatu eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu11. avaṉ āṭcip pakuti kāviri yāṟṟiṉ teṉpakutiyiliruntatu. atu nellum karumpum virivākap payirākiya vaḷamikka kaḻaṉikaḷ nirampiya nāṭu. 
avaṉ vaḷḷaṉmaiyiṉ payaṉil paṅkupeṟṟa oru pulavar avaṉaippaṟṟi ivvāṟu kūṟukiṟār.
“avaṉ māḷikai aṭaiyāta vāyil uṭaiyatu. viruntiṉar palarōṭaṉṟi avaṉ eṉṟum uṇavuṇṇa amarntatillai.”12
avaṉ nāṭṭai ālaṅkāṉam veṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ paṭaittalaivarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉāṉa atukai paṭaiyeṭuttāṉ. evvi taṉpaṭaikaḷiṉ talaimaiyil vīramākap pōrceytu, pōril peṟṟa puṇkaḷāl māṇṭāṉ.13
evviyiṉ uṟaviṉaṉāṉa vēḷpāri paṟampu eṉṟa oru ciṟu nilappakutiyiṉ talaivaṉ. āṉāl pulavarkaḷukku ātaravaḷitta māperum puravalaṉ eṉṟa muṟaiyil tamiḻ moḻiyuḷḷaḷavum avaṉ peyar tamiḻar niṉaivil maṅkāmal nilaipeṟattakkatu. evviyiṉ vīḻccikkuppiṉ pāri uyarnta paṟampu malaiyaiyum ataṉaic cūḻnta nilaṅkaḷaiyum kaikkoṇṭu mūvaracarkaḷil evaraiyum mēlāḷāka ēṟkāmal taṉiyāṭciyāḷaṉāka naṭakkat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ.
avaṉ taṅku taṭaiyaṟṟu aruñceyal vēṭṭattil muṉainta vīramaṟavaṉ. atē camayam kavalaiyaṟṟa iṉpa vāḻviṉaṉ. tūya uḷḷamum vaḷḷaṉmaiyumuṭaiyavaṉ. kavitaiyil avaṉ ārvam peritāyiruntatu. nāṭōṭiyāka vanta ovvoru pāṇaṉum avaṉ araṇmaṉaiyil aṉpuṭaṉ ātavu peṟṟu viruntaḷikkap peṟṟāṉ.
añcā neñcam, vaḷḷaṇmai, enta iṭarilum kalaṅkātu makiḻntu niṟkum vīṟuvakai-kaviñarkaḷ tam pāṭṭuṭait talaivaṉukku iruppatākap pukaḻntu pāṭum paṇpukaḷ oṉṟu kuṟaiyāmal avaṉiṭam iṇaintiruppatai avarkaḷ kaṇṭārkaḷ. pāriyiṉ ippaṇpukaḷaik kavitai moḻikaḷil pukaḻntum avaṉ koṭaittiṟattai viyantu pārāṭṭiyum cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar avaikkaḷaṅkaḷilēyē pulavarkaḷ pāṭiṉar. itu mūvaracar poṟāmaiyaik kiḷaṟiṟṟu. avarkaḷ paṟampu malaiya muṟṟukai yiṭat tam paṭaikaḷai aṉuppiṉārkaḷ.
pāriyiṉ tuṇaiyāṭkaḷukku malaikkaṇavāykaḷiṉ mēṭupaḷḷaṅkaḷ yāvum naṉku terintiruntaṉa. avaṟṟai avarkaḷ tiṟampaṭak kāttuvantārkaḷ. pōril iru tiṟaṅkaḷiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvu peritāyiruntālum cila nāṭkaḷ pāri paṭaikkala vāyppilum tokaiyilum mikavum mēmpaṭṭa etirikaḷait taṉ taṉi vīrattāl kāttu niṉṟāṉ. āṉāl iṟutiyil etirikaḷ paṭippaṭiyāka malaimītu pōriṭṭu muṉṉēṟi, pāriyai muṟiyaṭittuk koṉṟaṉar.14
pāriyiṉ keḻutakai naṇparāṉa kapilar mūvaracar paṭaikaḷum paṟampai muṟṟukaiyiṭṭa camayam kīḻvarum pāṭalkaḷaip pāṭiṉār.
“mūvaracarum tam oṉṟupaṭṭa paṭaikaḷuṭaṉ muṟṟukai yiṭṭālum, paṟampuṭilaiyai velvatu aritu. vaḷamikka paṟampu nāṭṭiṉ ūrkaḷ munnūṟu uṇṭu. munnūṟum ippōtu pulavar kaḷukku uriyaṉa. nāṉum pāriyumtāṉ iṅkē irukkiṟōm. eṅkaḷ paṟampulaiyum itō irukkiṟatu. pāṇarāka eṅkaḷaip pāṭa varuvīrāṉāl, vāruṅkaḷ.”15
“pārikkuriya paṟampai vellavatu eḷiteṉṟā eṇṇū kiṟīrkaḷ?” muḻaṅkum muracuṭaiya mūvaracarum muṟṟukai yiṭṭālum, akkālattilum atu uḻaviṉṟiyē naṉku māvaḷaṅkaḷ tarum. mutalatu mellilaiyuṭaiya mūṅkil tarum arici. iraṇṭāvatu palāmaraṅkaḷ tarum iṉiya kaṉikaḷ. mūṉṟāvatu vaḷḷikkoḻuṅ koṭikaḷ tarum iṉiya kiḻaṅkukaḷ. nāṉkāvatu tēṉ kūṭukaḷiṉ mītu mantikaḷ tāvuvataṉāl kuṉṟukaḷil vaḻintōṭum tēṉ.”
“vāṉaḷāva vaḷarntakaṉṟatu paṟampumalai ucci. atiluḷḷa naṟunīrc cuṉaikaḷō vāṉattu mīṉkaḷpōla mikappala. ovvoru paṇṇaiyilum oru tēraiyum, ovvoru marattarukilum oru yāṉaiyaiyum nīṅkaḷ kāval vaittālum, uṅkaḷ vīrarāl atai muṟṟukaiyiṭamuṭiyātu. avarkaḷ vāḷkaḷ ataṉ vaḻiyākap pātai uṇṭupaṇṇi muṉṉēṟavum iyalātu.”
“malaiyaik kaippaṟṟum vakaiyai nāṉ aṟivēṉ. ciṟiya yāḻiṉ narampukaḷai uḷarntu iṉṉicaiyeḻuppi, uṅkaḷ peṇṭiruṭaṉ āṭippāṭi varuvīrāṉāl, avaṉ uṅkaḷukkut taṉ nilaṅkaḷ yāvaṟṟaiyum malaiyaiyum taruvāṉ.”16
pāriyiṉ vīramikka uṟaviṉarkaḷilē avaṉ pātukāppi lēyē ovvoruvarum vīḻntupaṭṭaṉar. avaṉ muṟaiyaṟṟa maṭivukku varunta avaṉ iru putalviyarkaḷē mīntaṉar. avaṉ celvattaiyē tam celvamākak koṇṭu vāḻnta pāṇar kulam iṟantupaṭṭa tam talaivaṉukkāka vāyviṭṭaraṟṟiṟṟu. tuṇaiyaṟṟut tavitta iru putalviyar kaṇṇīruṭaṉ avarkaḷ kaṇṇīr kalantatu.
pāriyiṉ aṉpukkuriya pāṇar kapilar pāriyiṉ putalviyar kaḷaip pēṇum poṟuppai mēṟkoṇṭār. paṟampaiviṭṭup piriyum camayam avar ammalaiyai nōkkik kīḻvarumāṟu pulampiṉār :-
“māṇpumikka paṟampē! nī orukāl eṅkaḷ aṉpukkuriya poruḷāka iruntatuṇṭu. appōtu matukkuṭaṅkaḷ tiṟantiruntaṉa. āṭukaḷ kollappaṭṭaṉa. nāṅkaḷ virumpumaḷavu uṇṇumpaṭi kaṟiyuṭaṉ camaittacōṟu vaṭṭikkappaṭṭatu. ippōtu pāri iṟantuviṭṭataṉāl, tuṇaiyaṟṟavarāy, tuyaruṟṟavarāy, kaṇkaḷi liruntu nīr aruviyāka oḻuka, nāṅkaḷ uṉṉaiviṭṭu viṭai koṇṭu celkiṟōm. naṟuṅkūntaluṭaṉ vaḷaiyaṇinta kaiyiṉarāṉa pāriyiṉ putalviyarukkēṟṟa kaṇavaraik kāṇūmāṟu celkiṉṟōm.”
ivvāṟu araṟṟiyavaṇṇa kapilar peṇkaḷuṭaṉ tamiḻakattiluḷḷa vēḷirkaḷuḷ viccikkō, iruṅkōvēḷ ākiya iruvaraiyum mutalil aṇūkiṉār. iruvarum avarkaḷai maṇantu koḷḷa maṟuttapiṉ avar avarkaḷaip pārppaṉarkaḷukkē koṭuttār.17
ceyyāṟṟut tīrattil ceṅkaṇmāviṉ kōmāṉāṉa naṉṉaṉ cōḻa araciṉ iṉṉoru peyarpeṟṟa talaivaṉ. peruṅkaucikaṉār eṉṟa pulavar avaṉaip pukaḻntu malaipaṭukaṭām eṉṟa pāṭalaip pāṭiyuḷḷār. ippāṭaliliruntu avaṉ āṭcip pakutiyil naviram eṉṟa malai uccimītu kāriyuṇṭik kaṭavuḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkiya civaperumāṉiṉ kōyil oṉṟu iruntatākat terikiṟatu.18
pāṇṭiya aracar mēlurimai ēṟṟa mukkiyamāṉa vēḷir āy, porunaṉ, paḻayaṉmāṟaṉ ākiyavarkaḷē.
ivarkaḷil māvēḷāy eṉpavaṉ vēḷāḷar marapiṉaṉ. potiyamalaikkum ataṉaic cūḻnta nilaṅkaḷukkum uriyavaṉ. avaṉ āṭcip pakutiyiṉ talainakar āykuṭi19. āykuṭiyai āṇṭavarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉāṉa āy aṇṭiraṉ orukāl koṅkar kaḷai muṟiyaṭittu avarkaḷai mēlaikkaṭalukkut turattiya tākat teriyavarukiṟatu.20 āy aṇṭiraṉiṉ piṉṉurimaiyāḷaril oruvaṉāṉa titiyaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉai etirtta kūṭṭil oruvaṉākac cērttu ālaṅkāṉap pōril muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.21
porunaṉ eṉpatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vēḷiriṉ kuṭippeyar. itu potiyamalaiyiṉ mēṟkuppuṟattiluḷḷatu.22 ippakuti iṉṟum nāñcil nāṭu eṉṟē aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. itu taṟkālat tiruvāṅkūr araciṉ oru pakutiyāy uḷḷatu.
mōkūrt talaivaṉ paḻayaṉ māṟaṉ paṇṭai māṟar kuṭiyiṉ talaivaṉ. avarkaḷ potiyamalai yarukil kuṭiyēṟiya varkaḷ. pāṇṭiya aracar kuṭiyum immūla marapukkuriyatē. pāṇṭiyaṉ mutalām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ āṭciyil paḻayaṉ māṟaṉ pāṇṭiya aracil maṉṉarkkaṭutta uyarmatippuṭai yavaṉā yiruntāṉ.23
cōḻa aracaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ perumpaṭaiyuṭaṉ maturaiyai muṟṟukaiyiṭṭa camayam, paḻayaṉ māṟaṉ virainaṭaik kutiraivīrarum, veñcīṟṟamuṭaiya yāṉaikaḷum aṭaṅkiya oru perumpaṭaiyuṭaṉ avaṉait tākkic cōḻarpaṭaiyai muṟṟilum nilaikulaiyac ceytāṉ.24
cēraṉukku uṭpaṭṭa orutalaivaṉai avamatittataṟkākap paḻayaṉ māṟaṉait taṇṭikka eṇṇi, cēra aracaṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ avaṉ āṭcip pakutiyaip paṭaiyeṭuttut tākkiyatākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.25
aḻumpil, katiraimalai, pāli, takaṭūr ākiyavaṟṟiṉ vēḷir cēra aracaṉukku uṭpaṭṭavarkaḷ.
aḻumpil vēḷ aḻumpil nakariṉ talaivaṉ. annakaram peritum kuṭṭanāṭṭaic cērntatāyirukkavēṇṭum. pāṇṭiyaṉ mutalām neṭuñceḻiyaṉ cēranāṭṭaip paṭaiyeṭuttapōtu, aḻumpilvēḷai etirttuttākki muṟiyaṭittu avaṉ āṭcip pakuti muḻuvataiyum pāṇṭiya aracuṭaṉ cērttukkoṇṭāṉ.26
vallamaimikka avvaracaṉāṭcikkuppiṉ aḻumpilvēḷ annilap pakutiyai mīṭṭukkoṇṭāṉ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ aracavaip perumakkaḷil avaṉ mīṭṭum talaimai yiṭam vakikkakkāṇkiṟōm.27
kutiraimalait talaivaṉāṉa piṭṭaṉkoṟṟaṉ cērarpaṭait talaivaṉāyiruntāṉ.28
vēṇmāṉ pāli vēḷiriṉ kuṭippeyar. pāli eṉpatu vaināṭṭukkum vaṭakiḻakku malapārukkum vaṭakkē taṟpōtu kuṭaku eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭum pakuti muḻuvatum uḷḷaṭakkiya curaṅkat toḻiṟpakutiyiluḷḷa araṇ amaippuṭaiya oru nakaram29. im maṇṭalattiluḷḷa vēṟu iraṇṭu nakaraṅkaḷ arayamum viyalūrum ākum.30
pālivēḷāṉa naṉṉaṉ vēḷmāṉ kolaipurintavaṉ eṉṟa koṭumpeyarukku āḷāṉāṉ. ciṟu kuṟṟam ceyta oru ciṟu peṇṇukku, avaḷ uṟaviṉar avaḷ eṭaiyiṉ oṉpatu maṭaṅku taṅkam maṉṉippīṭākat taruvatākak kūṟiyum kēḷāmal avaṉ avaḷaik kolait taṇṭaṉaikku uṭpaṭuttiṉāṉ.31
im marapukkuriya talaivaṉ oruvaṉ cēra aracaṉ kaḷaṅkāyk kaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēralāl peruntuṟaiyil naṭaipeṟṟa pōril muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭāṉ.32
atiyamāṉ maḻavarkuṭit talaivaṉ, avaṉ iṉṟaiya cēlam māvaṭṭattiluḷḷa tarmapuri eṉṉum takaṭūr āṇṭavaṉ33. avaṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ teṉṉāṭṭil karumpup payiraik koṇṭu vantārkaḷ.34 cēra aracaṉ peruñcēralirum poṟaiyiṉ āṭcik kālattil atiyamāṉ kuṭittalaivaṉ neṭumāṉ añci eṉpavaṉ. avaṉ malāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭaiyeṭuttu malaiyamāṉ talainakarāṉa kōvalūraic cūṟaiyāṭiṉāṉ35. ittaṟuvāyil paraṇar eṉṉum pulavar  avaṉ vīrappukaḻ pāṭiṉār.
pēravāvum vīramum vāynta talaivaṉātalāl, avaṉ taṉ āṭcip pakutiyai virivupaṭutta virumpiṉāṉ. kutiraimalai mēliruntu kaṇṭāl kaṇṇukkut terikiṟa ammalai cūḻnta nāṭu muḻuvatum avaṉukkuttarac cēra aracaṉ muṉvariṉum, avaṉ amaiyātu mēlum kōriṉāṉ. itaṉāl pōr mūṇṭatu. cēra aracaṉ paṭaiyuṭaṉ avaṉai aṭakkap puṟappaṭṭāṉ. takaṭūr muṟṟukaiyiṉpōtu neṭumāṉ añci paṭupuṇpaṭṭu viraivil māṇṭāṉ. cēra aracaṉ peruñcēralirumpoṟai āṭciyai viritturaikkumiṭattil im muṟṟukai paṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.36
atiyamāṉ avaiyil avaṉ pēraṉpukkum, matippukkumuriya pulavarāyirunta avvaiyār avaṉaiyum avaṉ vīraraiyum paṟṟi aṇṭaiyayalilirunta talaivarkaḷ koṇṭirunta accam kuṟittuk kīḻvarum pāṭalil viritturaittuḷḷār :-
“etirikaḷiṉ kōṭṭaikaḷait tākki muṉai maḻuṅkiya tantaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya uṉ yāṉaippaṭaikaḷaik kaṇṭavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kōṭṭai vāyil katavukaḷaip piṇikkum vallirumpuk kampikaḷaip putuppikkiṉṟaṉar. kālaṭiyil tuvaittaḻitta etirikaḷiṉ kuruti tōynta kuḷampukaḷaiyuṭaiya uṉ kutiraip paṭaikaḷaik kaṇṭōr taṅkaḷ kōṭṭaivāyilkaḷukkut tiṇṇiya muḷ marakkaṭṭaikaḷāl vāyil vaḻiyai aṭaikkap pārkkiṉṟaṉar. vāḷpuṇ ēṟṟa taḻumpukaḷ pala tāṅkiya uṭal kaḷaiyuṭaiya uṉ koṭuñceyal vīrarkaḷaik kaṇṭōrellām tam amputtūṇiyiluḷḷa ampukaḷai vīṇākkātu pēṇukiṉṟaṉar.”
āṉālnīyō, tam kōṭṭaivāyiliṉ uḷḷē uṉ paṭaikaḷait taṭuttu niṟuttuvataṟkāka uṉ etirikaḷ ataṉ muṉ erikkum aiyaviyiṉ naccuppukaiyaip poruṭpaṭuttāmal muṉṉēṟik kūṟṟuvaṉpōla avarkaḷaik kaippaṟṟi māykkiṟāy.
“antō, alaiyāṭum katirmaṇikaḷ niṟainta vayal kaḷaiyuṭaiya uṉ etiriyiṉ nilaṅkaḷai iṉip pātukāppavar yār?”
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ

9. camūka vāḻvu
mūvaracarkaḷaiyum avarkaḷukkuk kīḻ uḷḷa vēḷirkaḷaiyum paṟṟiya mēṟkaṇṭa varalāṟṟiliruntu avarkaḷ aṭikkaṭi pōriṭṭu vantārkaḷeṉṟum, avarkaḷ kuṭikaḷ pōrkaḷiṉ naṭuvē, pōr muḻakkaṅkaḷiṭaiyē tāṉ vāḻntuvantaṉar eṉṟum kāṇalām. ittakaiya nilaiyil amaiti kālak kalaikaḷ eppaṭit taḻaikka muṭiyum eṉṟu kēṭkavum tōṉṟum. itaṟku viṭai eḷitu. paṭaittuṟaip payiṟcipeṟṟa vīrarmaṭṭumē pōrilīṭupaṭṭaṉar. maṟṟa makkaḷ matilnakarkaḷil vāḻntu. tollaiyiṉṟit tattam toḻilkaḷai naṭattiṉar.
talainakarkaḷiliruntu tolaivilirunta ciṟṟūrkaḷil vāḻnta ēḻaimakkaḷtām aṭikkaṭi ayalaracar tākkutalukku ilakkākik kaṭum tuṉpaṅkaḷukku āḷāki vantārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kaṉṟukālikaḷ koṇṭēkappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ vīṭukaḷ eri yūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. cila camayam avarkaḷ tam ūrkaḷiliruntu veḷiyē turattappaṭṭō, mikac ciṟitaḷavu etirppuk kāṭṭiṉāl kūṭak kollappaṭṭō avatiyuṟṟaṉar. āṉāl maturai, karūr, kāvirippaṭṭiṉam pōṉṟa periya nakarkaḷiluḷḷa peruntokaiyāṉa makkaḷ pōriṉ aṭṭūḻiyaṅkaḷiliruntu kiṭṭat taṭṭa muḻu viṭutalai peṟṟē vāḻntaṉar.
valimaiyāṉa araṇkāval vāynta mukkiya nakaraṅkaḷil palavaṟṟiṉ makkaḷum itupōlavē etiripaṭaikaḷatu vallāṭciyiṉ tīya viḷaivukaḷiliruntu peritum pātukāp paṭaintiruntaṉar. kalaittoḻilāḷarkaḷukkum potu makkaḷukkum nalamtarum vāṇikamum kaittoḻilkaḷum peritum ittakaiya araṇ kāvalvāynta nakarkaḷilēyē naṭaipeṟṟaṉa.
vāṇikariṉ cāttukkaḷ nakarukku nakar paṭaivīrariṉ pātukāppuṭaṉ payaṇam ceytaṉa. uḷnāṭṭiluḷḷa mukkiya vāṇikakkaḷaṅkaḷ maṉṉaṉ paṭaivīrarkaḷāl kāval ceyyap paṭṭu vantaṉa. aracapātaikaḷil cuṅkavarikaḷ pirikkappaṭṭaṉa.
āṭcimuṟai vaṉ muṭiyāṭciyāy illai. pēraḷavu kuṭiyāṭcippaṇpu amaintiruntatu. ituvum potu nalattukkup peritum ukantatāyiruntatu.
araciyaliṉ talaivaṉ marapurimaiyuṭaiya ōr aracaṉē. āṉāl avaṉ āṟṟal aintu kuḻukkaḷiṉ āṟṟalāl varaiyaṟuk kappaṭṭiruntatu. ivaiyē aimperuṅ kuḻāṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa.1 akkuḻukkaḷ muṟaiyē kuṭikaḷ, kurumār, maruttuvar, kaṇikaḷ, allatu kuṟikārar, amaiccar ākiyōriṉ pērāṭkaḷ aṭaṅki yavaiyāyiruntaṉa. ippērāṭkaḷ maṉṟam kuṭimakkaḷiṉ urimai kaḷaiyum kāppurimaikaḷaiyum pātukāttu vantaṉa.
kurumār ellā viṉaimuṟaikaḷaiyum iyakkiṉar. maruttuvar maṉṉaṉ uṭalnilaiyaiyum makkaḷ uṭalnalaṅ kaḷaiyum pātikkum ellāc ceytikaḷaiyum kavaṉittuvantaṉar. kaṇikaḷ potuviṉai muṟaikaḷukkuriya kālaṅkaḷai varaiyaṟai ceytatuṭaṉ, mukkiya ceytikaḷai muṉṉaṟivittaṉar. amaiccarkaḷ varivaruvāyp pirivaiyum celavaiyum aṟamuṟaiyaiyum kaṇkāṇittaṉar.2
ik kuḻāṅkaḷil ovvoṉṟukkum talainakaril taṉittaṉi iṭaṅkaḷ varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa3. mukkiya nikaḻccikaḷiṉ pōtum, aracaṉ aracirukkaiyil amarntu kolumaṉṟam kūṭum camayaṅkaḷilum, maṉṉurimai ūrvalaṅkaḷilum avarkaḷ kalantu koṇṭaṉar.
inta aimperum kuḻāṅkaḷaippaṟṟi iṉṉum vivaramāṉa ceytikaḷ aṟiya nām virumpuvatu iyalpu. āṉāl namakkuk kiṭaittuḷḷa paṇṭai ēṭukaḷil ivaṟṟiṉ mikutiyāṉa takavalkaḷ akappaṭavillai.
araciyal āṭci urimaikaḷ yāvum aracaṉiṭamum aimperu’ kuḻāṅkaḷiṭamumē poṟuppāka viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyarākiya mūṉṟu aracarkaḷum oruvarai oruvar cārāta taṉiyaracarāṉālum, mūvaracukaḷilum itē muṟai tāṉ kaiyāḷappaṭṭateṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakka ceyti ākum. ākavē mūvaracukaḷaiyum niṟuviyavarkaḷ teṉṉāṭṭil vantu kuṭiyēṟumuṉ vāḻnta mūlat tāyakamākiya makatappēraracilirunta muṟaiyaiyē iṅkum koṇṭuvantu piṉpaṟṟiṉar eṉṟu karuta iṭamuṇṭu.
aracaṉ tirumuṉpum araca cūḻalum perumatippukkum pukaṭṭāravārattukkum uriyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. peruntokai yāṉa paṇimakkaḷ avaṉukku ūḻiyam ceytaṉar. avarkaḷē aṭikkaṭi eṇpērāyaṅkaḷ eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ vivaram varumāṟu :-
maṇapporuḷāḷar, mālaikkārar, aṭaippakkārar, pākkuppaṇiyāḷar, kavacakkārar, āṭaiyaṇi puṉaivōr, pantamēntikaḷ, meykāvalar.
aracaṉ maṇikaḷ patikkappeṟṟup poṉṉālāṉa muṭi aṇintiruntāṉ. kaikaḷil poṉ kāppukkaḷum valatu kālil taṅkakkaḻalum, kaḻuttil muttu allatu maṇimālaiyum avaṉāl aṇiyappaṭṭa aṇimaṇikaḷ. muttukkōvaikaḷil oppaṉai ceyyappaṭṭa cīriya kuṭai oṉṟu, avaṉ araṇmaṉai yil tavacilamarntirukkum camayattilum araṇmaṉaikku veḷiyē pōkumiṭaṅkaḷilum avaṉ talaikku nērākap piṭikkappaṭṭaṉa.
potuvāka aracaṉ yāṉaimītō, kutiraimītō, nālu kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭiya tēriṉmītō ivarntu ceṉṟāṉ. kālaiyilum mālaiyilum araṇmaṉai vāyilil periya muracaṅkaḷ muḻaṅkiṉa. nāḻikai vaṭṭilkaḷāl kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭa ovvoru nāḻikai muṭivaiyum aṟivippataṟkeṉṟu araṇmaṉaiyil nāḻikai vāṇar amarvu peṟṟiruntaṉar.4
aracikkum avaḷ paṇippeṇṭirukkumāka araṇmaṉai yil oru taṉippakuti otukkappaṭṭiruntatu. aracurimaiyai avaḷ taṉ vaḻiyurimaiyākak koṇṭiruntālaṉṟi avaḷ muṭi aṇivatillai. ellā maṉṉurimaip potu amayaṅkaḷilum aracaṉuṭaṉ avaḷ vīṟṟiruntāḷ. āṉāl araṇmaṉaiyil araciyiṉ urimaiyi ṭaṅkaḷ āṭavar pukamuṭiyātavaiyā yiruntaṉa.5 peruṅkuṭip peṇṭiraṉṟik kuṟaḷkaḷum, kūṉkaḷum alikaḷum, avaḷaic cūḻntu paṇiceytaṉar.6
ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ araci vēṇmāḷ nilaveṟitta ōr iravil araṇmaṉai nilāmāṭattil taṉ kaṇavaṉaik kāṇacceṉṟa camayam avaḷaic cūḻntuceṉṟa paṇippeṇṭir cilappati kārattil kīḻvarumāṟu varuṇikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉar7:-
“muḻavum yāḻum iyakkikkoṇṭu cila peṇkaḷ vantaṉar. kūṉum kuṟaḷum nāṉam cantaṉam ākiyavai tāṅki vantaṉar. pēṭiyar peṇkaḷ uṭaiyil naṟumaṇac cāntum poṭiyum koṇarntaṉar. cilar kaiyil naṟumaṇa neykaḷum naṟumpu kaiyum mālaikaḷum mellaṇaikaḷum ēntiṉar. cilar nilaik kaṇṇāṭikaḷum āṭaikaḷum aṇimaṇikaḷum cumantu vantaṉar. mēlum cilar viḷakkai ēntiyavarāy, avviḷakkiṉ oḷiyil tāṅkaḷ aṇinta vaḷaikaḷ miṉṉumpaṭi naṭantuvantu” nīṭu vāḻka araci eṉṟu vāḻtteṭuttaṉar.
araciyaliṉ mukkiya paṇiyāḷar talaimai kuru, talaimai kaṇi, amaiccar, paṭaittalaivar ākiyōr8. muṟainaṭuvar, taṇṭalāḷar kaṭamaikaḷaic ceyyat taṇip paṇiyāḷar iruntaṉar. āṉāl ellā urimai vaḻakkukaḷilum kuṟṟavaḻakku kaḷilum aracaṉē kaṭaici nilaikkuriya ucca uyarnaṭuvarā yiruntāṉ. naṭuvarāyamarntiruntavar muṟaimaṉṟattiluḷḷa maṟṟa ellāp paṇiyāḷariṭamiruntum taṉittu vēṟu pirittaṟiyum vakaiyil taṉippaṭṭa talaiyaṇi aṇintiruntār.9
vaḻakkāṭikaḷukku ilavacamākavē muṟai vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. āyiṉum taṇṭaṉaikaḷ mikak kaṭumaiyāka iruntaṉa. kuṟṟamum itaṉāl arunikaḻvākavē iruntatu. kaiyil toṇṭiyuṭaṉ piṭippaṭṭa tiruṭaṉ kolait taṇṭaṉai tarappaṭṭāṉ10. takāp puṇarccic ceyalil piṭipaṭṭavaṉ kolai ceyyappaṭṭāṉ11. avveṇṇattuṭaṉ maṟṟoruvaṉ vīṭṭil nuḻaintavaṉ kālkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa12. kuruṭṭu nampikkaikaḷ cārnta accamē cila camayam nītiyiṉ peyarāl aracaṉaic ceyalkaḷ ceyvittaṉa. makkaḷ vāḻvu kuṟitta aracaṉ āṇaikaḷ yāṉai mītēṟic ceṉṟa paṇi mutalvarkaḷāl talainakarameṅkum muracaṟaintu terivikkappaṭṭatu.13
tīrvai, cuṅkam, nilavari ākiyavaiyē vari varumāṉattil mukkiya pakutikaḷ, tīrvaikaḷ ellāk kaṭal tuṟaimukaṅkaḷilum pirikkappaṭṭaṉa. iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭa carakkukaḷ yāvum araca ciṉṉam tāṅkiya muttirai poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. tīrvai celuttap paṭṭapiṉpē carakkukaḷ vaṇikariṉ paṇṭa cālaikaḷukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa14. cuṅkaṅkaḷ vaṇikac cāttukkaḷ celvataṟkariya araca pātaikaḷilum ovvoru araciṉ ellaikaḷilum teṇṭappaṭṭaṉa15. nilavari paṇamākavō carakkākavō koṭuppavaṉatu viruppappaṭi koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
kuṭimaṉṉarum vēḷirum koṭukkum tiṟai, ellaip puṟap pōrnaṭavaṭikkaiyil peṟṟa koḷḷai varuvāy, muttup paṇṇaikaḷ, kāṭṭiyāṉai, kāṭṭuvaḷam ākiya aracar taṉik kaḷaṅkaḷil varum ātāyaṅkaḷ ākiyavai aracaṉ varuvāyil perumpakutiyāyamaintaṉa. nilavariyil āṟil orupaṅku nērmai vāynta aracaṉ paṅkāyiruntatu. araciyalārāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa taṇṇirukkīṭākat taṉippaṭa uḻavarkaḷiṭamiruntu taṇṇīrvari pirikkappaṭṭatu.
aracaṉ araciyalukku maṭṭumaṉṟic camutāyattukkum talaivaṉāyiruntāṉ. vaḻakkamāṉa avaṉ meykāvalar mutaliya paṇiyāḷarkaḷ cūḻavē ceṉṟālum, annilaiyilēyē makkaḷuṭaṉ taṅkutaṭaiyiṉṟip paḻakiṉāṉ. talainakariṉ viḻākkaḷil avaṉ talaimai vakittāṉ. pañcam, perunōy kālaṅkaḷil nōṉpukaḷ, vēḷvikaḷāṟṟuvatilum avaṉ mutaliṭam tāṅkiṉāṉ. makkaḷ iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷai avaṉ pakirntāṉ kuṟainta aḷavil aracavai marapukaḷ aṅṅaṉam pakirumpaṭi avaṉai valintu kaṭṭāyap paṭuttiṉa.
cila aracariṭam makkaḷ vaḻakkattukku mikutiyāṉa paṟṟuk kāṭṭiṉar. paṭaiyeṭuppāḷar taṅkaḷ ūrkaḷaik kaippaṟṟiya vivaraṅkaḷil, ūr makkaḷ muḻuvatumē taṅkaḷ vīṭu kuṭikaḷai viṭviṭṭut tam aracaṉ irunta pakutiyilēyē ceṉṟu kuḻumiya ceytikaḷ teriyavarukiṉṟaṉa.16 viḻākkālaṅkaḷil, potu ampalaṅkaḷilum cari, taṉi illaṅkaḷilum cari, eṅkum makkaḷāl maṉṉaṉ veṟṟiyum nīṇṭa vāḻvum kuṟittu vāḻttu vaḻipāṭu ceyyappaṭṭatu.
kōyililiruntu mīṇṭa orunaṅkai taṉ tōḻiyiṭam pēcumpōtu vaḻakkamāṉa ivvāḻttaik kēṭkiṟōm. “(em aracaṉ) ātaṉ vāḻka, (em aracaṉ) aviṉi vāḻka eṉa eṉ tāy vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ. attuṭaṉ nāṉum ōcaipaṭāmal uḷḷattuk kuḷḷāka (eṉ kātalaṉ) ceḻuṅkaḻaṉiyai uṭaiyāṉ viraintu ūr tirumpuka eṉṟu vaḻipaṭṭēṉ” eṉṟu avaḷ kūṟukiṟāḷ.17
āriyar piṉpaṟṟiya pirama, kṣattiriya, vaiciya, cūttirar eṉṟa nāl varuṇap pākupāṭu tamiḻarkaḷiṭaiyil iruntatillai. tirunūl aṇiyappeṟṟavarkaḷukkellām āriyarkaḷāl irupiṟap pāḷar eṉṟa ciṟappuppeyar vaḻaṅkappaṭukiṉṟatu. paṇṭait tamiḻilak kiyattil atu pārppaṉarai maṭṭumē kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. itiliruntu tirunūlaṇinta kṣattiriyarum, vaiciyarum, tamiḻakattil irukka villai eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.
tūya taṉittamiḻiṉattavariṭaiyē mikavum uyar matippuk kuriyavarkaḷ aṟivar allatu cittarkaḷē. avarkaḷ kālattiṉ mūṉṟu kūṟukaḷaiyum atāvatu iṟantakālam, nikaḻkālam, varuṅ kālam mūṉṟaiyum aṟintavarkaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ otuṅkiya camaya vāḻvu vāḻntatāl, perunakaraṅkaḷukku veḷiyilēyē taṅkiyiruntaṉar. pārppaṉar āṭal naṅkaiyaruṭaṉum kūttiyaru ṭaṉum ūṭāṭat tayaṅkavillai.18 kātalariṭaiyē tūtuvarāyc cellavum tayaṅkavillai. āṉāl aṟivarkaḷ ivaṟṟiliruntu muṟṟilum vilakiyiruntaṉar.19
aṟivarukkaṭuttapaṭi matippuṭaiyavar uḻavar allatu vēḷāḷar. aṟivar tuṟavikaḷē. ākavē camutāyam paṟṟiya maṭṭil, iv vēḷāḷarē ucca uyarvakappiṉarāvar. avarkaḷē nāṭṭiṉ uyarkuṭip perumakkaḷ allatu nilakkiḻār marapiṉarāyiruntaṉar. avarkaḷ veḷḷāḷar atāvatu veḷḷattai āṇṭavarkaḷ eṉṟum, kārāḷar allatu mukilai āṇṭavar eṉṟum aḻaikkap paṭṭaṉar. ip paṭṭaṅkaḷ peruveḷḷaṅkaḷai aṇaiyiṭṭut taṭuttu uḻavut toḻilukkāka atait tēkkivaippatil avar kaḷukkirunta tiṟattaik kāṭṭiṉa.
cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷum tamiḻaka vēḷiril perumpālāṉavarkaḷum inta vēḷāḷar marapaic cērnta varkaḷē. ciṟitaḷavē nilamuṭaiya ēḻai vēḷāḷar kuṭiyiṉar potuvāka ‘vīḻkuṭi uḻavar’ allatu vīḻcciyaṭainta vēḷāḷar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. maṟṟa vēḷāḷarkaḷ celvak kuṭiyiṉar eṉpataiyē itu kuṟittirukkakkūṭum.20
perumpukaḻk karikālaṉ aruvāḷarai veṉṟu avarkaḷ nāṭṭait taṉ aracuṭaṉ cērttukkoṇṭapōtu, avaṉ veṉṟa  nāṭukaḷait taṉ vēḷāḷar talaivarkaḷukkup pakirntu koṭuttāṉ21. it talaivarkaḷiṉ vaḻivantavar iṉṟum taṅkaḷ nilaṅkaḷuṭaṉ araciyalāriṉkīḻ22 nēraṭiyāṉa urimaipeṟṟa varāy ciṟunilak kiḻārāka vāḻkiṉṟaṉar23. iṉṟum avarkaḷ mutalikaḷ allatu mutal vakuppiṉar eṉṟē aḻaikkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar.
vaṭukam allatu taṟkālat teluṅku nāṭṭai veṉṟa vēḷāḷa kuṭiyiṉar vēḷamar eṉṟaḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar. aṅkuḷḷa perunilakkiḻār iṉṟum iṉṉum ivvēḷamar vakuppiṉarē.
kaṉṉaṭa nāṭṭil ivvēḷāḷar peḷḷāḷamarapai niṟuvip pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka nāṭāṇṭaṉar.
vēḷāḷarkaḷukkuk kaṅkakulam allatu kaṅkavamcam eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu. ēṉeṉil avarkaḷ piḷiṉi, ṭālami ākiyavar kaḷāl kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa kaṅkait tīrattiluḷḷa vallamaivāynta kaṅkariṭē eṉṟa māperuṅkuṭi marapiliruntu taṅkaḷ kulamarapai varaṉmuṟaiyākakkoṇṭaṉar.
vēḷāḷarkaḷē perumpālum niṟainta maicūriṉ oru pakuti ki. pi. 10,11-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil kaṅkavāṭi eṉṟaḻaikkap paṭṭatu.
immarapaic cērnta maṟṟoru kuṭivaḻi kaṅka vamcam eṉṟa peyaruṭaṉ 11,12-ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil oricāvai āṇṭatu.
āyar allatu iṭaiyarum, vēṭṭuvar allatu vēṭarum uḻavarukku aṭuttapaṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷ.24 poṟkollar, karumāṉ, taccar, kuyavar mutaliya kalaittoḻilāḷar āyarkaḷukku aṭutta paṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷ. avarkaḷukku aṭutta paṭiyiluḷḷavarkaḷ paṭaiyāṭciyar allatu paṭaikkalam mēṟkoṇṭavarkaḷ. valaiyar allatu mīṉ toḻilāḷarum pulaiyar allatu tōṭṭikaḷum kaṭaicip paṭiyiliruntārkaḷ.
uyarvakuppiṉar teruvil ceṉṟapōtu, tāḻ vakuppiṉar avarkaḷukku vaḻiviṭṭaṉar25. perumakaṉaik kaṇṭa pulaiyaṉ allatu tōṭṭi vaḻipaṭupavaṉpōla iru kaikaḷaiyum kūppit talaivaṇaṅkiṉāṉ.26
aṭimaittaṉam tamiḻariṭai iruntatillai. appaḻaṅ kālattil avarkaḷ aṭaintirunta uyar nākarikappaṭiyai itu kuṟittuk kāṭṭukiṟatu.
tamiḻarkaḷiṭaiyayēyuḷḷa mēṟkāṭṭiya vakuppumuṟai mekāsteṉis kaṇṭu tīṭṭiya paṇṭai makatappēraraciluḷḷa makkaḷ nilaiyaip peritum ottuḷḷaṉa.
avar kuṟittuḷḷapaṭi makkaḷtokuti ēḻu vakuppukkaḷākap pirivupaṭṭiruntatu. mutal vakuppu aṟivar vakuppē. āṉāl tokaiyil ivarkaḷ mikamikak kuṟaivāṉavarkaḷē. taṉippaṭṭa maṉitarkaḷāl avarkaḷ vēḷvikaḷilum maṟṟat toṭakkattil aracaṉ avarkaḷait taṉ pēravaikku āṇṭiṉ pañcapparuvaṅkaḷ, maḻai, araciyalkōḷāṟukaḷ ākiyavaipaṟṟiya muṉṉaṟivurai nalkiṉar.
inta aṟivarkaḷ āṭaiyaṟṟavarkaḷākavē iruntārkaḷ. kuḷirkālattil avarkaḷ veyilkāyumpaṭi veṭṭaveḷiyi lēyē taṅkiṉārkaḷ. vēṉiṟkālattil avarkaḷ pulveḷi kaḷilum tāḻnilaṅkaḷilum maraṅkaḷiṉaṭiyil taṅkiṉārkaḷ.
iraṇṭāvatu vakuppu uḻavar vakuppu. atu taṉ nēra muḻuvataiyum nilam paṇpaṭuttuvatilēyē celaviṭṭatu. avarkaḷ tam uḻaippiṉ nāṉkilorupaṅkai aracaṉukku iṟaiyākat tantaṉar.
āyarkaḷum vēṭarkaḷum mūṉṟām vakuppiṉar. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kālnaṭaikaḷaip pēṇiyum kūṭāraṅkaḷil taṅkiyum nāṭōṭi vāḻvu vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ kālnaṭaikaḷaiyē variyākat tantaṉar. vayalkaḷil vitaitta payirkaḷai aḻikkum paṟavaikaḷaiyum vilaṅkukaḷaiyum akaṟṟi nilattaip pātukāp pataṟkāka aracaṉiṭamiruntu avarkaḷ paṭi nel peṟṟaṉar.
nāṉkām vakuppu toḻil vakuppu. poruḷkaḷ vāṅki viṟṟal, kūlivēlai ceytal avarkaḷ vēlai. paṭaikkalat toḻilāḷarum kavacat toḻilāḷarum, ellāvakaippaṭṭa kalaittoḻilāḷarkaḷum ippakuppiṉar.
pōr vīrar aintām vakuppāyiṉar. avarkaḷ aracaṉ celavil vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷukkup paṭaittuṟai vēlai tavira vēlai illai. amaitikkālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ nērattaik kuṭittuc cōmpittirivatilēyē kaḻittaṉar.
āṟām vakuppu mēṟpārvai ceytatu, nāṭṭilum nakarilum naṭappatai oṟṟaṟintu aracaṉukkō taṇṭalāḷarukkō aṟivappatu avarkaḷ kaṭamai.
ēḻāvatu vakuppu taṉṉāṭciyuṭaiya nakarkaḷil potu āṭcik kāriyaṅkaḷil maṉṉaṉ allatu taṇṭalāḷarkaḷukku aṟivurai kūṟiya maṉṟattār aṭaṅkiyatāyiruntatu.
nāṭṭiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkac caṭṭatiṭṭaṅkaḷ vakuppukku vakuppu kalappumaṇattaik kaṇṭittatu. evarum oru vakuppiliruntu maṟṟoru vakuppukku māṟavum icaivaḷikkap paṭavillai. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, ōr uḻavaṉ iṭaiyaṉāka māṟavō, allatu iṭaiyar vakuppu allatu kalait toḻil vakuppiliruntu peṇkoḷḷavō muṭiyātu.
aṟivar vakappukkumaṭṭum iccaṭṭatiṭṭam muḻuvilak kaḷittatu. avar enta vakuppiṉarākavum irukkalām. ēṉeṉil avar vāḻvu eḷitāṉa oṉṟalla. attuṭaṉ avarkaḷil mikac cilarē muṉṉaṟiviṉtiṟam vāykkappeṟṟaṉar.27
paṇṭai makatampaṟṟiya mēṟkūṟiya vakuppup piriviṉai viḷakkattil oṟṟarkaḷaiyum mēṟpārvaiyāḷarkaḷaiyum aṟivuraiyāḷarkaḷaiyum taṉi vakuppukkaḷāka mekastaṉis karutiyatu tavaṟu eṉpatu teḷivu. maṟṟa vakuppukaḷellām tamiḻari ṭaiyilum makatattilum muṟṟilum icaikiṉṟaṉa.
itiliruntu iru iṉaṅkaḷumē mikattol paḻaṅkālat tilēyē nākarikamaṟṟa nilaiyiliruntu vilaki nīṇṭa kālamākak kuṭivāḻvum nilaiyāṉa āṭcimuṟaiyum uṭaiyavarāy iruntaṉar eṉṟum, pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷākap pōrum muraṭṭup pūcalum avarkaḷiṭaiyē illātiruntaṉa eṉṟum teriya varukiṟatu. avarkaḷ nākarikam āriyar nākarikattaiviṭa mikavum paḻamai yāṉatu eṉpatil aiyamillai. ēṉeṉil āriyarkaḷiṭaiyē pōrvīrarpaṭi kurumārpaṭikku aṭuttāyiruntatu. nērmāṟākat tamiḻariṭaiyē vēḷāḷarē camayakurumāruk kaṭuttapaṭiyāyi runtaṉar. pōrvīrar vakuppu avarkaḷukku maṭṭumaṉṟi, āyarka ḷukkum uḻavut toḻilāḷarukkam kūṭak kīḻppaṭṭiruntatu.
nām kuṟippiṭum kālattukkuk kuṟaintatu aintu allatu āṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpiruntē pārppaṉar tamiḻakattil kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ tamiḻarmītu taṅkaḷ cāti varuṇamuṟaiyaic cumanta arumpāṭupaṭṭaṉar.
namakkuk kiṭṭiyuḷḷa mikap paḻamaiyāṉa tamiḻ ilakkaṇat tiṉ āciriyar tolkāppiyar eṉṟa oru pārppaṉar.28 avar ki. mu. 1-vatu allatu 2-vatu nūṟṟāṇṭukkuriyavar. avar aṭikkaṭi aṟivar allatu cittarkaḷaippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṟār.29 āṉāl camutāya vakuppukkaḷaik kuṟikkum iyalil āciriyar aṟivaraippaṟṟiya pēccaiyē eṭukkavillai. tirunūlaṇinta pārppaṉaraiyē30 mutal vakuppiṉarākak kuṟippiṭukiṟār.31
mikunta tiṟamai nayattuṭaṉ  pōrvīrarai vilakki aracarai maṭṭum avar teyvika vakuppil cērttukkoḷkiṟār. cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyarākiya mūvaracarmaṭṭum oru vakuppāka amaiyap pōtiyavareṉṟu avar karutiṉār pōlum! vāṇikattāl vāḻpavarē mūṉṟām vakuppu eṉa avar kuṟittār. āyiṉum aracarō vaṇikarō tirunūl aṇintatāka avar kuṟikkavillai!
itaṉpiṉ avar vēḷāḷarait taṉiyākap pirittu avar kaḷukku uḻavallatu toḻilillai eṉṟu vakukkiṟār. iṅkum avar vēḷāḷar cūttirar eṉṟu kuṟikkavillai. āyiṉum potunilai vēḷāḷar cūttirarākak kaṇikkappaṭa vēṇṭiya varkaḷ eṉṟa toṉipaṭak kuṟikkiṟār. atēcamayam aracarāka amarnta vēḷāḷar kṣattiriyarākak karutappaṭavēṇṭu meṉṟum pirikkiṟār.
tamiḻarait taṅkaḷ cāti varuṇamuṟaikkuk koṇṭuvarap pārppaṉar ceyta mutal muyaṟci ituvē. āṉāl, tamiḻakattil kṣattiriyar, vaiciyar, cūttirar ākiya pirivukaḷ illāta nilaiyil avarkaḷ veṟṟiyaṭaiya muṭiyavillai. ēṉeṉil iṉṟaḷavum taṉṉai kṣattiriyaṉ eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷum oru paṭaiyāṭciyiṉ vīṭṭil allatu taṉṉai vaiciyaṉ eṉṟu kūṟikkoḷḷum oru vaṇikaṉ vīṭṭil veḷḷāḷaṉ uṇavukoḷḷavō kuṭikka nīrpeṟavō ceyvatillai.
tolkāppiyar tam ilakkaṇattil āyar allatu iṭaiyaraippaṟṟiyum, vēṭuvar allatu vēṭaraippaṟṟiyum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. āṉāl vakuppup pirivupaṟṟiya iyalil avar maṟavar, valaiyar, pulaiyar mutaliya vakuppukkaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟavillai.32 ēṉeṉil pārppaṉīya cātimuṟaiyuṭaṉ muraṇ paṭātapaṭi avarkaḷaippaṟṟik kuṟippiṭa avarāl muṭiyavillai.33
tamiḻmakkaḷ aṇinta āṭai camutāyattil avarkaḷ paṭimuṟaiyaiyum avarkaḷ marapiṉaṅkaḷaiyum poṟuttup palvakai vēṟupāṭuṭaiyatu. tūya taṉittamiḻariṭaiyē naṭuttara vakuppiṉar potuvāka araiyaic cuṟṟi muḻantāḷvarai paṭiyum paṭi oṉṟum talaiyaic cuṟṟit taḷarvākak kaṭṭiya oṉṟumāka iraṇṭu āṭaikaḷē aṇintārkaḷ.34 avarkaḷ, talaimayirai veṭṭuva tillai. atai iyaṟkai vaḷarcciyaḷavukkē vaḷaraviṭṭaṉar allatu orupuṟamāka atil oru periya muṭiyiṭṭuk kaṭṭiṉar. intat talaiyiṉ muṭiyaikkaṭṭa uyar vakuppiṉar paḷapaḷappāṉa nīlamaṇikaḷ kōtta niṟamārnta iḻaikaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. iḻaikaḷ kuñcampōl toṅkaviṭappaṭṭaṉa.35
nākamarapucārnta maṟavar talaivaṉ araiyāṭaiyai nīlaniṟa vārāl iṟakkik kaṭṭiṉāṉ avaṉ talaiyil mayiliṟakukaḷ cerukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.36
pārppaṉar tam talaimayiraik kuṟukattaṟittaṉar. āṉāl talaiyucciyil oru kuṭumiyai viṭṭuvaittaṉar. oru tamiḻppulavar kutiraiyiṉ talaiyil uḷḷa kuṟumayirukkup pārppaṉa iḷaiñar talaimayirai uvamai kūṟiyuḷḷār37. potu vītikaḷaik kāvalkātta vīrarum aracaṉ araṇmaṉai yiluḷḷa paṇiyāṭkaḷum caṭṭaikaḷ aṇintārkaḷ.38
ip paṇṭaikkālattil muḻumēṉi uṭai oru talaivaṉ ciṉṉamāyamaiyavillai, oru vēlaikkāraṉ ciṉṉamākavē karutappaṭṭatu. perumakkaḷ veppamāṉa vaḷinilaiyil mikunta vāyppukkēṭiṉṟi enta aḷavu uṭai aṇiyamuṭiyumō anta aḷavu kuṟainta uṭaiyē aṇintaṉar.
tamiḻppeṇṭiriṉ potunilai āṭaiyil tōḷum kaiyum iṭuppuvaraiyuḷḷa uṭalum muṟṟilum veṟumaiyākavē iruntaṉa. āṭai eṉpatellām muḻuvatum araiyiliruntu toṭaṅkik kaṇaikkālvaraiyilumē iruntaṉa.39 tiṟantirunta uṭaṟpakuti potuvākac cantaṇa mutaliya naṟumaṇac cāntukaḷāl oppaṉai ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu40. nākappeṇkaḷō amarāvatic cetukkuk kalaiyil kāṇappaṭuvatupōlak kiṭṭattaṭṭa āṭaiyaṟṟavarkaḷākavē iruntatākat terikiṟatu.
āṭal naṅkaiyar aṇinta melliya malmal āṭai araiyi liruntu tuṭai naṭuvaraikkumē mūṭiṉa. atuvum mikanēriya tāyiruntatāl atu mēṉiyai oru ciṟitum maṟaikkavillai41. malaṅkuṭi maṅkaiyar taṅkaḷ araiyaic cuṟṟik kaṭṭik kayiṟṟil māṭṭappaṭṭa taḻaikkottukkaḷaiyē aṇintaṉar42. pāṇarkaḷiṉ ūr cūḻvaraliṉpōtu avarkaḷuṭaṉ ceṉṟa avarkaḷ maṉaiviyar muṟṟilum āṭaiyaṟṟavarkaḷākavē varuṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar43. uṇmaiyil paṇṭaiya intiyāvil ammaṇattaṉmai ōr iḻipaṇpākak karutappaṭṭatākat teriyavillai.
tamiḻppeṇṭir taṅkaḷ kūntalaip putumaivāynta muṟaikaḷil puṉaintaṉar. atai avarkaḷ aintu pakutikaḷākap pirittu, ovvoṉṟaiyum taṉittaṉiyākac cuṟṟi allatu piṉṉi aintu taṉittaṉi muṭikaḷuṭaṉ vaṉappu nayamuṭaiyaṉa eṉṟu karutap paṭṭavakaikaḷilē muṭippukkaṭanta pakutiyai talaiyiṉpiṉ toṅkaviṭṭaṉar. kūntalaip pēṇuvatilum avarkaḷ mikunta kavaṉam celuttiyatākat terikiṟatu. ēṉeṉil iḷam peṇkaḷ talaiyōṭṭiṉ meṉṟōl teriyumaḷavu taṅkaḷ talai mayiraik  kattirikaḷāl oṭṭak kattirittu, aintu muṭikaḷ maṭṭum oṉṟiliruntu oṉṟu akalvāka viṭuvatum, peṇkaḷ vaḷarcci yuṭaṉ vaḷarcciyāka im muṭippakuti paṭippaṭiyāka virivupaṭuttap paṭṭu iṟutiyil talaimuḻuvatum uṭkoḷḷuvatum vaḻakkameṉṟu tōṟṟukiṟatu.44
kūntalai ivvāṟu muṭikkumuṉ aimpālākap pakukkum vaḻakkam iṉṉum parmiyariṭaiyē irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kēḷvip paṭukiṟōm. itu eṉṟum āriyappeṇṭirāl mēṟkoḷḷap paṭavillai. taṟpōtu itu tamiḻariṭamiruntum muṟṟilum maṟaintuviṭṭatu.
āṭavarum, peṇṭirum, ellā vakuppiṉarum, tam talaikku eṇṇeyiṭṭaṉar. peṇṭir talaikōti muṭikkum pōtu aṭikkaṭi maṇap poruḷkaḷai vaḻaṅkiyatuṭaṉ palvakai malarkaḷālum aṇimaṇikaḷālum atai oppaṉai ceytaṉar.
irupālārum palavakai naṟuneykaḷāl mēṉikku maṇa mūṭṭiṉar, cenniṟam, poṉṉiṟam koṇṭa palvakaip poṭi kaḷaiyum vaḻaṅkiṉar45. mātar kaṇṇimaikaḷukkuk karuniṟa mai tīṭṭiṉar46. uyar vakuppiṉar illaṅkaḷil cāmpirāṇi mutaliya maṇappukaip poruḷkaḷ pukaippaṭṭaṉa.47
āṭai aḷavu kuṟaivāyiruntālum tamiḻ makkaḷ aṇi maṇikaḷai ērāḷamākap puṉaintārkaḷ. kaḻuttu, kai, iṭuppu ākiyavaṟṟil aṇinta palvakai aṇimaṇikaḷē avarkaḷ mēṉi oppaṉaiyil mikac ciṟappuvāynta pakutiyāyiruntatu.
vēḷirum irunilakkiḻavarum vilaiyuyarnta maṇimālai allatu muttumālai aṇintaṉar. kaikaḷil paḷuvēṟiya keṭṭik kāppup pūṇṭaṉar. araca kuṭiyiṉarum ciṟukuṭi maṉṉarum itaṉuṭaṉ taṉicciṟappuppaṭak kālil kaḻal pūṭṭiṉar.
vēḷir kuṭimakaṉ oruvaṉ aṇimaṇi kīḻvarumāṟu varuṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu :-
“avaṉ kālkaḷil iraṭṭaip poṉṉiḻaip piṉṉaluṭaṉ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭa ciṟu maṇikaḷiṉ varicai vāynta kaḻal kiṭantatu. avaṉ araiyaic cuṟṟip poṉ kācukaḷ nirai niraiyākat tūkkiya pavaḷakkoṭi araiñāṇ toṅkiṟṟu. ataṉmītu avaṉ iṭuppil mika nēriya oru malmal āṭai uṭuttiyiruntāṉ. naṇṭiṉ kaṇkaḷpōla aḻakuṟap puṉaiyap paṭṭa piṉṉal vēlaip pāṭuṭaiya aḻakiya tōḷvaḷaikaḷ tōḷai aṇi ceytaṉa. kaḻuttil toṅkiya iraṭṭaip paṭṭaip poṟ caṅkiliyiliruntu maḻu vāḷ paṭaikaḷiṉ ciṟṟurukkaḷum atiṟkōtta erutiṉ uruvāynta oru patakkamum toṅkiṉa. avaṉ talaimītu mup purikaḷāka muṟukkiya muttup pavaḷaṅkaḷālāṉa talaimālaiyum ataṉmēl oru pūmālaiyum oḷimikka nīlamaṇikaḷkōtta iḻaiyāl oruṅku piṇaikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa.”48
“tāḻnilai vakuppiṉariṭaiyē mātar caṅku vaḷaikaḷ, veṇmaṇi, nīlamaṇikaḷālāṉa kaḻuttumālai, mutaliya malivāṉa nakaikaḷ aṇintaṉar. naṭuttara vakuppiṉar perumpālum taṅka nakaikaḷē aṇintārkaḷ. ēṉeṉil intak kālattil intiyāvil veḷḷi mikavum aritāṉa oru poruḷ. celvariṉ nakaikaḷ mika vilaiyēṟiyatāka iruntaṉa. potu āṭalmakaḷir pūṇkaḷē mikap pakaṭṭāravāramāṉavaiyāy iruntaṉa.”
“āṭalnaṅkai yaṇinta pūṇkaḷaippaṟṟic cilappati kārattil tarappaṭum kīḻvarum varuṇaṉai nakaiyaṇi maṇivakaiyil irunta āravāra iṉpa vāḻvaik kāṭṭavallatu.”
“avaṉ kūntalai maṇaneyyil tōyttāḷ. anta ney muppattiraṇṭu vakaic ceṭikaḷiliruntum aivakai maṇap poruḷ kaḷiliruntum pattuvakait tuvarpporuḷkaḷiṉālum puṉainti yaṟṟappaṭṭatu. akiṟpukaiyil kūntalai ulartti piṉ avaḷ ataṉai aimpālākap pakuttu kattūri kalanta naṟuney pūciṉāḷ. kālaṭikaḷai avaḷ cempañcikkuḻampāl cevvaṇṇam tōyat tīṭṭiṉāḷ. kāliṉ ciṟu viralkaḷukkup pīlikaḷ māṭṭiṉāḷ.”
“avaḷ kālaṭikaḷil kaṇaikkāliliruntu peruviral varai oṉṟaṭuttoṉṟu itaḻ virittāṟpōṉṟu paṭarum kālaṇi vakaiyaṇintāḷ. kaṇaikkālil maṇic cilampu, cataṅkai mutaliya nālvakaik kaḻalkaḷaṇintāḷ. tuṭaikaḷil tuṭai māṭṭikaḷ ēṟiṉa. vaṉappamaiya maṭitta mika nērttiyāṉa nīlaniṟa malmal āṭaiyai avaḷ iṭuppil cērttu ataṉmītu muppattiraṇṭu maṇikaḷuḷḷa mēkalai aṇintāḷ.”
“muḻaṅkaikkumēlāka mika uyarnta muttumaṇikaḷālāṉa poṉpotinta tōḷvaḷaikaḷum, muṉkaikaḷil paḷapaḷappāṉa valampuri, pavaḷa vaḷaikaḷuṭaṉum, cila poṉ vaḷaikaḷum cila ariya vēlaippāṭamainta vayira marakatamaṇikaḷ patitta vaḷaikaḷum pūṭṭiṉāḷ. kaiviralkaḷukkup paṟpala vakaippaṭṭa mōtiraṅkaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. oṉṟu makaravāy pōṉṟiruntatu. oṉṟu naṭuvē marakatamum cūḻa vayiraṅkaḷum uṭaiyatāyiruntatu.”
“kaḻuttil (vayiṟṟu naṭuvaraivantu maṟaikkumpaṭi)” palavakai poṉmālaikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. oṉṟu taṅkac caṅkiliyākavum oṉṟu muṟukkiya poṉṉiḻai vaṭivākavum maṟṟoṉṟu poṉtaḻai mālaiyākavum iruntatu. kaḻuttiṉ piṉṉē mālaikaḷ aṉait taiyum cērttu oru nilaippaṭuttip piṭikkumpaṭi mikap pakaṭṭāṉa tōṟṟamuṭaiya orucērt tiṟukki aṇiyappeṟṟatu.
“tōṭukaḷil māṟimāṟi vayiram nīlamum iṭam peṟṟaṉa.”
“talaiyil avaḷ cūṭṭiya aṇimaṇip piṉṉalkaḷ maṟṟa ellā aṇimaṇikaḷaiyum orucēra vellat takkavaiyāy iruntaṉa.”49
peṇṭir vīṇpakaṭṭillāmalē vāḻkkait toḻil kaḷaṅkaḷilum iṉpap poḻutu pōkkukaḷilum piṟaruṭaṉ tārāḷamākap paḻakiṉar. nakaraṅkaḷilum paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷilum ēḻai vakuppup peṇṭir teruviṟpaṉaiyāḷarkaḷākavum, ciṟu vaṇika rākavum, kaṭaikkārarākavum, celvar vīṭukaḷil paṇiyāṭ kaḷākavum toḻilīṭupāṭu, koṇṭiruntaṉar. ciṟṟūrkaḷil avarkaḷ vayal kaḷilum tōṭṭaṅkaḷilum āṭavaruṭaṉ ottu vēlaiceytu avarkaḷ kaṭṭanaṭṭaṅkaḷait tāṅkaḷ pakirntukoṇṭaṉar.
uyarvakuppu mātar peritum vīṭṭilēyē vāḻntaṉar āṉāl avarkaḷum camūkattiliruntu muṟṟilum tuṇṭupaṭṭu viṭavillai. aracimutal ellā uyarkuṭimātarumē kōyil kaḷukkuc ceṉṟu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. mālai nēraṅkaḷil avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ vīṭṭumāṭi muṟṟaṅkaḷil vantiruntu teruvil kāṭcikaḷaip pārttiruntārkaḷ. viḻakkālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ ūrvalaṅkaḷil kalantukoṇṭārkaḷ. naṇparkaḷ, uṟaviṉarkaḷukku aḻaippuk kūṟavum avarkaḷ ceṉṟārkaḷ.50
peṇṭirukkirunta taṅkutaṭaiyaṟṟa viṭutalaiyurimai kāraṇamāka, iḷamparuvattiṉar tirumaṇattiṟku muṉpē kātalilīṭu paṭuvataṟku vāyppiruntatu. ōr iḷamaṅkai kātalaṉuṭaṉ uṭaṉ pōkkup peṟuvatukūṭa, piṉṉāl avarkaḷ uṟaviṉariṭam mīṇṭuvantu maṇavāḻvil īṭupaṭuvatāṉāl, takātateṉṟu karutappaṭavillai.
aracavai, paṭaivīṭu, cōlai ākiya ellā iṭaṅkaḷilumē annāḷ poṉṉiṉ pēravā āḷavillai. aṉpē āṇṭatu. irupālārum oruvaruṭaṉ oruvar parimāṟikkoḷḷum muṟai iṉṟiruppataiviṭa iṇakka nayamum paṇpunayamum uṭaiyatā yiruntatu. innilaiyil annāḷaiya kavitaiyil perumpakuti kātalpaṟṟiyatāyiruppatilum, kātal pākkaḷ eḻutuvatupaṟṟiya ilakkaṇa vitikaḷ amaintirunta tilum viyappillai.
iḷamparuvattiṉar maṇavāḻvukku muṉ kātal kūrtal tamiḻariṭaiyē aṉṟē mikap paḻamaivāynta nilaipeṟṟuviṭṭa oru vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. eṉavētāṉ annāḷil tiruvaḷḷuvar perumāṉ iyaṟṟiya pukaḻmikka aṟamuṟaic camutāya oḻukka nūlil51 nūliṉ mūṉṟu pālkaḷil oru pāl kātalpaṟṟiya tāyuḷḷatu. ivvaḻakkam iṉṉum tamiḻakattil muṟṟilum aḻintupaṭṭu viṭṭateṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkillai. ēṉeṉil atu iṉṉum malaiyāḷakkaraiyil nīṭikkiṟatu. irupālāruk kiṭaiyilum irunta toṭarpaip paṇṭaik kaviñar coṟkaḷālēyē kūṟuvatu nalam.
kātalpaṟṟiya cila pāṭalkaḷiliruntu kīḻvarum karuttuk kaḷait tarukiṟōm :- 
oru peṇ naṭantukoṇṭa vakaipaṟṟi avaḷ tōḻi peṇṇiṉ kātalaṉiṭam kīḻvarumāṟu pēcukiṟāḷ :- 
“eṉṉaik kaṉivuṭaṉ nōkki, eṉ aṇimaṉikaḷait tirutti, cantaṇakkuḻampāl eṉ tōḷkaḷil toyyil eḻutukiṟāṉē - antap peruntaṉmaiyuṭaiya iḷaiñaṉukkē, eṉ ayalār kūṟukiṟapaṭi nāṉ eṉ neñcaip paṟikoṭuttu viṭṭēṉā eṉṟu nī kēṭkiṟāyākkum? aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ nāṉ colvataik kēḷ.”
“nī kūṟukiṟapayē, kaṭaṟkaraiyilē nāṉ avaṉuṭaṉ nīṇṭa nēram tiṇṇiya kaṭal kōraikaḷaip paṟṟi iḻuttup paṟittuk koṇṭē ulavuvatuṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam paṟippatāl kaikaḷellām civantuviṭum. āṉāl itellām eṉakkāka avaṉ ceytutanta kōraip pommaikkākattāṉ. ayalārkaḷ kaṭṭiviṭukiṉṟa kataikaḷaiyellām itaṟkāka nī nampiviṭuvatu viyappukkuriya tākavē yirukkiṟatu. ēṉeṉil inta ayalārkaḷukku ūrvampu pēcātapōtu maṉa amaitiyē yiruppatillai pōlirukkiṟatu.”
“viyappārvattuṭaṉ nōkkum uṉ kaṇ pārvaiyil paṭuvataṟku añci nāṉ eṉ vīṭṭiliruntu tirumpi naṭantēṉ. avaṉ nāṉ tirumpi varuvatukaṇṭu, catuppu nilattil vaḷarntu kiṭanta cila malarkaḷaip paṟittu avaṟṟai oru mālaiyākak kaṭṭi atai eṉakkut tantāṉ. avaṉ eṉṉiṭam kāṭṭiya intac ciṟitaḷavu naṭpurimaikkākavā, ittakaiya pōlikkataikaḷuṭaṉ uṉṉai eṉṉiṭam aṉuppiyavarkaḷaik kaṇṭittuk kēṭkāmal, eṉṉiṭam kēḷvikēṭka vantirukkiṟāy?”*
“eṉ tōḷkaḷil avaṉ cantaṇakkuḻampāl karumpiṉ taṇṭiṉai aḻakākattāṉ eḻutiṉāṉ. eṉakku appaṭi eḻuta varāteṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē eḻutiṉāṉ. intac ciṟiya aṉpā taravukkākavā eṉ viḷaiyāṭṭut tōḻiyar kūṟiya cōmpēṟi vampuraikaḷukkuc cevikoṭuttuk kēṭṭuk koṇṭu eṉṉait tontaravu paṭuttukiṟāy.”52
kuṟumpaṉāṉa kaṭṭaṟṟa iḷaiñaṉoruvaṉ kaṭikātalaṉāka māṟuvataiyum avaṉaippaṟṟip peṇ taṉ tōḻiyiṭam pēcuvataiyum kīḻē kāṇkiṟōm.
“nāṉ kūṟuvataik kēḷ, tōḻi!”
“nam ciṟu vīṭukaḷai aḻittu, nam talaiyiluḷḷa mālaikaḷaik kulaittu, nām teruvilvaittu viḷaiyāṭum pantaip paṟit tōṭi namakku ellaiyillāta tollai koṭukkum antak kuṟumpuc ciṟuvaṉai nī aṟivāy allavā?”
oru nāḷ, nāṉum eṉ tāyum vīṭṭu vēlaiyilīṭu paṭṭirukkumpōtu, avaṉ vīṭṭukku vantu nīr vēṭkaiyāy irukkiṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. ‘avaṉukku oru kalattil nīr koṭu’ eṉṟu eṉ tāy ēviṉāḷ. nāṉum avaṉ kuṟumpukaḷai maṟantu oru kalan taṇṇīrkoṇṭu ceṉṟu koṭuttōṉ. avaṉō eṉṉaik kaiyaip piṭittu aṇaikka muyaṉṟāṉ. ‘aiyaiyō! inta iyaiñaṉ eṉṉa ceykiṟāṉ pār!’ eṉṟu kūviṉēṉ.
“kūkkuralaikkēṭṭu eṉ aṉṉai nāṅkaḷ niṉṟa iṭattukku ōṭi vantāḷ. nīr kuṭippatil avaṉ vikkiṉāṉ eṉṟu nāṉ orupoy aḷantēṉ. eṉ tāy avaṉ mutukait taṭavik koṭuttāḷ. appōtu ak kaḷvaṉ makaṉ eṉṉaik kuttiviṭu pavaṉpōl nōkki eṉṉaic cirikkavaittāṉ.”53
kuṉṟaṅkaḷilum mullaiccōlai nilaṅkaḷilum varum puttārvak kātal kāṭcikaḷuḷ kīḻvaruvataṉaiviṭap puttārva muṭaiya etaiyum kāṇamuṭiyātu.
“nāṉum eṉ talaiviyum putuveḷḷattāl poṅkip puraṇṭōṭiya kāṭṭāṟṟil kuḷittukkoṇṭiruntōm. āṟṟiṉ naṭu nīrōṭṭattarukē talaivi vaḻukki, ōṭṭattil nilaikoḷḷamāṭṭāmal mitantu ceṉṟāḷ. avaḷ iṭar nilaikaṇṭa mālaikaḷaṇinta oru vīra iḷavaracaṉ avaḷai ātaravāka eṭuttuk karaikku koṇṭuvantu cērttāṉ. accamayam avaḷ tiraṇṭa mārpakaṅkaḷ avaṉ tōḷkaḷmītu poruntik kiṭantaṉa eṉṟu arukiluḷḷōr kaṇṭu pēcikkoṇṭārkaḷ. itukēṭṭu eṉ talaivi, ‘iṉi nāṉ inta iḷaiñaṉiṭamē eṉ uḷḷattaip patiya viṭṭu viṭukiṟēṉ’ eṉṟu cūḷuraittāḷ. ”
“avaṉ malaṅkuṟavariṉ oru vallamaivāynta kuḻuviṉ talaivaṉ.”
“nāṉ avarkaḷukku eṭuttuk kūṟiṉēṉ. ‘kuṟavar kuṭippeṇ taṉ kātalaṉai maṟantu naṭantatum kiṭaiyātu. kuṟavar eyta ampu taṉ kuṟiyil tappuvatum kiṭaiyātu. ākavē malaṅkuṟavarākiya nīṅkaḷ marapu poyppīrkaḷāṉāl, vaḷḷikkoṭikaḷ kiḻaṅku vīḻttamāṭṭā. tēṉīkkaḷ tēṉiṟāl kaṭṭamāṭṭā. uṅkaḷ malaṅkaḻaṉikaḷ payirvaḷampeṟamāṭṭā’ eṉṟēṉ.”
“eṉ coṟkaḷkēṭṭa tāy, atait tantaiyariṭam54 kūṟiṉāḷ. avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ viruppattukkuriya maṟṟōr iḷaiñaṉai avaḷuṭaṉ maṇam puṇarvikka virumpiyiruntārkaḷ. ākavē itukēṭṭu avarkaḷ ciṉam mūṇṭatu. kaṇkaḷ aḻaṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ villum ampum tērntaṉar. orunāḷ muḻuvatum paḻicceyalkaḷilēyē avarkaḷ eṇṇam ceṉṟatu.”
“āṉāl iḷaiyōr irucārāriṭattum kuṟṟamillai eṉpataik kaṇṭapiṉ avarkaḷ kōpam taṇintatu. tam makaḷ avaḷ kātalaṉai maṇappataṟku avarkaḷ icaivaḷittaṉar.”
“itaṉpiṉ kōtta kaikaḷuṭaṉ nāṅkaḷ kuravaik kūttāṭiṉōm.”
“piṉṉar kulattiṉ mūttōr ūrp potuvil kūṭi, aṟivaṉ talaimaiyil maṇaviḻā ayara muṟpaṭṭaṉar.”55
kaṟpuneṟiniṉṟa aṭakkamikka oru naṅkaiyiṉ kātal varalāṟu mēlē tarappaṭṭatu. kātal muṉaippuṭaiya akkālattiya kuṟumpukkārap peṇ oruttiyiṉ ceyti kīḻē tarappaṭukiṟatu.
“kāyāmpū otta karuviḻi naṅkāy, kēḷ!”
“aḻakiya mālaiyaṇinta vil ēntiyavaṉāy ētō vēṭṭaiyait toṭarntu vantavaṉpōla ōr iḷaiñaṉ eṉmuṉ varuvāṉ. vantu kaṉivuṭaṉ nīṇṭanēram eṉṉai nōkkuvāṉ. āṉāl oru colkūṭac collāmal ceṉṟuviṭuvāṉ. avaṉaip paṟṟiya eṇṇam eṉ uṟakkattaiyē keṭuttatu. nāṉ uḷḷūrat tuyaraṭaintēṉ. avaṉ taṉ kātalaik kaṇṇiṉāl terivittā ṉēyaṉṟi, nāvāl uraippatillai. nāṉum peṇṇāy iruppatiṉāl nāṇam kāraṇamāka avaṉai evvaḷavu kātalikkiṟēṉ eṉṟu avaṉiṭam kūṟamuṭiyavillai.”
“maṟaivāṉa eṉ kātaliṉ tuṉpam poṟukkamāṭṭāmal nāṉ ceyta oru ceyal ippōtu niṉaittāl kūṭa nāṉ veṭkappaṭat takkatāyirukkiṟatu. oru nāḷ nāṉ nam paṇṇai yarukil ūñcalil uṭkārntukoṇṭiruntēṉ. avaṉ vaḻakkam pōl eṉ muṉ vantu niṉṟāṉ. nāṉ avaṉai aḻaittu, “aiya eṉṉai ūñcalil ciṟitu āṭṭiviṭu” eṉṟēṉ. “makiḻcciyuṭaṉ appaṭiyē ceykiṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟi avaṉ ūcalāṭṭiṉāṉ. appōtu nāṉ ūcaliliruntu naḻuvi viḻupavaḷ pōla naṭittu avaṉ tōḷkaḷmītu viḻuntēṉ.”
“avaṉ eṉṉai uṭaṉē taṉ kaikaḷāl iṟukappiṭittāṉ. nāṉum uṇarviḻantavaḷpōla avaṉ tōḷmītu kiṭantēṉ. avaṉ eṉṉai mēlum iṟuka aṇaittukkoṇṭāṉ. āṉāl nāṉ kaṇ viḻittatum kaṉivuṭaṉ taṭavikkoṭuttup pōkumpaṭi viṭṭuviṭṭāṉ. āṉāl eṉṉai ārvamākat tāṉum kātalippatai avaṉ ceyal uṟutiyākak kāṭṭiviṭṭatu.”56
paṇṭaik kātal pāṭalkaḷil oru putumai vāynta vaḻakkam kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atuvē maṉamuṟivuṟṟa kātalaṉ taṉ kātalai veḷippaṭaiyākak kūṟi naṭutteruvil taṟkolai muyaṟciyil iṟaṅkum paḻakkam ākum. oru peṇṇiṭattil nīkka muṭiyāta kātalkoṇṭu, avaḷ kātalait tāṉ peṟātu pōy viṭṭapōtu, avaṉ paṉaiyiṉ karukkumaṭṭaikaḷai eṭuttu avaṟṟaik koṇṭu kutirai uruvam oṉṟu ceyvāṉ. atil maṉikaḷum mayiliṟakukaḷum vaittu oppaṉai ceytu, kaṭivāḷam pōla oru kayiṟṟai ataṉmītu  māṭṭik koṇṭu teruvaḻikāya ataṉ mītu ūrvāṉ. ūrum pōtu taṉ kātalimītu kātaṟpāṭṭukkaḷaip pāṭikkoṇṭē celvāṉ57.
ik kāṭci kaṇṭu cila camayam kātaliyē iraṅki maṉamāṟu vatuṇṭu. allatu avaḷ uṟaviṉar avaṉai maṇam ceyyumpaṭi avaḷait tūṇṭuvar. āṉāl kātalai inta aḷavu veḷip paṭuttiyum kātali avaṉiṭam kaṉivu kāṭṭavillaiyāṉāl, avaṉ ceṅkuttāṉa kuṉṟiliruntu vīḻntu taṉ uyirai māyttuk koḷvāṉ.
ovvoru nakarilum ūrilum potumakaḷir teru oṉṟu iruntatu. periya nakaraṅkaḷil kalvi kēḷvikaḷilum kalaikaḷilum tērccipeṟṟa āṭal aṇaṅkukaḷ iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ celvamikka peruṅkuṭi makkaḷiṉ kātaṟ kiḻattiyarāyiruntaṉar. ivvāṭal naṅkaiyarukku aracaṉ taṉi matippum pātukāppum aḷittāṉ. avarkaḷ tērilum civikaikaḷilum cellavum, maṉṉaṉ pūṅkāk kaḷil ulavavum, poṉṉālāṉa veṟṟilaic celvaṅkaḷum kavarimāṉiṉ veṇmayirk kaṟṟai viciṟikaḷum vaḻaṅkavum, illaṅkaḷiliruntu veḷic cellumpōtu uṭaivāḷaṇinta meykāvalar kuḻuviṉ kāppuṭaṉ cellavum icaivaḷikkappaṭṭaṉar.58
piṟar maṉaiviyaruṭaṉ ūṭāṭuvatu peruṅkuṟṟamākak karutappaṭṭut taṇṭikkap paṭṭālum, potumakaḷirai allatu āṭal naṅkaiyarai aṇukuvatō, avarkaḷuṭaṉ pūṅkākkaḷil ulavuvatō, potu nīrāṭṭutturaikaḷil avarkaḷuṭaṉ kaḷittu viḷaiyāṭuvatō avamatippeṉṟu karutappaṭavillai.
iḷaiñarkaḷ 16-lum iḷanaṅkaiyarkaḷ 12-lum maṇap paruvameytiyavar eṉṟu karutappaṭṭaṉar59. an nāḷaiyakātaṟ pāṭalkaḷil pommaiyum viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷum tuṟakkāta paruvattilēyē peṇkaḷai iḷaiñar kātalāṭum nikaḻcci kaḷukkup pala cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭalām. ic ciṟuparuva maṇaviyait tāṉ mekastaṉis kuṟippiṭukiṟārāṉālum, ‘pāṇṭiya nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ āṟuvayatilēyē kuḻantai peṟṟu viṭukiṟārkaḷ’ eṉṟu kūṟum pōtu avar uyarvu naviṟciyilīṭupaṭukiṟār eṉpatil aiyamillai.60
maṇaviṉaikkuc cila nāṭkaḷukku muṉṉāl, aṇiyiḻai pūṇṭa peṇkaḷ yāṉai mītēṟi naṇparkaḷiṭamum uṟaviṉar kaḷiṭamum ceṉṟu tirumaṇa viḻāvukku varumpaṭi aḻaippuk kūṟiṉar. peṇ illattiṉmuṉṉē akalamāṉa uyarnta kāvaṇam amaikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ mēltaḷameṅkum pūmālaikaḷ toṅkaviṭappaṭṭaṉa. maṇaviṉaikkākak kuṟitta naṉṉāḷil, pārppaṉak kurukkaḷ vantu kāvaṇattaṭiyil tirukkaṉal ēṟṟiṉāṉ. muracum kuḻalum caṅkum iṉṉicai iyampiṉa maṇamakaṉum maṇamakaḷum tirukkaṉalaic cuṟṟi mummuṟai valam varumpaṭi iṭṭuc cellappaṭṭaṉar.
ēṟṟiya viḷakkukaḷuṭaṉ naṟumpukai, malarkaḷ, maṇap poṭikaḷ ākiyavai ēntiyavaṇṇam peṇṭir maṇamakkaḷ mītu malar tūvip pāṭalkaḷuṭaṉum vāḻttukkaḷuṭaṉum avarkaḷai maṇavaṟaikku iṭṭēkiṉar.61
nakaraṅkaḷil ēḻai vakuppiṉarum, malaṅkuṭiyiṉarum, aṉṟu pārppaṉar celvākkukku uṭpaṭavillai. ākavē tirumaṇa viṉai aṟivar allatu tamiḻp pārppaṉarkaḷālēyē nikaḻttap paṭṭatu.62
tirumaṇa mutaliya kuṭumpa viḻākkālaṅkaḷ tavira, maṟṟac camayaṅkaḷil potuvāka uṇavu nērattil kuṭumpam kūṭuvatillai. ovvoru kuṭumpa uṟuppiṉarum avaravarukku vāyppāṉa camayattil tam uṇavai uṭkoṇṭu vantaṉar.
uṇavu eppōtum eḷiya uṇavākavē iruntatu. uṇaviṉ perumpakuti ariciyē. pāl, veṇṇey, tēṉ mikap potup paṭaiyākavē vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa.
vevveṟu vakuppiṉarum uyirvāḻkkaikku uṭkoṇṭa uṇavu vakaikaḷai oru nāṭucūḻvāṇar kīḻvarumāṟu viritturaikkiṟār:-
“eyiṉar allatu vēṭar cenniṟamuṭaiya muraṭṭu ariciyuṭaṉ uṭumpiṉ iṟaicci kalantu tēkkiṉ akaṉṟa ilaiyil iṭṭup parimāṟiṉar. āyar pālil vēkavaitta cōḷamum moccaiyum, cāmaiyum, nalkiṉar. uḻavu nilaṅkaḷil toḻilāḷar kōḻiyiṟaiccip poriyaluṭaṉ veḷḷai arici uṇavu vaṭṭittamaittaṉar. kaṭaṟkaraiyil mīṉ paṭavar paṉai olaip paṭṭaikaḷil poritta mīṉuṭaṉ cōṟiṭṭaṉar.”
“pārppaṉar māṅkāy ūṟukāyuṭaṉ cōṟum, maṇamuṭaiya karuvēmpiṉ ilaiyuṭaṉ veṇṇeyil venta mātuḷam piñcum tantaṉar. uḻavar allatu vēḷāḷar iṉiya tiṉpaṇṭaṅkaḷum, palāppaḻaṅkaḷum, vāḻaippaḻaṅkaḷum tēṅkāyiṉ kuḷircci poruntiya nīrum vaḻaṅkiṉar. kaḷḷuk kaṭaikaḷil palanāḷ aricimā uṇaviṭṭuk koṭṭililēyē taṅkik koḻukka vaikkappaṭṭa āṇpaṉṟiyiṉ vaṟuvaluṭaṉ kaḷḷum vaḻaṅkiṉar.”63
toḻilāḷar, paṭaivīrar, nāṭucūḻvāṇar ākiya ēḻai vakuppiṉarkaḷāl teṉṉaiyiliruntu iṟakkiya kaḷ parukap paṭṭatu64. celva vakappiṉar arici, tātakippū65 mutaliya maṇap poruḷkaḷiliruntu vaṭikkappaṭum naṟumaṇap mūṭṭappaṭṭa tēṟal vakaiyai vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ66. yavaṉar kalaṅkaḷil koṇṭu varappaṭṭa kuḷirnaṟun tēṟal vakaikaḷ mika vilaiyēṟiyavaiyāy iruntirukkavēṇṭum. ivaiyē maṉṉariṉ viruppattukkuriya kuṭivakaikaḷāy iruntaṉa.67
kāṭaic caṇṭaikaḷ, āṭalpāṭalkaḷ, icaiyaraṅkukaḷ, camaya viḻākkaḷ ākiyavaiyē potumakkaḷiṉ talaiyāya poḻutu pōkkukaḷāka iruntaṉa. mātar vaḷḷaippāṭṭum ammāṉaip pāṭṭum pāṭumpaṭi kiḷikaḷukkuk kaṟpittum, ūñcalil ūcalāṭiyum, tāyam, kaḻaṅku, pantu mutaliyavai yāṭiyum  vīṭṭil poḻutu pōkkiṉar.
tāyam eṉpatu taṟpōkaiya cokkaṭṭāṉ āṭṭamē. āṉāl cokkaṭṭāṉkāykaḷ ippōtiruppatu pōla nīḷ catukkamāṉa kaṭṭaikaḷāy illai. karumpuḷḷiyiṭṭa naṇṭiṉ tōṭupōla vaṭṭavaṭivamāyiruntaṉa. kaḻaṅkāṭalil ovvoṉṟum orupākkaḷavāṉa ēḻu ciṉṉañ ciṟu kaḻalkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. āṭuvōr uṭkārn tiruntapaṭiyē kaḻalkaḷai oṉṟoṉṟāka, iraṇṭiraṇṭāka eṉa ēṟṟi ēḻēḻvarai mēlē eṟintu viḻātu uḷḷaṅkaiyilō, puṟaṅkaiyilō tāṅka muyaṉṟaṉar. pantāṭ ṭattil ikāyal toṭarntu aṭitta vaṇṇam pantucellum ticaiyil muṉṉākavō, piṉṉākavō, cuḻaṉṟō ceṉṟāṭiṉar.68
paṇṭait tamiḻariṭaiyē kaviṉ kalaikaḷ mika uyarnta nilaiyil vaḷarccipeṟṟiruntaṉa.
potuk kalvimuṟaiyil icaittuṟai ōr iṉṟiyamaiyāk kūṟāy iruntatu. uḷḷattil meṉmaiyum iṇakka nayamum vāynata uṇarccikaḷai eḻuppum iṉimaiyāṉa paṇkaḷil tamiḻar mēmpaṭṭiruntaṉar. icaippaṭṭiyil ēḻu karaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. avai tāram, uḷai, kural, iṉi, tuttam, viḷari, kaikkiḷai eṉpaṉa69. mukkiyamāṉa paṇkaḷ allatu icaikkuralkaḷ nāṉku. avai pālai, kuṟiñci, marutam, cevvaḻi eṉpaṉa70. ovvoru paṇṇilum pala kiḷait tiripukaḷ iruntaṉa. avaiyaṉaittum cērntu 103 taṉippaṇ kūṟukaḷ71 annāḷaiya icai nūlkaḷil vakuttuṇarappaṭṭaṉa.
icaiyiṉ pakutikaḷākiya tāḻkural, iṭaik kural, uccak kural ākiyavai aṟiyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. āṉāl avai oruṅkē pāṭappeṟavillai.72 oṉṟaṉpiṉ oṉṟākap pāṭap paṭṭaṉa.
vaḷikkaruvikaḷil (tuḷaik karuvikaḷil) palvēṟu vakaip paṭṭavai vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. āḻnta muḻakkamuṭaiya caṅkukaḷ kōyilkaḷilum, tirumaṇa viḻākkaḷilum, cāviḻākkaḷilum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. pullāṅkuḻalil eṭṭut tuḷaikaḷ iruntaṉa. atu palvakai icainayaṅkaḷai veḷippaṭuttat takkatāyiruntatu.
orumuka muracamum, irumuka muracamum, palvēṟuvakai ūtu kompukaḷum, ovvor icaiyaraṅkilum, kuḻaluṭaṉ iṇaintaṉa.
yāḻil nāṉku vakaikaḷ iruntaṉa. mikac ciṟitum potuvāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatum ēḻu narampukaḷuṭaitāyiruntatu.73 maṟṟa yāḻ vakaikaḷ muṟaiyē patiṉāṉku, patiṉēḻu, irupattoru narampukaḷ uṭaiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa74. vācikkappaṭum pōtu, yāḻ iṭatu kaiyil paṟṟappaṭṭatu. ataṉ nāṉku viralkaḷ muṟukkāṇikaḷiṉ mītu paṭintaṉa. narampukaḷ peruviral tavira valatu kaiyil maṟṟa viralkaḷil ētēṉum oṉṟāl uḷarappaṭṭaṉa.75
tamiḻ icaiyum, āriya icaiyum, taṉittaṉiyākak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai oṉṟuṭaṉoṉṟu toṭarpaṟṟa iru vēṟumuṟaikaḷ eṉpataiyum; icaiyil muḻuniṟaivupeṟa virumpu kiṟavarkaḷ iraṇṭaiyum kaṟṟaṉar eṉpataiyum itu kāṭṭukiṉṟatu.
āṭalum oru kaviṉ kalaiyākap payilappaṭṭatu. akkālattil vaḻakkilirunta palavakai āṭalkaḷukkum vitikaḷ vakuppaṭṭiruntatuṭaṉ, akkalaikkuriya ilakṇa nūlkaḷ    ēṟkeṉavē amaintiruntaṉa. ivai iṉṟu kiṭṭavillai. āṉāl āṭalaippaṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷ cilappatikāratti liruntu teriyavarukiṉṟaṉa. innūliliruntē āriyak kūttu, tamiḻkkūttu eṉṟu iruvakaiyāṉa āṭalvakaikaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu aṟikiṟōm.
āriyak kūttil patiṉoru vakaikaḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai kīḻvarum nikaḻccikaḷaic cittarittaṉa76 :-
(1)civaperumāṉ tiripuram erittal.
(2)tiripurattukkuc cella nāṉmukaṉ koṇarnta tēril civaperumāṉ ēṟutal.
(3)kañcaṉāl aṉuppappaṭṭa yāṉaiyaik kaṇṇaṉ aḻittal.
(4)kaṇṇaṉ pāṇācuraṉuṭaṉ maṟpōriṭṭu avaṉaik kollutal.
(5)kantaṉ ilaṅkaiyil acuraṉai muṟiyaṭittal.
(6)kantaṉ acurar paṭaiyuṭaṉ pōriṭal.
(7)kaṇṇaṉ pāṇācuraṉ kōṭṭaiyai aḻittal.
(8)kāmatēvaṉ aliyuruvil taṉ makaṉ aniruttaṉaik kāppāṟṟap pāṇācuraṉ kōṭṭaiyil pukal.
(9)koṟṟavai acurarkaḷai aḻittal.
(10)tirumakaḷ acurarai ēmāṟṟal.
(11)intiraṉ, maṉaivi ayirāṇi pāṇācuraṉ kōṭṭai yiṉ vaṭakku vāyilmuṉ tōṉṟuvatu.
tamiḻk kūttukkaḷil palavakaikaḷ iruntaṉa. cila teyvaṅ kaḷaiyō, aracaraiyō, avarkaḷ vīrac ceyalkaḷaiyō pukaḻntaṉa. cila maṉitaraiyō, vilaṅkukaḷaiyō avinayittaṉa. cila kātaṟ kāṭcikaḷaic cittarittaṉa. ivaiyaṉṟik kuravaik kūttum makkaḷ virumpiya āṭal vakaiyāy iruntatu. itil ēḻu, eṭṭu allatu oṉpatupēr vaḷaiyamāka oruvaruṭa ṉoruvar kaikōttu āṭuvar. ivvāṭalil āṭavarum peṇṭirum kalantukoṇṭaṉar. ataṉuṭaṉ kātal allatu pōrpaṟṟiya pāṭalkaḷ iṇaikkappaṭṭaṉa.77
ikkūttukkaḷil palavum āṭalaṇaṅkukaḷ allatu kūttiyarāl nikaḻttappaṭṭaṉa.
āṭalaṇaṅkiṉ kalaippayiṟci mika iḷamaiyil aintu vayatilēyē toṭaṅkappaṭṭu ēḻāṇṭukaḷ toṭarntu naṭaipeṟṟatu.
avaḷ payiṟcippāṭat tiṭṭaṅkaḷ iṉṟu kalaippayiṟci niṟaivuṭaiya naṅkaikkukkūṭap perumaitarattakkatākum.
āṭavum, pāṭavum, meṉṉaya naṭaiyuṭaiya paḻakkaṅkaḷ payilavum avaḷ kaṟpikkappaṭṭāḷ. itallāmalum yāḻum kuḻalum muḻavum kaiyāḷavum, ayalmoḻikaḷil iyaṟṟappaṭṭa pāṭalkaḷaip pāṭavum avarkaḷ paḻakiṉar. ōviyam varaital, nīrviḷaiyāṭṭu, pakaṭṭāṉa palvaṇṇat tūḷkaḷāl mēṉi oppaṉai ceytal, aḻakiya pūmālaikaḷ toṭuttal, pūṇaṇi maṇipuṉaital, paḷḷiyaṟai ciṅkārittal, kālakkaṇip puṇartal, paruvattiṟkēṟṟa ceyal takuti yuṇartal, palvēṟu kalai iyal tuṟaikaḷiṉ ceytikaḷpaṟṟi vācittal, putirkaḷ pōṭutal, putirkaḷ viṭuttal, piṟar karuttukkaḷai uyttuṇarntu kūṟal ākiya ittaṉaiyum, avarkaḷ muḻu niṟaipayiṟciyiṉ kūṟukaḷāy iruntaṉa.78
curukkamākak kūṟuvatāṉāl āṭavarkaḷukkup poḻutu pōkkum iṉpakkaḷippum taruvaṉa eṉṟō, avarkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷil viyappārvam, kavarcci ākiyavaṟṟai ūṭṭuvaṉa eṉṟō karutattakka yāvaṟṟaiyum āṭalaṇaṅku payiṉṟāḷ.
avaḷatu paṉṉiraṇṭām vayatil, aracaṉ nakarp perumakkaḷ ākiyōr muṉṉilaiyil avaḷ araṅka vāḻvil aṭi eṭuttu vaittāḷ.
camayattukkēṟpa uṭaṉaṭip pāṭal pāṭavalla oru kavitai yāciriyaṉum, akkaviñaṉ pāṭalukku uṭaṉaṭiyākap paṇṇa maittup pāṭavalla icaiyāciriyaṉum, oru yāḻāciriyaṉum, oru kuḻalāciriyaṉum, oru taṇṇumaiyāciriyaṉum āṭalaṇaṅ kuṭaṉ eppōtum uṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ ovvoruvarumē avaravar tuṟaikaḷil tuṟaipōkap payiṟcipeṟṟavarā yiruntaṉar.
araṅkamēṭai iraṇṭaṭi uyaramum patiṉāṉkaṭi akalamum patiṉāṟaṭi nīḷamum uṭaiyatāyiruntatu. mēṭaiyil ēḻaṭi uyarattil tūṇkaḷiṉ ātārattiṉmēl oru mēṟkaṭṭi iṭap paṭṭiruntatu. im mēṟkaṭṭiyiṉ mēl kāval teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ōviyan tīṭṭappaṭṭa eḻiṉikaḷ (tiraikaḷ) araṅka mēṭaiyiṉ nāṟpuṟamum  toṅkaviṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa.
āṭṭam viḷakkuvaikkum nērattilēyē toṭaṅkap paṭṭatu. araṅkamēṭai muḻutum oḷimayamākkappaṭṭatu.
mēṭaiyiṉ akalattil āṟaṭi āṭalaṇaṅkiṉ āṭṭattuk keṉa otukkappaṭṭiruntatu. avaḷukkuppiṉ avaḷ āṭṭattiṟku vēṇṭiya tūṇṭutalkaḷ tara iraṇṭu mūṉṟu mutiya āṭalaṇaṅkukaḷ niṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷukkuppiṉ āṭalaṇaṅkaḷukku ōyvukoṭuttu makkaḷaik kaḷippikkum muṟaiyil iṭai nēraṅkaḷil pāṭavalla cila pāṭakar iruntaṉar.
taṉ ciṟappaṇimaṇiyuṭaṉ āṭalaṇaṅku araṅkattil ēṟiṉāḷ. viḷakkoḷiyil meyyākavē cuṭariṭṭoḷi vīcum nilaiyil, merukiṭappaṭṭa poṉṉum, paṭṭai tīrnta maṇikaḷum nirampiya avaḷ aṇimaṇikaḷiliruntu āyira oḷikkatirikaḷ nāṟpuṟamum vīcuvatu pōṉṟiruntaṉa.
āṭalait toṭaṅkukaiyil āṭalnaṅkai taṉ veḷḷi maṇik kuralil taṉṉaik tīṅkukaḷiliruntu kāttu aruḷ pālikkumpaṭi teyvaṅkaḷai vaṇaṅkit teyvavāḻttup pāṭiṉāḷ. ataṉpiṉ muṉpē tērnteṭuttut tiṭṭam ceyyappaṭṭa āṭalpāṭal kaḷai ovvoṉṟāka āṭiṉāḷ.
avaḷ uṭalum, uṭaluṟuppukaḷum maṉita uṭal koḷḷattakum aḻakiya palavakaip pāvaṅkaḷum tōṟṟa ellā vaḷaivu, neḷivu kuḻaikaḷum kāṭṭiṉa. avaḷ iḷamaiyum, eḻilum, avaḷ māya icaiyum maruṭciyūṭṭum iṉṉaya nilai kaḷum, vaṇṇa oḷi viḷakkaṅkaḷum iṉṉicai yalaikaḷum kāṇpōraik kaḷimayakkattil āḻttiṉa.
aracaṉ avaḷukku āyiratteṭṭup poṉ kācu peṟumāṉamuḷḷa poṉmālaiyai attakaiya taṟuvāykaḷil paricāka aḷittāṉ.79
ōviyam, ciṟpam ākiya kalaikaḷilum, tamiḻar pōtiya aḷavu, uyartērcci peṟṟiruntaṉar. teyvaṅkaḷ, maṉitar, vilaṅku vaṭivaṅkaḷ, taṉimaṉitar illaṅkaḷ, kōyil, araṇmaṉai pōṉṟa potuviṭaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ matilkaḷ palvēṟu vaṇṇaṅkaḷāl tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.80 paṭukkaiyaṟait tiraikaḷilum nāṭaka araṅkattiraikaḷilum yāḻ mutaliya icaikkaruvikaḷuk kuriya tuṇi uṟaikaḷilum pūkkaḷum pūṅkoṭikaḷum meyvaṭivuṟa aḻakāka varaiyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.81
marattālum melliya kiṭaiyālum mika aḻakiya patumaikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa82. kōyilkaḷilum maṭaṅkaḷilum tēva tēviyar uruvaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. taṅkaḷ vēṇṭukōḷ kaḷaic cevimaṭuttu varamaḷikkum āṟṟal oviyar kaittiṟattāl appatumaikaḷukku aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu mūṭa nampikkai yuṭaiya potumakkaḷ eḷitil nampiṉar. anta aḷavukku mika nērttiyāṉa muṟaiyil avai uyirttōṟṟam uṭaiyavaiyāy mey vaṇṇam tīṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa. āyiṉum kal, ulōkam mutaliya uṟutivāynta poruḷkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷ, cilaikaḷ paṟṟiya kuṟippu etuvum annāḷaiya ilakkiyattil illai.
7 allatu 8-m nūṟṟāṇṭil kaṭṭamaikkappaṭṭa māmallapuram ciṟpaṅkaḷaiviṭap paḻamaivāynta entac ciṟpak kalaic ciṉṉamum tamiḻakattil kiṭaikkāmaliruppataṉ kāraṇattai itu viḷakkukiṟatu.
ēḻai eḷiya vakuppiṉariṉ vīṭukaḷ kaḷimaṇṇāl kaṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. kūraikaḷ pul vēyappaṭṭaṉa. allatu teṉṉai ōlaiyō paṉai ōlaiyō pāvappaṭṭaṉa. cuvarkaḷil cemmaṇ pūcappaṭṭatu. nakaraṅkaḷiluḷḷa vīṭukaḷ perumpālum ceṅkalāl kaṭṭappaṭṭu ōṭaṭukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. cuvarkaḷ cuṇṇāmpu tīṟṟappeṟṟaṉa. māṉ kaṇ pōla vaṭivamuṭaiya palakaṇikaḷ vaḻiyāka uḷḷaṟaikaḷukkuḷ oḷiyum kāṟṟum pukuntaṉa.
vāyil mukappu allatu pūmukam eppōtum vīṭṭiṉ oru muṉaippāṉa pakutiyāy amaintiruntatu. atil ēṟuvataṟkut teruviliruntu paṭivaricakaiḷ iṭappaṭṭu, irupuṟamum oṭuttiṇṇaikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟilēyē varukaiyāḷar allatu vīṭṭu makkaḷ ōyvu nēraṅkaḷil taṅki yiruntaṉar.83 vāyil mukappup potuvākak kaṭṭaṭattiliruntu vilakiyiruntatu. iṭaiyēyuḷḷa muṟṟattil vīṭṭukkuriya vaṉuṭaiya āṭu māṭukaḷ mutaliya kālnaṭaikaḷukkut toḻukkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
celvar māḷikaikaḷukkuk kāraikkaṭṭu mettaikaḷ, kūṭakōpuraṅkaḷ, vēṉiṟkālattukkum maḻaikālattukkum, vēṟu vēṟāṉa taṉippaṭukkaiyaṟaikaḷ ākiyavai iruntaṉa. uyar irukkaikaḷ, kaṭṭilkaḷ, mēṟkaṭṭikaḷ, aṉṉattiṉ tūvi niṟainta mettaikaḷ, cilaiyuruvaṅkaḷāl ēntappaṭṭa viḷakkukaḷ, peṇṭir kuḻantaikaḷ iṉpap poḻutu pōkkuk kāṉa ūñcalkaḷ mutaliya palvēṟuvakaippaṭṭa taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum vaḻakkattiliruntaṉa.
kōyilkaḷum araṇmaṉaikaḷumkūṭac ceṅkalālēyē kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. akkāla ilakkiyattil kal kaṭṭaṭamāka etuvum kuṟippiṭappaṭavillai. oruvēḷai periya kōṭṭaikaḷiṉ matilkaḷum, ñāyilkaḷum kōpuraṅkaḷum maṭṭum araikuṟai vēlaippāṭuṭaiya kaṟkaḷum, nīṟum koṇṭu kaṭṭap paṭṭirukkakkūṭum.
kiṭṭat taṭṭa ellā nakarkaḷum, ūrkaḷum pakaivar kaḷiṭamiruntum tiruṭarkaḷiṭamiruntum pātukāppāka araṇ ceyyappaṭṭatu. ovvoru ūrum muḷmaraṅkaḷiṉ ceṟinta vēliyāl kākkappaṭṭiruntatu. ovvoru nakarattaic cuṟṟilam oru matilum akaḻiyum, akaḻiyaic cuṟṟilum mikunta akalamuṭaiya muḷmarakkāṭṭu vaḷaiyamum iruntaṉa. maturai. karūr, kāñci ākiya peru nakaraṅkaḷiṉ kōṭṭaikaḷiṉ ñāyilkaḷil muṟṟukai yiṭupavarkaḷmītu ampukaḷ vīcavum, kaṟkaḷ eṟiyavumvalla poṟikaḷ iruntaṉa.
matilarukē varuvōraip paṟṟi iḻuttuk kollat takka tūṇṭil muṉpōṉṟa poṟikaḷum, irumpu viralkaḷāl paṟṟik kiḻikkum poṟikaḷum iruntaṉa. tavira, matilēṟivara muyalpavar kaḷmītu kotikkum neyyaiyum, urukkiya īyattaiyum koṭṭavalla koti ulaikaḷ pēṇappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷaik kuttit taḷḷuvataṟ kuriya nīṇṭa īṭṭikaḷum, vēl kampukaḷum iruntaṉa.84 kōṭṭaiyiṉ paṭaikkalaccālaiyil uṭaivāḷkaḷ, vāḷkaḷ, vil amputtūṇi niraikaḷ, kēṭayaṅkaḷ, mārpuk kavacaṅkaḷ, tērkaḷ, kutiraic cēṇaṅkaḷ, yāṉaip paṭāṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa cēmittu vaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
kōṭṭai muṟṟukaivakaiyil tamiḻar piṉpaṟṟiya muṟaip paṭi, mutalil kōṭṭaicūḻnta kāṭṭil oru perum piḷavu ceytu vaḻi uṇṭupaṇṇappaṭum. ataṉpiṉ akaḻi tūrttup paṭaikaḷ matilai aṇūka iṭam uṇṭupaṇṇappaṭum. iṟutiyil ēṇikaḷ mūlam matil ēṟi uṭcellavō allatu araṇ takarkkumpaṭi payiṟcitarappaṭṭa yāṉaikaḷ mūlam vāyilkaḷai muṟikkavō muyaṟcikaḷ ceyyappaṭum.
pōr aṇi vakuttu niṟuttappaṭṭapōtu, yāṉaikaḷ muṉṉaṇiyilum, avaṟṟiṉpiṉ tērkaḷum, kutirai vīrarum, iṟutiyilēyē kālāṭpaṭaiyiṉarum niṉṟaṉar. potumuṟaik kālāḷvīrar iṭatukaiyil māṭṭut tōlāl ceyyappaṭṭa periya kēṭayaṅkaḷum, valatu kaiyil īṭṭi allatu pōrk kōṭarikaḷum tāṅkiṉar. villavar iṭatukaiyil nīṇṭa villum, tōḷil toṅkalāka amputtūṇiyum tāṅkiṉar. tavira, īṭṭi vīrar, villavar ākiya irutiṟattārum iraṇṭumuḻa nīḷamuḷḷa akaṉṟavāḷ aṇintiruntaṉar.
kālāḷkaḷ tāṅkiyataiviṭak kutiraivīrar kuṟainta paḷuvuṭaiya paṭaikkalaṅkaḷum ciṟiya kēṭayaṅkaḷum tāṅkiṉar. paṭaikaḷai naṭattiya talaivarkaḷ tērilum yāṉaikaḷilum ēṟic ceṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ eppōtum uyarkuṭiyāḷarāy iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ īṭṭiyum vāḷum ēntiṉar. poṉ vēlaippāṭuṭaiya kēṭayam tāṅkiṉar. iruppuk kavacam aṇintaṉar. pōrukkuriya tērkaḷ paḷuvaṟṟaṉavāy, uyaram kuṟainta iraṇṭu uruḷai kaḷiṉ mītu ēṟṟappaṭṭiruntaṉa. tēriṉ taṭṭaic cuṟṟilum muraṭṭuppāy vēyntiruntatu. ataṉuḷ pākaṉ vīraṉ ākiya iruvarukku maṭṭumē iṭam iruntaṉa.
ovvoru tērum iraṇṭu kutiraikaḷāl iḻukkap paṭṭatu nāṉku allatu ēḻu kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭiya peruntērkaḷ ūrvalaṅ kaḷilum viḻākkaḷilum tāṉ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
etirikaḷait tākkumpōtu, villāḷikaḷ ampukaḷai etirikaḷiṉ yāṉaikaḷiṉmītum, avaṟṟiṉ mītu ivarnta talaivarkaḷ mītum vīciṉar. villāḷikaḷ kaitērntavar kaḷākavum, peruntiraḷākavum iruntāl, mutal ampu māriyilēyē etiri yāṉaip paṭaiyai maruṇṭōṭa vaittu avarkaḷiṭaiyē kuḻappam uṇṭu paṇṇuvar. āṉāl yāṉaikaḷ tākkutalai etirttu niṉṟu viṭṭāl, kutirai vīrarkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ mītu vēlkaḷālum, kālāḷ vīrar nīṇṭa īṭṭikaḷālum tākkuvar. itaṉpiṉ neruṅkiya kaikalappil avarkaḷ etirikaḷiṉ vāṭpaṭai, pōrkkōṭarip paṭai kaḷuṭaṉ maṟpōriṭuvar.
yāṉaip paṭaiyē oru paṭaiyiṉ mikap periya valimai vāynta pakutiyāyiruntatu. ākavētāṉ oru vīraraiyō mikap pala vīrarkaḷaiyō koṉṟu vīḻttuvataiviṭa oru yāṉaiyaik kolvatō, pōrukkatavātapaṭi ceyvatō mikapperiya aruñceyalākak karutappaṭṭatu.
tamiḻariṭaiyē paṭaivīrar vakuppaic cārnta pōrvīrar tam maṉṉariṭam mikavum uṇmaip paṟṟuṭaiyavarāyiruntaṉar. oru pulavar tam talaivariṭam ōr iḷavīraṉaic cuṭṭikkāṭṭik kūṟuvatāvatu: “aḻakiya tērum yāṉaip paṭaiyum koṇṭa vēntē! nīr parukum kaḷḷai inta iḷaiñaṉuṭaṉ paṅkiṭṭup parukuvīrāka. ēṉeṉil ivaṉ tantai koṭumpōril um tantaiyiṉ tantai uyiraik kākkat taṉ uyirai viṭṭāṉ. um pakaivar tam īṭṭikaḷai ummai nōkki vīcumpōtu vīramikka ivviḷaiñaṉ etirikaḷukkum umakkum iṭainiṉṟu pōr ceyvāṉ.” eṉṟu kūṟiṉār.85
maṟavariṭamiruntu itē vīram maṟakkulap peṇṭiriṭamum karukkoṇṭiruntatu. oru pulavar kūṟṟu itai viḷakkukiṟatu.
“ā, eṉṉē ivaḷ tuṇiccal! maṟavar kulattukkut takuti vāynta tāyē ivaḷ! nēṟṟu muṉṉāḷ tāṉ ivaḷ tantai pōrkkaḷattil oru yāṉaiyai vīḻttit tāṉum kaṭum pōr ceytu vīḻntāṉ: nēṟṟu ivaḷ kaṇavaṉ oru yāṉaip paṭai muḻuvataiyum kulaittāṉ. āyiṉum iṉṟu pōrmuracu kēṭṭa vuṭaṉ avaṉ niṟai makiḻcci koṇṭuḷḷāḷ. avaḷ taṉ makaṉukku veḷḷāṭai uṭuttut talaivīci, kaiyil īṭṭi tantu kaḷattukku aṉuppukiṟāḷ.”86
iṉṉoru maṟakkulattāypaṟṟi maṟṟum oru pulavar viritturaikkiṟār.
“naṭuṅkum uṭalum vaṟṟiya kaikaḷum uṭaiyavaḷ tāṉ ivaḷ! āṉāl makaṉ pōrkkaḷattiliruntu ōṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu kēṭṭatumē, avaṉ uṇmaiyilēyē puṟaṅkāṭṭiyiruntāl, avaṉukkup pālūṭṭiya mārpakaṅkaḷai veṭṭi eṟivēṉ eṉṟa cūḷurai kūṟikkoṇṭu, kaiyil vāḷēntik kaḷam ceṉṟāḷ. kuṟṟuyirākak kiṭanta makaṉuṭal kaṇṭu, avaṉaip peṟṟapoḻutu aṭainta makiḻvaiviṭak kaḻi makiḻveytiṉāḷ.”87
pōril iṟantāl vīrarkaḷukkeṉa vāṉulakil otukkap paṭṭirunta taṉiyiṭattukkut taṅkaḷ uyirkaḷ cellum eṉṟu vīrarkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ. immūṭa nampikkaiyil avarkaḷuk kirunta uṟuti kāraṇamāka, tammaṉṉar cēvaiyil uyirviṭa avarkaḷ ciṟitum tayaṅkavillai. maṉṉarkaḷukkum itē mūṭanampikkai iruntatu. pōril mikak kaṭumpuṇpaṭṭu vīḻnta pōtum cari, mutukaiyālō, nōyālō iṟakka nērntapōtum cari, avarkaḷ pārppaṉar parappiya tiruvārnta aṟukiṉmītu kiṭattappaṭṭu, vīra māḷvupeṟumpaṭi vāḷāl piḷakkappaṭṭu iṟappataiyē virumpiṉar.88
aṇṭaiyayalāruṭaṉ ōyātu naṭaipeṟṟa pōrkaḷ paṭaivīrarukku iṭaiviṭāp payiṟci tantu avarkaḷ vīra uṇarvai vaḷarttuppēṇiṉa. maṉṉar āyaccuṟṟattiṉ oru pakutiyāṉa pāṇarum, pulavarum avarkaḷiṭaiyē vīrap pukaḻārvattai uṇṭupaṇṇavum atai vaḷarkkavum mikap peritum utaviṉar. amaitik kālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ maṉṉaṉukkum avaṉ vīrarkaḷukkum avarkaḷ muṉṉōrkaḷiṉ vīrac ceyalkaḷ paṟṟiya kātaikaḷ kūṟi makiḻvittaṉar. pōrkkālaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ paṭaiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu mūtātaiyariṉ vīrac ceyalkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟam paṭi tam pōrp pāṭalkaḷāl vīrarkaḷaiyum talaivarkaḷaiyum ūkkiṉar.
tamiḻppāṭalkaḷ kūṟukiṟapaṭi pulavarkaḷil nāṉku vakuppiṉar iruntaṉar. avarkaḷē pāṇar, kūttar, porunar, viṟaliyar eṉpavarkaḷ.
pāṇarkaḷ mikat tārnta vakuppiṉar. avarkaḷ nakarp puṟaṅkaḷil potumakaḷiruṭaṉ vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ nakarukkunakar ceṉṟapōtum avarkaḷ maṉaiviyarum piḷḷai  kaḷum avarkaḷuṭaṉ uṇavukkalamum, camayaṟkalaṅkaḷum, tūkkik koṇṭu puṟappaṭṭaṉar. kūttarkaḷ naṭikarkaḷ āvar. avarkaḷ āṭiyavai  muṟaiyāṉa nāṭakaṅkaḷ alla. icai nāṭakamuṟai cārntavai. atai avarkaḷ pāṭiyē naṭittaṉar.
porunar allatu pōrppāṭakar maṉṉaṉ allatu talaivaṉ paṭaiyiṉ oru pakutiyē yāvar. avarkaḷ oru ciṟu muracu ēntic ceṉṟaṉar. atil oru kuṟuṅkōl kaṭṭap paṭṭiruntatu. ataṉutaviyāl avarkaḷ muracaṟaintaṉar.
cōḻa aracaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷiyaippāṭum pulavar kōvūr kiḻār kūṟuvatāvatu : “eṉ muracil kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum kuṇil koṇṭu nāṉ muracaiyaṭikkum ovvoru taṭavaiyum, uṉpakaivar naṭuṅkukiṉṟaṉar” eṉṟu avar kuṟikkiṟār. itiliruntu porunaṉ muracu aṟaiyum pōtellām, atu paṭaiyeṭuttuc cellutaṟku oru aṟikuṟi eṉṟu terikiṟatu.89
iv vakuppiṉ peṇpulavar viṟaliyar eṉap paṭṭaṉar.
appaṇṭaik kālattiya ilakkiyattiṉ oru perum pakutikkuriya āciriyarkaḷ intap pōrppāṭakarēyāvar.
akkālap pulavarkaḷ viṭṭucceṉṟa pāṭalkaḷiliruntu namakku maturaimā nakariṉ oru viḷakkamāṉa muḻu uyir ōviyam kiṭaittuḷḷatu. anta oviyamē tamiḻar camutāya vāḻvu kuṟitta inta iyaliṉ niṟaimuṭivākak kūṟattakkatāyamaikiṉṟatu.90
“viṭiya oru yāmattukku muṉpē, pārppaṉa iḷamāṇa varkaḷ maṟaivācakaṅkaḷai ōtattoṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. icaiva lārkaḷ tam yāḻai mīṭṭi atil pāṭaṟpayiṟci toṭaṅku kiṉṟaṉar. appa vāṇikar eḻuntatum kaṭaikaḷiṉ nilattaḷattait tuppuravākka muṉaikiṉṟaṉar. kaḷviṉaiñar munti varukiṉṟa tam vāṭikkaikkārarai eṇṇik kaṭaikaḷait tiṟakkiṉṟaṉar.”91
“pāṇar tam kālai vāḻttukkaḷaip pāṭiyavaṇṇam valam varukiṉṟaṉar. kōyilkaḷilum, tuṟaviyar maṭaṅkaḷilum, pāṇṭiyaṉ araṇmaṉaiyilum caṅkukaḷ muḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. iṭiyōcaipōla muracukaḷ iyampukiṉṟaṉa92. kālaik katiravaṉ iḷaṅkatirkaḷ kōṭṭaiyiṉ kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ uccikaḷukkup poṉmulām pūci nakariṉ pāriya nīḷa akalaṅkaḷai eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.”
“peru vītikaḷ āṟukaḷpōṉṟa nīḷa akalamuṭaiyaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ irumaruṅkilum uḷḷa uyariya kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ perum pālum mēlmāṭikaḷaiyum pala māṭip palakaṇikaḷaiyum uṭaiya vaiyāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.”93
“ovvoru kōyililum oru koṭi paṟakkiṟatu. ovvoru kaḷ vilaiyakattilum oru kaḷitukil āṭukiṉṟatu. ovvoru vāṇikakkaḷattiṉ paṇṭacālaiyilum oru taṉikkoṭi tikaḻkiṉṟatu. ataṉuṭaṉ aracaṉ paṭaikaḷpeṟṟa ovvoru veṟṟi kuṟittum ovvoṉṟākap palvaṇṇat tukiṟkoṭikaḷ alaivīcukiṉṟaṉa. ikkoṭikaḷiṉ tiraḷum pala vaṇṇaṅkaḷum cērntu, nakaram oru viḻāvukkāka aṇiceyyappaṭṭatō eṉṟa tōṟṟam aḷikkiṟatu.”
“pakaip pulaṅkaḷil cūṟaiyāṭiya maṉṉaṉ paṭaip pirivukaḷ nakarukku vantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. kutiraikaḷ, yāṉaikaḷ, muṟṟukaiyiṭṭuveṉṟa kōṭṭaikaḷiṉ aṇimaṇivāyikaḷ pōṉṟa veṟṟic ciṉṉaṅkaḷai avarkaḷ koṇarkiṉṟaṉar. vēṟucila paṭaip pirivukaḷ iravil eriyūṭṭip pakaivar ūrkaḷi liruntu kavarnta āṉiraikaḷai, īṭṭikaḷaiyē kōlākakkoṇṭu turattikkoṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. maṉṉaṉukkuk kāṇikkaiyākat tarak kuṭi maṉṉar paricukaḷuṭaṉ avarkaḷaip piṉtoṭarntu varukiṉṟaṉar.”
“ciṟitu nērattukkuḷ utirip pūkkaḷum mālaikaḷum niṟainta kūṭaikaḷai ēntiya pūvāṇikar, maṇattūḷ, pākku veṟṟilai ākiyavaṟṟiṉ viṟpaṉaiyāḷar terukkaḷil ulaviyum uyar māṭaṅkaḷiṉ niḻalil otuṅki iḷaippāṟiyum kāṭci tarukiṉṟaṉar cuvai tarum iṉpaṇṭaṅkaḷum iḷamaṇa malarkaḷum koṇṭu cellum mutiya peṇṭir vāyiltōṟum ēṟi iṟaṅki vīṭṭi luḷḷavarkaḷiṭam poruḷkaḷai viṟpaṉai ceykiṉṟaṉar.”
“celva vakuppiṉar kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭiya tērkaḷil allatu taṉippayiṟcipeṟṟa kutinaṭaipōṭum pōrk kutiraikaḷ mītu celkiṉṟaṉar.”
“avvappōtu oru matayāṉai taṉ caṅkilip piṇippai aṟuttukkoṇṭu kāvalaraik koṉṟu vītivaḻiyē pāyntōṭu kiṉṟatu. atukaṇṭu ēḻai viṟpaṉaiyāḷarum ciṟu vaṇikarum accamum kiliyum koṇṭu aṅkumiṅkum paraparappuṭaṉ ōṭukiṉṟaṉar. yāṉaiyiṉ muṉṉum piṉṉum kāvalmakkaḷ ōṭukiṉṟaṉar. teruviluḷḷa makkaḷai eccarikkum paṭi avarkaḷ caṅkukaḷai muḻakkikkoṇṭu celkiṉṟaṉar.”
“avvappōtu kuṭittu veṟikoṇṭa pōrvīrar teruviṉ amaiti keṭukkiṉṟaṉar.”
“akaṉṟa catukkaṅkaḷil amaintuḷḷa cantaikaḷil pal vakaipporuḷkaḷum viṟpaṉaikku aṭukkiyuḷḷaṉa.”94
“vaṇṭikaḷ, tērkaḷ, tēriṉ aṇimukaṭukaḷ; vāḷāl tuṇikkamuṭiyāta kavacaṅkaḷ, iruppu araik kackaikaḷ; tōl mitiyaṭikaḷ, kavariyiṉ veḷḷiya vāl mayirāl ceyta viciṟikaḷ; yāṉai ūkkum aṅkucaṅkaḷ, mukapaṭāttiṉ kāppuk kēṭayaṅkaḷ; cempu pittaḷaiyāl ceyta palavakai uruppaṭikaḷ; aḻakiya pū mālaikaḷ, pūcucāntam, pukaiccāntam mutaliya collutaṟkariya palavakaip poruḷkaḷ accantaikaḷil koṭṭappaṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.”
“aṇimaṇikārar vītiyil annāḷaiya vaṇikarkaḷē vakaiyaṟintirunta nālvakai vayiraṅkaḷ, marakataṅkaḷ, māṇikkaṅkaḷ, nīlaṅkaḷ, puṭparākaṅkaḷ, vaiṭūriyaṅkaḷ, kōmētakaṅkaḷ, oḷitarum muttukkaḷ, cempavaḷa maṇikaḷ ākiyavai viṟkappaṭṭaṉa.”
poṉ vāṇikar oru taṉi vītiyil vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ cātarūpam kiḷicciṟai, āṭakam, cāmpūnatam eṉṟa peyarkaḷaik koṇṭa nālvakaippaṭṭa taṅkattaiyum viṟṟaṉar.
“paruttiyālum kampaḷiyālum paṭṭālum, iyaṉṟu pal vaṇṇamum palvakaip paṭivaṅkaḷum koṇṭa āṭaikaḷ pakaṭṭāka nirainiraiyāka maṭittaṭukkappaṭṭu ovvor aṟuvaik kaṭaiyilum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāka viṟpaṉaikkuk kāttuk kiṭantaṉa.”
“miḷakuc carakkukaḷ, nel, cāmai, koḷḷu, payaṟu, eḷḷu mutaliya patiṉāṟuvakaik kūlaṅkaḷiṉ kuviyalkaḷ kaṭaikaḷilum vītikaḷilum nirampikkiṭantaṉa.”
“kaiyil eḵku muḻakkōlum paṭiyaḷavaikaḷum ēntiyavāṟu tarakarkaḷ eṅkum cuṟṟittirikiṉṟaṉar. makkaḷ vāṅkum miḷakaiyum, kūlavakaikaḷaiyum, vaṇikar aḷantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar.”
“aṟaṅkūṟavaiyaṅkaḷil kalainiṟai naṭuvar talaimai yiṭattil vīṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kaṭuñciṉamō mikuti kaṇṇōṭṭamō iṉṟi, irutiṟaccāṉṟukaḷaiyum oppa eṭai pōṭṭup pārttu, accamō calukaiyō iṉṟic caṭṭamuṟai kaḷai āynturaikkiṉṟaṉar.”*
“amaiccaravaiyil kāviti eṉṉum uyar paṭṭam peṟṟa pukaḻuṭaiya maṉṉar, matiyamaiccarkaḷ vīṟṟirukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ tam kūriya aṟivuttiṭpattāl maṉṉaṉukkum avaṉ āḷum makkaḷukkam nērakkūṭum tīmaikaḷai muṉṉaṟintu taṭuttum, naṉmaikaḷai aṟintu perukkiyum ceyalāṟṟu kiṉṟaṉar.”
matilcūḻnta oru pērakal vaḷaivukkuḷ pāṇṭiyaṉ araṇ maṉai tikaḻkiṉṟatu96. ataṉ vāyil mika uyaramāka iruppataṉāl, yāṉaikaḷ tammītuḷḷa koṭikaḷait tāḻttāmalē uḷḷē nuḻaiya muṭikiṟatu.
oru kaviñar vāyilaip pāṭiyuḷḷār.
“vāyilmītuḷḷa kōpuram oru malai pōṉṟatu. ataṉ aṭiyiluḷḷa vāyil malai kuṭaintu tiṟantu uruvākkiya turappuvaḻi pōṉṟirukkiṟatu.”
“vāyil katavukaḷ maṭakkuk katavukaḷāy, irumpu viciṟi iṇaikkappaṭṭu, tiṇṇiya kaṉatta irumpukkampikaḷum tāḻkaḷumiṭṭu valivūṭṭappaṭṭavaiyāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ivaiyāvum ceṅkurutivaṇṇattil tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. vāyil tūṇkaḷil kāval teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.”
“uḷmuṟṟa veḷiyil maṇal parappappaṭṭuḷḷatu. am maṇalil nīṇṭa vālmayiruṭaiya kavarimāṉum, kuṟuṅkālkaḷai yuṭaiya aṉṉaṅkaḷum viḷaiyāṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṟṟattiṉ maṟṟorupuṟattil maṉṉaṉ kutirai ilāyaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil maṉṉaṉ ciṟappurimaikkuriya uyariya tōṟṟamuṭaiya pōrkkutiraikaḷ enta nēramum āyattamāka niṟkiṉṟaṉa.”
“araciyārukkum araciyāriṉ āyattārukkum taṉippaṭṭa vīṟumikka māṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ ellaiyil maṉṉaṉ tavira maṟṟa enta āṭavarum nuḻaital kūṭātu. avaṟṟil vāṉavilliṉ ēḻu niṟaṅkaḷum koṇṭa koṭikaḷ āṭukiṉṟaṉa. cuvarkaḷ merukiṭṭa pittaḷai niṟamākat niṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iṭaiyiṭaiyē veḷḷipōla miṉṉum tiṟattuṭaṉ avai nīṟiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cuvariṉmītu tīṭṭappaṭṭa aḻakiya pūṅkoṭi ōviyaṅkaḷum, tūṇkaḷiṉ nīla niṟamum cērntu mika aḻakiya ōviyakkāṭciyāy amaikiṉṟaṉa.”
“kolumaṇṭapattil pāṇṭiyaṉ aḻakuṭaṉ vīṟṟiruk kiṟāṉ. avaṉ iṭuppil muṟukalākat tēyttumaṭiyiṭap paṭṭa orē oru āṭai aṇintirukkiṟāṉ.97 ataṉ mītu maṇipatitta kaccai iṟukak kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.”
“muḻaṅkaikkumēl, mūṭāta mēṟkaimītu, aḻakiya vēlaip pāṭamainta poṉṉālāṉa kaiyaṇikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.”
“avaṉ akaṉṟa tōḷkaḷ iṉiya maṇamuṭaiya cāntaṇiyap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ataṉmītu pū mālaiyuṭaṉ oḷimikka muttumālai oṉṟum puraḷkiṉṟatu.”
“aracaṉaic cūḻntu pala araṇkaḷaik kaippaṟṟiyum, pala yāṉakaiḷaip pōril vīḻttiyum pukaḻpeṟṟu, pala kalaṅkaḷil naintu muṟivuṟṟa porumaiyuṭaiya kavacamaṇintavāṟu avaṉ nampikkaikkuriya uramikka vīrarum paṭaittalaivarkaḷum niṟkiṉṟaṉar.”
“kūttarum, pāṇarum, yāḻppāṇarum, maṉṉaṉ muṉṉilai yil taṅkaḷ tiṟaṅkaḷkāṭṭit tērum yāṉaikaḷum paricilkaḷākap peṟukiṉṟaṉar.”
“kuḷirnta mālai nēraṅkaḷil, perumakkaḷ kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭa aḻakiya tērkaḷil ēṟic celkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ kālāḷvīraṉ avarkaḷuṭaṉ tēriṉ arukilēyē ōṭivarukiṟāṉ98. avarkaḷ civanta āṭai aṇikiṉṟaṉar. poṉṉāl iḻaikkap paṭṭa uṟaikaḷil, vilāppakkamāka avarkaḷ vāḷkaḷ toṅkukiṉṟaṉa.”
“peruṅkuṭip peṇṭir kālil cilampum kaiyil poṉ vaḷaikaḷum aṇintu māḷikaiyiṉ māṭaṅkaḷil vantu niṉṟu ulavukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ mēṉimītuḷḷa naṟumaṇam terukkaḷil paravukiṉṟatu.”
“potu vītikaḷil caṅkaṟuppōr, maṇikōppavarkaḷ, poṉ viṉaiñar, kaṉṉār, taiyal vēlaikkārarkaḷ, necavu vēlaiyāṭkaḷ, ōviyar, pūvāṇikarkaḷ, maṇapporuḷ vāṇikar mutaliya palvēṟu vakaippaṭṭa makkaḷ miṭaintu neruṅkit tattam poruḷkaḷai viṟṟavaṇṇam celkiṉṟaṉar99.”
“uṇavakaṅkaḷum, aruntakaṅkaḷum, ippōtu varuvōr pōvōr tiraḷkaḷāl nirampiyuḷḷaṉa. avarkaḷ māṅkaṉiyum palākkaṉiyum, kaṟkaṇṭum, pacuṅkāykaḷum, kāyccaṟkiḻaṅkum carkkaraip poṅkalum iṉiyavaimalinta kaṟiyuṇavukaḷum uṇṇukiṉṟaṉar.”
“palvakai aravaṅkaḷai aṭakki mēleḻuntu muracukaḷum icaikkaruvikaḷum tiṭumeṉa muḻaṅki, makkaḷai mālai vaḻipāṭṭu kkuk kūviyaḻaikkiṉṟaṉa. miṉṉum aṇimaṇiyāṭai puṉainta peṇṭir tattam kaṇavaruṭaṉum makkaḷuṭaṉum naṟumpukaiyum malarum tāṅkip putta maṭaṅkaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar.”
“kuṭainta pāṟaikaḷiluḷḷa kukaikaḷ pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum otukkiṭaṅkaḷil pārppaṉat tuṟavikaḷ tattam camayakkiḷai kaḷukkuriya mantiraṅkaḷai muṇumuṇukkiṉṟaṉar. ammaṭaṅkaḷiṉ matilkaḷ celvaṇṇam pūcappaṭṭu vāṉaḷāviyuyarntuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟai yaṭuttu aḻakiya pūntōṭṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.”
“vēṟu cilar pārppaṉac camayak kōyilkaḷukkac ceṉṟu teyvaṅkaḷukku mālai vēḷviyāṟṟukiṉṟaṉar.”
“at teyvaṅkaḷil mutaṉmaiyāṉavar aimpūtaṅkaḷaiyum paṭaittu āṟṟal cāṉṟa maḻuppaṭai ēntiya teyvam (civaperumāṉ) āvar.”100
“mālai niḻaṟpaṭalaṅkaḷ neruṅkuntōṟum, ovvoru vīṭṭilum viḷakkukaḷ ēṟṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṉṉiṟa uṭaiyaṇinta iḷaiñarkaḷ orupuṟam, malarkaḷum aṇimaṇiyiḻaikaḷum pūṇṭa potumakaḷir; maṟṟorupuṟam vītikaḷil ulavat toṭaṅku kiṉṟaṉar. teruvil yāṉaikaḷ cellāmal taṭuppataṟkākak kavaikkaṟkaḷ pōṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟaic caṭṭai paṇṇāmal taḷḷāṭiyavaṇṇam avaṟṟiṉmītē kuṭiveṟiyar celkiṉṟaṉar.”
“iḷantāyarāṉa naṅkaiyarkaḷ viḷakkukaḷuṭaṉum uṟaviṉarkāvaluṭaṉum yāḻum muḻavum uṭaṉiṇaiya iṉiya paṇ pāṭikkoṇṭu, tam kuḻantaikaḷukkut tīṅkaṟṟa nalvāḻvu aruḷumpaṭi tēviyai vaṇaṅkuvataṟkāka aviyuṇavum kāṇik kaikaḷum koṇṭu celkiṉṟaṉar. pūcainaṅkaiyuṭaṉ avarkaḷ 
avvaruḷuṇavaip pakirntuṇkiṉṟaṉar.
“puṟañcērikaḷil ēḻai vakuppiṉar kaṇiyariṉ āṇaippaṭi murukaṉukku viḻāveṭukkiṉṟaṉar. kallā muraṭṭup paṇkaḷuṭaṉum āravārattuṭaṉum pāṭṭu muḻakkattuṭaṉum avarkaḷ kaikōttu āṭukiṉṟaṉar. cilliṭṭu ārpparikkum tāḷaṅkaḷum muracaṅkaḷum cērntu muraṇicai muḻakkukiṉṟaṉa.”
“paṭippaṭiyāka āṭalpāṭaṟ kuḻukkaḷ kalaikiṉṟaṉa. ciṟu kaṭaikkārarkaḷ tam kaṭaikaḷai aṭaikkiṉṟaṉar. iṉṉuṇā vāṇikar tattam kaṭai muṉṟililēyē tuyir koḷkiṉṟaṉar. nakaril aravam muṟṟilum ōykiṟatu.”
“iravuk kāvalar ippōtu terukkaḷil kāval varukiṉṟaṉar. piḻaiyā ilakkuṭaiya villampēntiyavarkaḷāy, añcāta neñcuṭaṉum, tuyilāta kaṇkaḷuṭaṉum, avarkaḷ nakar cuṟṟukiṉṟaṉar. maḻaipoḻiyum iruṇṭa iravukaḷil teruvellām veḷḷakkāṭāka ōṭumpōtum avarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kaṭamaikaḷil ciṟitum tavaṟuvatillai.”101

30.	maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ tolkāppiyar āriyar eṉṟa karuttai maṟuttār. nūlāciriyaraippōla avar āriyar eṉṟu karutupavar iṉṉum cilar uṇṭu. āyiṉum ikkaruttu iṉṟu valuviḻantē varukiṟatu. akattiyar tolkāppiyar kataiyaiyiṉṟi itaṟku ātāram illai. akkataikkō tolkāppiyattil oru ciṟitukūṭa ātāram kiṭaiyātu. āyiṉum tolkāppiyattai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu āciriyar kāṭṭum āriya - tamiḻiṉap paṇpu vēṟupāṭukaḷ ivvaṭippaṭait tavaṟutalāl valiviḻakkavillai. tolkāppiyar kūṟum antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar piriviṉai cāti vēṟupāṭu aṉṟu, toḻil vēṟupāṭē, atuvum nāṭṭiṉ naṭuppakutiyākiya marutattiṇaiyai maṭṭumē atu kuṟittatu. nālu pirivukaḷum vēḷāḷar vakuppiṉ pirivukaḷēyākum. ēṉeṉil vēḷāḷarē marutanilakkuṭikaḷ.
31.	tolkāppiyar oru āriyap pārppaṉar eṉṟa karuttait toṭarntu eḻuntatē intak karuttum. tirunūl aṇital mutalāṉa kuṟiyīṭukaḷ āriyap pārppaṉarukkē uriyaṉa eṉṟa karuttu itaṉuṭaṉ iṇaikiṟatu. āṉāl tirunūl aṇital tamiḻp pārppaṉar allatu kurumāriṭamiruntu kurumār toḻilai mēṟkoṇṭa āriyar kaikkoṇṭa vaḻakkam eṉpatil aiyamillai. ēṉeṉil āriyar intiyā varumuṉ nūlum nūṟṟalum uṭaiyavarallar. cilar āriyar varumpōtu atait tōlāl aṇintiruntaṉar eṉṟum, atu avarkaḷ vēṭṭuva nilaik kālattil ampuk kūṭukaḷ toṅkaviṭuvataṟkāka iṭṭa vārē eṉṟum, iṉṟum pūṇūlil māṉtōl tuṇṭu iṇaippatu ammarapiṉ ciṉṉamē eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.
	iṉṟum pārppaṉar maṭṭumiṉṟi, kammiyar mutaliya kalait teḻilāḷarum tirunūlaṇikiṉṟaṉar. cilar karutuvatupōla itu putu vaḻakkeṉṟu tōṉṟavillai. nūlaṇital nūlaṟiviṉ kuṟiyātaliṉ, taṉittuṟai nūlaṟivu iṉṟiyamaiyātu vēṇṭappaṭṭa toḻil tuṟaiyiṉar yāvarum toḻiṟ kuḻukkaḷ amainta kālaiyil atai mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. tamiḻp pārppaṉar ākama nūlaṟivukāṭṭa aṇinta ataṉaiyē tam vēta aṟivukāṭṭa āriyap pārppaṉar mēṟkoṇṭaṉarātal vēṇṭum eṉṉalām.

90.	māṅkuṭi marutaṉāriṉ maturaik kāñci, nakkīrariṉ neṭunalvāṭai iḷaṅkō aṭikaḷ cilappatikāram
91.	maturaikkāñci, aṭikaḷ 654 toṭarcci. kiṟittuvak kallūri māta veḷiyīṭu (ūhcaḷaivayain ūṭiṭaṭanapana āyapayaṇainē) pattuppāṭṭu eṉṟa talaippil tantuḷḷa iraṇṭu cuvaikaramāṉa kaṭṭuraikaḷ maturaikkāñci. neṭunalvāṭai ākiyavaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷai moḻi peyarttut tarukiṉṟaṉa.

10. tiruvaḷḷuvar kuṟaḷ
akkālac ceyyuḷ nūlkaḷuḷ pala muḻu vaṭivilē iṉṟu namakku vantu cērntuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṭaiyē talaiciṟanta makkaṭ pukaḻ uṭaiyatu vaḷḷuvarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa muppāl allatu kuṟaḷ ākum. piṉvanta talaimuṟaikaḷaṉaittilumē ataṉ celvākku mikutiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
tiruvaḷḷuvar vāḻkkaiyaippaṟṟi namakku ārāycci muṟaikku otta cāṉṟukaḷ etuvum kiṭṭavillai. āṉāl avar taṟpōtu ceṉṉaimānakariṉ pēṭṭaikaḷuḷ oṉṟāyirukkum paḻamaivāynta mayilāppūril piṟantavar eṉṟu potuvāka nampappaṭukiṟatu. tam peru nūlukkup pāṇṭiyaṉ ātaravaiyum pāṇṭiyaṉuṭaiya caṅkappulavar opputalaiyum peṟum nōkkattuṭaṉ avar maturaikkuc ceṉṟār.
ip pulavarkaḷil cilar maturaiyiṟ piṟantavarkaḷ. maṟṟum cilar cōṇāṭṭiluḷḷa uṟaiyūriliruntum, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉat tiliruntum vantavarkaḷ. vēṟu cilar koṅku nāṭṭiluḷḷa cellūriliruntum, toṇṭaināṭṭiluḷḷa vēṅkaṭattiliruntum, tamiḻakattiṉ ēṉaiya pakutikaḷiliruntum vantavarkaḷ.
‘iṉiya nāṟpatu’ eṉṟa ciṟiya nūliṉ āciriyar pūtaṉcēntaṉār; aṟaneṟi vācakaṅkaḷaṭaṅkiya tirikaṭukattiṉ āciriyar nallātaṉar; kalittokai tokutta nallantuvaṉar; iṉpattuṟai atāvatu akapporuḷ ilakkaṇam tanta iṟaiyaṉār; kuṟiñcippāṭṭu, iṉṉā nāṟpatu ākiya ciṟumaṇic celvaṅkaḷai aḷitta kapilar; ālaṅkāṉappōr veṟṟikaṇṭa pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ; veṟṟivākaiyai maturaik kāñciyāl viṟal paṭap pāṭiya māṅkuṭi marutaṉār; neṭunalvāṭai, tirumuru kāṟṟuppaṭai mutaliya kaviṉuṟu ciṟu kāviyaṅkaḷiyaṟṟiya pulamaicāl nakkīrar; cuvaimikka putta peruṅkāviyamākiya maṇimēkalaiyai uruvākkittanta puttap perum pulavar cīttalaic cāttaṉār ākiyōr appulavar peruṅkuḻuvil iruntaṉar.
ivarkaḷallāmal, maruttuvam, kaṇitam, ilakkiyam ākiyavaṟṟil taṉicciṟappaṭainta perumpērāciriyarkaḷum iṭam peṟṟiruntaṉar. āṉāl ivarkaḷukkuriya taṉic ciṟappu nūlkaḷ nammaivan teṭṭavillai.
annāḷaiya talaiciṟanta kaviñar, pulavar ākiyavar                            kaḷiṭaiyē, tamiḻakattiṉ pulavarkaḷai ātarikkum puravalarkaḷil talaiciṟantavaṉāṉa pāṇṭiyaṉ ukkirapperuvaḻutiyiṉ oppu talaiyum etirnōkki niṉṟirukkavēṇṭum.
